`LICENSES/README.md`:

```md
# License scope

Unless a file carries a different license notice, the original source code and
documentation in this repository are licensed under the Apache License, Version 2.0.
See the root `LICENSE` file for the complete terms.

The following Linux eBPF programs retain their GPL license and are covered by
`GPL-2.0-only.txt` in this directory:

- `bpf/tcp.c`
- `bpf/frida.c`
- `bpf/fridastream.c`

Their generated BPF object files retain the same license, including when embedded
in a Go executable. The root Apache license does not relicense those objects.

Third-party components retain their respective licenses. PacketProxy, Go modules,
Frida, and the Java bridge supplied by frida-tools are not relicensed by this
repository. Preserve their applicable notices when distributing those components.

Desktop distribution ZIPs include `LICENSES/third-party-go/`. The build reads the
actual dependency versions and checksums from both shipped Go binaries, copies
upstream license/notice files from checksum-verified modules, and includes the Go
toolchain license. Its `manifest.json` records the modules, binaries, and files.
Packaging fails if a module's license is missing or the binaries use a different
Go toolchain than the packaging environment. Rebuild both binaries together.

References:

- https://www.kernel.org/doc/html/latest/bpf/bpf_licensing.html
- https://www.apache.org/licenses/LICENSE-2.0

```

`README.md`:

```md
# eBPF Analyzer

An eBPF-powered [PacketProxy](https://github.com/DeNA/PacketProxy) plugin for network analysis, with process attribution and native/Java stack inspection via Frida.

> **Experimental — early development.** This project is actively being developed and is not yet a stable release. Features, UI, and output formats may change without backward compatibility. Testing has focused on a rooted Android x86_64 emulator (API 34); other environments are not yet fully validated. Captured events and stacks may be incomplete. Intended for investigation and testing in controlled environments.

Monitor Android TCP connections, packet copies, and syscall events in a separate **eBPF Analyzer** tab. **No proxy listener, device proxy settings.** Original traffic continues normally; TLS payloads remain encrypted.

![Android requests traced to Java methods and matching I/O events in PacketProxy](docs/demo.gif)

*Trace Android requests to Java call sites and inspect the matching send/receive events.*

## Requirements

- PacketProxy on Windows/Linux x86_64 or macOS Intel/Apple Silicon.
- A rooted **Android x86_64** device with the required BPF/kernel support. Tested on Android Emulator API 34.
- Android Platform Tools (`adb`) available on PATH.

## Build with GitHub Actions

1. Open [Actions > Build](https://github.com/DoranekoSystems/ebpf-analyzer/actions/workflows/build.yml).
2. Choose **Run workflow**, or select a successful run triggered by a push or pull request.
3. Download the artifact for your desktop:

   | Desktop | Artifact |
   | --- | --- |
   | Windows x86_64 | `ebpf-analyzer-windows-amd64` |
   | Linux x86_64 | `ebpf-analyzer-linux-amd64` |
   | macOS Intel | `ebpf-analyzer-darwin-amd64` |
   | macOS Apple Silicon | `ebpf-analyzer-darwin-arm64` |

4. Extract the artifact and its enclosed distribution ZIP.

Each package contains the plugin JAR, desktop monitor, one Android x86_64 executable, and Frida scripts. The [workflow](.github/workflows/build.yml) builds these components and runs Go and UI tests. PacketProxy is installed separately.

## Install and run

### 1. Install the plugin

Copy `plugin/CommunicationOrigins.jar` to your home directory's `.packetproxy/extensions/` folder, then restart PacketProxy:

- Windows: `%USERPROFILE%\.packetproxy\extensions\`
- Linux/macOS: `~/.packetproxy/extensions/`

### 2. Install the Android executable

Run these commands from the extracted package directory. Replace `emulator-5554` with your device serial from `adb devices`.

```sh
adb -s emulator-5554 push android-x86_64/ebpf-analyzer /data/local/tmp/ebpf-analyzer
adb -s emulator-5554 shell su 0 chmod 755 /data/local/tmp/ebpf-analyzer
```

### 3. Start monitoring

Windows PowerShell:

```powershell
./monitor.exe -serial emulator-5554 -output captures/monitor.json
```

Linux/macOS:

```sh
chmod +x monitor
./monitor -serial emulator-5554 -output captures/monitor.json
```

The host monitor starts the Android collector automatically. Stop monitoring with **Ctrl+C**.

### 4. Open the analyzer

In PacketProxy, open **Extensions > eBPF Analyzer**, click **Open Monitor File...** at the **bottom right**, and select `captures/monitor.json` created by the monitor in step 3. The selected file path appears above the status bar. Enable the extension under Options if needed.

The Android collector refreshes its UID-to-package mapping when the installed package list changes. Apps installed during capture are resolved without restarting the monitor; temporary read failures keep the last good mapping and retry after five seconds. Shared or unmapped UIDs can still lack a unique package name.

Generate a **new TCP connection** in the target app, then select it to inspect **Packets**, **Connect**, and **I/O Events**. UDP/QUIC is outside TCP ownership tracking. Right-click a table and choose **Clear** to clear its displayed records while collection continues.

For a device other than `emulator-5554`, also set PacketProxy's Java property `-Dpacketproxy.adb.serial=YOUR_SERIAL`.

## Optional: native and Java stacks with Frida

1. Run a matching Android x86_64 `frida-server` as root, and install Python `frida` / `frida-tools` on the desktop.

   ```sh
   python -m pip install frida frida-tools
   python -c "import frida; print(frida.__version__)"
   ```

   Use the same version of `frida-server` as the Python `frida` version printed above. Run these commands with the Python interpreter used by PacketProxy (default: `python`; override with `-Dpacketproxy.frida.python=/path/to/python`). Installing the Python packages does not install or start `frida-server` on Android.

2. Copy `frida/agent.js`, `frida/bridge.py`, and `frida/stream.py` into `~/.packetproxy/extensions/ebpf-frida/` under your home directory.
3. Select a connection and choose **Settings > I/O analysis: Frida**.
4. Wait for **Collecting PID ...**, generate traffic, and select an event to inspect its saved stacks.

Collection covers the selected PID's supported TCP I/O across all threads and connections. Java frames depend on the executing thread; past Java stacks cannot be recovered. Stop with **Stop**, **None**, or **Manual**.

## License

User-space code and documentation: [Apache-2.0](LICENSE). eBPF programs: [GPL-2.0-only](LICENSES/GPL-2.0-only.txt). See [license scope](LICENSES/README.md).

```

`bpf/frida.c`:

```c
// SPDX-License-Identifier: GPL-2.0-only
// Explicit one-shot debug capture; never loaded by the passive collector.
typedef unsigned long long u64;
typedef unsigned int u32;
#define SEC(n) __attribute__((section(n), used))
#define UINT(n, v) int(*n)[v]
#define TYPE(n, v) v *n
static void *(*lookup)(void *, const void *) = (void *)1;
static u64 (*pid_tgid)(void) = (void *)14;
static u64 (*now)(void) = (void *)125;
static void *(*task)(void) = (void *)158;
static void *(*regs)(void *) = (void *)175;
static long (*read_kernel)(void *, u32, const void *) = (void *)113;
static long (*send_thread)(u32) = (void *)117;
struct state {
  u32 pid, tid, operation, armed;
  u32 pending, done;
  int signal_result;
  u32 skipped;
  u64 deadline, entered, exited;
  long long result;
  u64 saved[21];
};
struct {
  UINT(type, 2);
  UINT(max_entries, 1);
  TYPE(key, u32);
  TYPE(value, struct state);
} control SEC(".maps");
static int allowed(u32 op, u64 nr) {
  if (op == 1) return nr == 1 || nr == 20 || nr == 44 || nr == 46 || nr == 307;
  return nr == 0 || nr == 19 || nr == 45 || nr == 47 || nr == 299;
}
static int enter(u32 operation) {
  u32 zero = 0;
  struct state *s = lookup(&control, &zero);
  u64 id = pid_tgid();
  if (!s || !s->armed || s->done || s->pid != (id >> 32) || s->tid != (u32)id ||
      s->operation != operation || now() > s->deadline)
    return 0;
  u64 saved[21];
  if (read_kernel(saved, sizeof(saved), regs(task()))) return 0;
  // Async kernel workers and io_uring completion are not syscall callers.
  if (saved[17] != 0x33 || !allowed(operation, saved[15])) {
    s->skipped++;
    return 0;
  }
  s->pending = 1;
  s->entered = now();
  return 0;
}
SEC("kprobe/tcp_sendmsg") int send_entry(void *ctx) { return enter(1); }
SEC("kprobe/tcp_recvmsg") int receive_entry(void *ctx) { return enter(2); }
struct raw_args {
  u64 args[2];
};
SEC("raw_tracepoint/sys_exit") int io_exit(struct raw_args *ctx) {
  u32 zero = 0;
  struct state *s = lookup(&control, &zero);
  u64 id = pid_tgid();
  if (!s || !s->armed || s->done || !s->pending || s->pid != (id >> 32) || s->tid != (u32)id)
    return 0;
  s->pending = 0;
  if (now() > s->deadline) return 0;
  long long result = ctx->args[1];
  // EAGAIN is readiness, not completed input. Internal restart returns are not final.
  if (result == -11 || result == -512 || result == -513 || result == -514 || result == -516) {
    s->skipped++;
    return 0;
  }
  u64 saved[21];
  if (read_kernel(saved, sizeof(saved), (void *)ctx->args[0])) return 0;
  if (saved[17] != 0x33 || !allowed(s->operation, saved[15])) {
    s->skipped++;
    return 0;
  }
  __builtin_memcpy(s->saved, saved, sizeof(saved));
  s->result = result;
  s->exited = now();
  s->armed = 0;
  s->done = 1;
  s->signal_result = send_thread(5);
  return 0;
}
char frida_license[] SEC("license") = "GPL";

```

`bpf/fridastream.c`:

```c
// SPDX-License-Identifier: GPL-2.0-only
// PID-wide opt-in syscall-exit stream, separate from passive monitoring.
typedef unsigned long long u64;
typedef unsigned int u32;
#define SEC(n) __attribute__((section(n), used))
#define UINT(n, v) int(*n)[v]
#define TYPE(n, v) v *n
static void *(*lookup)(void *, const void *) = (void *)1;
static long (*update)(void *, const void *, const void *, u64) = (void *)2;
static long (*delete)(void *, const void *) = (void *)3;
static u64 (*pid_tgid)(void) = (void *)14;
static u64 (*now)(void) = (void *)125;
static void *(*task)(void) = (void *)158;
static void *(*regs)(void *) = (void *)175;
static long (*read_kernel)(void *, u32, const void *) = (void *)113;
static long (*signal_thread)(u32) = (void *)117;
static void *(*reserve)(void *, u64, u64) = (void *)131;
static void (*submit)(void *, u64) = (void *)132;
struct config {
  u32 pid, blocked_offset;
  u64 deadline;
};
struct pending {
  u64 entered, requested;
  u32 operation, pad;
};
struct event {
  u32 pid, tid, operation, flags;
  int signal_result;
  u32 pad;
  u64 entered, exited, requested;
  long long result;
  u64 saved[21];
};
struct {
  UINT(type, 2);
  UINT(max_entries, 1);
  TYPE(key, u32);
  TYPE(value, struct config);
} control SEC(".maps");
struct {
  UINT(type, 1);
  UINT(max_entries, 4096);
  TYPE(key, u32);
  TYPE(value, struct pending);
} pending SEC(".maps");
struct {
  UINT(type, 1);
  UINT(max_entries, 4096);
  TYPE(key, u32);
  TYPE(value, u64);
} inflight SEC(".maps");
struct {
  UINT(type, 27);
  UINT(max_entries, 1048576);
} events SEC(".maps");
struct {
  UINT(type, 2);
  UINT(max_entries, 1);
  TYPE(key, u32);
  TYPE(value, u64);
} dropped SEC(".maps");
static void drop(void) {
  u32 k = 0;
  u64 *n = lookup(&dropped, &k);
  if (n) __sync_fetch_and_add(n, 1);
}
static int allowed(u32 op, u64 nr) {
  return op == 1 ? (nr == 1 || nr == 20 || nr == 44 || nr == 46 || nr == 307)
                 : (nr == 0 || nr == 19 || nr == 45 || nr == 47 || nr == 299);
}
static int enter(void *ctx, u32 op) {
  u32 k = 0;
  struct config *c = lookup(&control, &k);
  u64 id = pid_tgid();
  u32 tid = id;
  if (!c || !c->pid || c->pid != (id >> 32) || now() > c->deadline) return 0;
  u64 saved[21];
  if (read_kernel(saved, sizeof(saved), regs(task()))) return 0;
  if (saved[17] != 0x33 || !allowed(op, saved[15])) return 0;
  struct pending p = {.entered = now(), .operation = op};
  read_kernel(&p.requested, 8, (char *)ctx + 12 * 8);  // x86_64 pt_regs.dx: TCP length
  if (update(&pending, &tid, &p, 0)) drop();
  return 0;
}
SEC("kprobe/tcp_sendmsg") int send_entry(void *ctx) { return enter(ctx, 1); }
SEC("kprobe/tcp_recvmsg") int receive_entry(void *ctx) { return enter(ctx, 2); }
struct raw_args {
  u64 args[2];
};
SEC("raw_tracepoint/sys_exit") int io_exit(struct raw_args *ctx) {
  u32 k = 0;
  struct config *c = lookup(&control, &k);
  u64 id = pid_tgid();
  u32 tid = id;
  if (!c || !c->pid || c->pid != (id >> 32)) return 0;
  struct pending *p = lookup(&pending, &tid);
  if (!p) return 0;
  struct pending original = *p;
  delete (&pending, &tid);
  if (now() > c->deadline) return 0;
  struct event e = {.pid = id >> 32,
                    .tid = tid,
                    .operation = original.operation,
                    .entered = original.entered,
                    .requested = original.requested,
                    .exited = now(),
                    .result = ctx->args[1]};
  if (read_kernel(e.saved, sizeof(e.saved), (void *)ctx->args[0])) return 0;
  if (e.saved[17] != 0x33 || !allowed(e.operation, e.saved[15])) return 0;
  // Include readiness/error metadata; do not trigger Java/native unwinding on retry.
  if (e.result == -11 || e.result == -512 || e.result == -513 || e.result == -514 ||
      e.result == -516)
    e.flags = 4;
  else if (lookup(&inflight, &tid))
    e.flags = 2;
  else {
    u64 mask = 0;
    if (read_kernel(&mask, 8, (char *)task() + c->blocked_offset) || mask & (1ULL << 4))
      e.flags = 3;
    else
      e.flags = 1;
  }
  void *slot = reserve(&events, sizeof(e), 0);
  if (!slot) {
    drop();
    return 0;
  }
  if (e.flags == 1) {
    if (update(&inflight, &tid, &e.exited, 1))
      e.flags = 2;
    else {
      e.signal_result = signal_thread(5);
      if (e.signal_result) {
        e.flags = 5;
        delete (&inflight, &tid);
      }
    }
  }
  __builtin_memcpy(slot, &e, sizeof(e));
  submit(slot, 0);
  return 0;
}
char stream_license[] SEC("license") = "GPL";

```

`bpf/tcp.c`:

```c
// SPDX-License-Identifier: GPL-2.0-only
// The loader verifies this tracepoint ABI before attaching. No kernel headers needed.
typedef unsigned long long u64;
typedef unsigned int u32;
typedef unsigned short u16;
typedef unsigned char u8;
typedef int s32;
#define SEC(name) __attribute__((section(name), used))
#define UINT(name, value) int(*name)[value]
#define TYPE(name, value) value *name
static void *(*lookup)(void *, const void *) = (void *)1;
static long (*update)(void *, const void *, const void *, u64) = (void *)2;
static long (*delete)(void *, const void *) = (void *)3;
static u64 (*now_ns)(void) = (void *)125;  // bpf_ktime_get_boot_ns, includes suspend
static u64 (*pid_tgid)(void) = (void *)14;
static u64 (*uid_gid)(void) = (void *)15;
static long (*comm)(void *, u32) = (void *)16;

#ifdef STACK_X86_64
static void *(*current_task_btf)(void) = (void *)158;
static void *(*task_pt_regs)(void *) = (void *)175;
static long (*read_kernel)(void *, u32, const void *) = (void *)113;
static long (*read_user)(void *, u32, const void *) = (void *)112;
#define STACK_BYTES 4096
// Linux x86_64 pt_regs ABI. Do not use this layout for arm64 or compat tasks.
struct user_regs {
  u64 r15, r14, r13, r12, bp, bx, r11, r10, r9, r8;
  u64 ax, cx, dx, si, di, orig_ax, ip, cs, flags, sp, ss;
};
struct origin {
  u64 start_ns;
  u32 pid, tid;
  s32 regs_error, stack_error, instruction_error;
  u32 status, captured, kind;  // 0 connect, 1 TCP send entry, 2 TCP receive entry
  struct user_regs regs;
  u8 instruction[2], padding[6];
  u64 connection_ns, io_requested;
  u8 stack[STACK_BYTES];
};
// Bounded storage: 128 pending snapshots, plus one scratch value per CPU.
// Only stack[0:captured] is valid; scratch tail is never exported by the collector.
struct {
  UINT(type, 1);
  UINT(max_entries, 128);
  TYPE(key, u64);
  TYPE(value, struct origin);
} origins SEC(".maps");
struct {
  UINT(type, 6);
  UINT(max_entries, 1);
  TYPE(key, u32);
  TYPE(value, struct origin);
} origin_scratch SEC(".maps");
struct {
  UINT(type, 2);
  UINT(max_entries, 1);
  TYPE(key, u32);
  TYPE(value, u32);
} origin_enabled SEC(".maps");
struct {
  UINT(type, 2);
  UINT(max_entries, 1);
  TYPE(key, u32);
  TYPE(value, u64);
} origin_drops SEC(".maps");

static __attribute__((noinline)) void capture_origin(u64 id, u64 task, u64 connection, u64 kind,
                                                     u64 requested) {
  u32 zero = 0;
  u32 *enabled = lookup(&origin_enabled, &zero);
  if (!enabled || !(*enabled & (kind ? 2 : 1))) return;
  struct origin *o = lookup(&origin_scratch, &zero);
  if (!o) return;
  __builtin_memset(o, 0, __builtin_offsetof(struct origin, stack));
  o->start_ns = id;
  o->pid = task >> 32;
  o->tid = (u32)task;
  o->connection_ns = connection;
  o->kind = (u32)kind;
  o->io_requested = requested;
  void *regs = task_pt_regs(current_task_btf());
  o->regs_error = read_kernel(&o->regs, sizeof(o->regs), regs);
  if (o->regs_error) {
    o->status = 5;
    goto publish;
  }
  // Only a saved native 64-bit user frame and a matching synchronous syscall.
  // Other execution paths are explicitly unsupported, never attributed by guess.
  if (o->regs.cs != 0x33 || !o->regs.sp || !o->regs.ip) {
    o->status = 3;
    goto publish;
  }
  u64 nr = o->regs.orig_ax;
  if ((!kind && nr != 42) ||
      (kind == 1 && nr != 1 && nr != 20 && nr != 44 && nr != 46 && nr != 307) ||
      (kind == 2 && nr != 0 && nr != 19 && nr != 45 && nr != 47 && nr != 299)) {
    o->status = 4;
    goto publish;
  }
  o->instruction_error = read_user(o->instruction, 2, (void *)(o->regs.ip - 2));
  if (o->instruction_error || o->instruction[0] != 0x0f || o->instruction[1] != 0x05) {
    o->status = 6;
    goto publish;
  }
  o->status = 1;
#pragma clang loop unroll(disable)
  for (u32 i = 0; i < STACK_BYTES / 256; i++) {
    // No page faults are induced. Stop at the first unreadable 256-byte block.
    long err = read_user(o->stack + i * 256, 256, (void *)(o->regs.sp + i * 256));
    if (err) {
      o->stack_error = err;
      o->status = o->captured ? 2 : 7;
      break;
    }
    o->captured += 256;
  }
publish:
  if (update(&origins, &id, o, 1)) {
    u64 *n = lookup(&origin_drops, &zero);
    if (n) __sync_fetch_and_add(n, 1);
  }
}
#endif

struct state_event {
  u64 common;
  u64 socket;
  u32 oldstate, newstate;
  u16 sport, dport, family, protocol;
  u8 saddr[4], daddr[4], saddr6[16], daddr6[16];
};
struct flow {
  u64 start_ns, established_ns, end_ns;
  u32 pid, tid, uid, state;
  u16 sport, dport, family, pad;
  u8 src[16], dst[16], comm[16];
};
struct {
  UINT(type, 1);
  UINT(max_entries, 8192);
  TYPE(key, u64);
  TYPE(value, u64);
} sockets SEC(".maps");
// Keep I/O ownership until destruction: buffered reads can follow TIME_WAIT/CLOSE.
struct {
  UINT(type, 9);
  UINT(max_entries, 8192);
  TYPE(key, u64);
  TYPE(value, u64);
} io_sockets SEC(".maps");
struct {
  UINT(type, 1);
  UINT(max_entries, 8192);
  TYPE(key, u64);
  TYPE(value, struct flow);
} flows SEC(".maps");
struct {
  UINT(type, 2);
  UINT(max_entries, 1);
  TYPE(key, u32);
  TYPE(value, u64);
} drops SEC(".maps");

static void dropped(void) {
  u32 zero = 0;
  u64 *n = lookup(&drops, &zero);
  if (n) __sync_fetch_and_add(n, 1);
}

SEC("tracepoint/sock/inet_sock_set_state")
int trace_state(struct state_event *ctx) {
  if (ctx->protocol != 6 || (ctx->family != 2 && ctx->family != 10)) return 0;
  u64 socket = ctx->socket;
  if (ctx->newstate == 2) {  // TCP_SYN_SENT: synchronous active-open context, not ACK/softirq.
    u64 id = now_ns();
    u64 task = pid_tgid();
    struct flow f = {};
    f.start_ns = id;
    f.pid = task >> 32;
    f.tid = (u32)task;
    f.uid = (u32)uid_gid();
    f.state = 2;
    comm(f.comm, sizeof(f.comm));
    // Invalidate an old pointer association before publishing a new lifetime.
    delete (&sockets, &socket);
    delete (&io_sockets, &socket);
    if (update(&flows, &id, &f, 1)) {
      dropped();
      return 0;
    }
    if (update(&sockets, &socket, &id, 0)) {
      delete (&flows, &id);
      dropped();
      return 0;
    }
#ifdef STACK_X86_64
    if (update(&io_sockets, &socket, &id, 0)) dropped();
    capture_origin(id, task, id, 0, 0);
#endif
    return 0;
  }
  u64 *idp = lookup(&sockets, &socket);
  if (!idp) return 0;  // Ignore passive opens and connections predating attachment.
  u64 id = *idp;
  struct flow *original = lookup(&flows, &id);
  if (!original) return 0;
  struct flow f = *original;
  f.state = ctx->newstate;
  f.sport = ctx->sport;
  f.dport = ctx->dport;
  f.family = ctx->family;
  if (f.family == 2) {
    __builtin_memcpy(f.src, ctx->saddr, 4);
    __builtin_memcpy(f.dst, ctx->daddr, 4);
  } else {
    __builtin_memcpy(f.src, ctx->saddr6, 16);
    __builtin_memcpy(f.dst, ctx->daddr6, 16);
  }
  if (ctx->newstate == 1 && !f.established_ns) f.established_ns = now_ns();
  if (ctx->newstate == 7 || ctx->newstate == 6) {
    f.end_ns = now_ns();
    delete (&sockets, &socket);
  }
  if (update(&flows, &id, &f, 0)) dropped();
  return 0;
}
#ifdef STACK_X86_64
// These are application I/O entry events, NOT packet emission/arrival events.
// Correlate by the actual struct sock pointer and its tracked lifetime, never time proximity.
SEC("kprobe/tcp_sendmsg")
int trace_send(struct user_regs *ctx) {
  u64 socket = ctx->di;
  u64 *id = lookup(&io_sockets, &socket);
  if (id) capture_origin(now_ns(), pid_tgid(), *id, 1, ctx->dx);
  return 0;
}
SEC("kprobe/tcp_recvmsg")
int trace_receive(struct user_regs *ctx) {
  u64 socket = ctx->di;
  u64 *id = lookup(&io_sockets, &socket);
  if (id) capture_origin(now_ns(), pid_tgid(), *id, 2, ctx->dx);
  return 0;
}
SEC("kprobe/inet_sock_destruct")
int trace_destruct(struct user_regs *ctx) {
  u64 socket = ctx->di;
  delete (&io_sockets, &socket);
  return 0;
}
#endif
char LICENSE[] SEC("license") = "GPL";

```

`build.ps1`:

```ps1
# Local development helper. Release packages are built by GitHub Actions.
param([ValidateSet('amd64')][string]$Arch = 'amd64')
$ErrorActionPreference = 'Stop'
Push-Location $PSScriptRoot
$previousOS = $env:GOOS
$previousArch = $env:GOARCH
$previousCGO = $env:CGO_ENABLED
try {
    New-Item -ItemType Directory -Force bin | Out-Null
    $bpfDefines = @()
    if ($Arch -eq 'amd64') { $bpfDefines = @('-DSTACK_X86_64=1') }
    clang -O2 -g -target bpfel -Wall -Werror @bpfDefines -c bpf/tcp.c -o go/internal/device/collector/tcp_bpfel.o
    if ($LASTEXITCODE -ne 0) { throw 'BPF compilation failed' }
    $env:GOOS = 'linux'
    $env:GOARCH = $Arch
    $env:CGO_ENABLED = '0'
    clang -O2 -g -target bpfel -Wall -Werror -c bpf/frida.c -o go/internal/device/fridacapture/frida_bpfel.o
    if ($LASTEXITCODE -ne 0) { throw 'Frida BPF compilation failed' }
    clang -O2 -g -target bpfel -Wall -Werror -c bpf/fridastream.c -o go/internal/device/fridastream/fridastream_bpfel.o
    if ($LASTEXITCODE -ne 0) { throw 'Stream BPF compilation failed' }
    New-Item -ItemType Directory -Force bin/android-x86_64 | Out-Null
    go -C go build -trimpath -o ../bin/android-x86_64/ebpf-analyzer ./cmd/device
    if ($LASTEXITCODE -ne 0) { throw 'Device build failed' }
    $env:GOOS = $previousOS
    $env:GOARCH = $previousArch
    $hostOS = (go env GOHOSTOS).Trim()
    $hostArch = (go env GOHOSTARCH).Trim()
    $env:GOOS = $hostOS
    $env:GOARCH = $hostArch
    $monitorName = if ($hostOS -eq 'windows') { 'monitor.exe' } else { 'monitor' }
    go -C go build -trimpath -o "../bin/$monitorName" ./cmd/monitor
    if ($LASTEXITCODE -ne 0) { throw 'Monitor build failed' }
} finally {
    $env:GOOS = $previousOS
    $env:GOARCH = $previousArch
    $env:CGO_ENABLED = $previousCGO
    Pop-Location
}

```

`frida/agent.js`:

```js
// Java bridge is supplied by the installed frida-tools package, not vendored.
const Java = bridge;
let targetTid = 0,
  armed = false,
  continuous = false;
let javaStatus = 'Not initialized';
const functionStarts = new Map();

function functionOffset(address, symbol, module) {
  if (!module || !symbol.name) return '';
  try {
    const name = symbol.name.replace(/\s*\+0x[0-9a-f]+$/i, '');
    const key = module.path + ':' + module.base + ':' + name;
    if (!functionStarts.has(key)) {
      if (functionStarts.size >= 4096) functionStarts.clear();
      functionStarts.set(key, module.findSymbolByName(name));
    }
    const start = functionStarts.get(key);
    if (start === null || start.compare(module.base) < 0 || start.compare(address) > 0) return '';
    return address.sub(start).toString();
  } catch (_) { return ''; }
}

rpc.exports = {
  catalog() {
    const modules = Process.enumerateModules();
    let classes = [], javaError = '';
    if (Java.available) {
      try { Java.performNow(() => { classes = Array.from(new Set(Java.enumerateLoadedClassesSync())).filter(name => !name.startsWith('[')).sort(); }); }
      catch (e) { javaError = String(e); }
    } else javaError = 'No Java VM';
    return {
      modules: modules.slice(0, 2048).map(m => ({ name: m.name, path: m.path, base: m.base.toString(), size: m.size })),
      classes: classes.slice(0, 30000),
      truncated: modules.length > 2048 || classes.length > 30000,
      javaError,
    };
  },
  members(kind, name) {
    if (kind === 'module') {
      const module = Process.enumerateModules().find(m => m.path === name);
      if (!module) throw new Error('Module is no longer loaded');
      const functions = module.enumerateExports().filter(s => s.type === 'function');
      return { kind, name, members: functions.slice(0, 10000).map(s => s.name), truncated: functions.length > 10000 };
    }
    if (kind !== 'class' || !name || /[!*\/]/.test(name)) throw new Error('Invalid class query');
    if (!Java.available) throw new Error('No Java VM');
    let methods = [];
    Java.performNow(() => {
      for (const group of Java.enumerateMethods(name + '!*/s'))
        for (const klass of group.classes)
          if (klass.name === name) methods.push(...klass.methods);
    });
    methods = Array.from(new Set(methods)).sort();
    return { kind, name, members: methods.slice(0, 3000), truncated: methods.length > 3000 };
  },
  symbols(addresses) {
    return addresses.slice(0, 128).map((address) => {
      const p = ptr(address),
        module = Process.findModuleByAddress(p),
        s = DebugSymbol.fromAddress(p);
      return {
        address,
        module: module ? module.path : '',
        symbol: s.name || '',
        status: module ? 'resolved' : 'unmapped',
      };
    });
  },
  prepare(tid, stream) {
    continuous = !!stream;
    if (Process.arch !== 'x64') throw new Error('Active capture currently requires x86_64');
    targetTid = tid;
    javaStatus = Java.available ? 'Java available' : 'No Java VM';
    // Initialize the bridge before arming, without hooking libc I/O functions.
    if (Java.available)
      Java.performNow(() => {
        Java.backtrace({ limit: 1 });
      });
    Process.setExceptionHandler((d) => {
      if (
        !armed ||
        d.type !== 'breakpoint' ||
        (!continuous && Process.getCurrentThreadId() !== targetTid)
      )
        return false;
      // A syscall-return SIGTRAP has RIP immediately after 0f 05, not after int3.
      try {
        if (d.context.pc.sub(2).readU16() !== 0x050f) return false;
      } catch (e) {
        return false;
      }
      if (!continuous) armed = false;
      const registers = {};
      for (const k of [
        'pc',
        'sp',
        'rax',
        'rbx',
        'rcx',
        'rdx',
        'rsi',
        'rdi',
        'rbp',
        'r8',
        'r9',
        'r10',
        'r11',
        'r12',
        'r13',
        'r14',
        'r15',
      ])
        registers[k] = d.context[k].toString();
      let nativeFrames = [],
        nativeError = '';
      try {
        nativeFrames = Thread.backtrace(d.context, Backtracer.ACCURATE).map((p) => {
          const symbol = DebugSymbol.fromAddress(p), module = Process.findModuleByAddress(p);
          return { address: p.toString(), symbol: symbol.toString(), symbolName: symbol.name || '',
            functionOffset: functionOffset(p, symbol, module),
            module: module ? module.path : '', moduleName: module ? module.name : '' };
        });
      } catch (e) {
        nativeError = String(e);
      }
      let java = { source: 'Exception handler', frames: [] };
      try {
        if (!Java.available) java.error = 'No Java VM';
        else if (Java.vm.tryGetEnv() === null) java.error = 'Target TID has no Java environment';
        else java.frames = Java.backtrace({ limit: 64 }).frames;
      } catch (e) {
        java.error = String(e);
      }
      let stack = null;
      if (continuous)
        try {
          const r = Process.findRangeByAddress(d.context.sp);
          if (r)
            stack = d.context.sp.readByteArray(
              Math.min(4096, r.base.add(r.size).sub(d.context.sp).toUInt32())
            );
        } catch (e) {}
      send(
        {
          type: 'capture',
          tid: Process.getCurrentThreadId(),
          registers,
          nativeFrames,
          nativeError,
          java,
          javaStatus,
        },
        stack
      );
      return true;
    });
    return { javaStatus };
  },
  arm() {
    armed = true;
  },
  stop() {
    armed = false;
  },
};

```

`frida/bridge.py`:

```py
"""Bounded on-demand Frida operations. JSON in/out; diagnostics never enter stdout."""

import json, pathlib, subprocess, sys, threading, time
import frida, frida_tools


def execute(request, on_ready=None):
    snapshot = request["snapshot"]
    pid, tid = int(snapshot["pid"]), int(snapshot["tid"])
    if pid <= 0 or tid <= 0:
        raise ValueError("Invalid PID/TID")
    adb = request.get("adb", "adb")
    serial = request.get("serial", "emulator-5554")
    prefix = [adb, "-s", serial, "shell", "-T", "su", "0"]
    # The installed resolver verifies boot ID and PID start time against the capture.
    validation = {
        "pid": pid,
        "bootId": snapshot["connectionId"].split("/")[0],
        "eventNs": snapshot["capturedAtBootNs"],
        "addresses": [],
    }
    checked = subprocess.run(
        prefix + ["/data/local/tmp/ebpf-analyzer", "symbols"],
        input=json.dumps(validation) + "\n",
        text=True,
        capture_output=True,
        timeout=15,
        check=True,
    )
    answer = json.loads(checked.stdout)
    if "error" in answer:
        raise RuntimeError(answer["error"])
    identity = (
        subprocess.check_output(prefix + ["cat", f"/proc/{pid}/stat"], timeout=5)
        .split(b")")[-1]
        .split()[19]
    )
    device = frida.get_device(serial, timeout=5)
    session = device.attach(pid)
    helper = None
    script = None
    stop = threading.Event()
    captured = threading.Event()
    messages = []
    response = None
    try:
        if (
            subprocess.check_output(prefix + ["cat", f"/proc/{pid}/stat"], timeout=5)
            .split(b")")[-1]
            .split()[19]
            != identity
        ):
            raise RuntimeError("PID changed during attach")
        source = (pathlib.Path(frida_tools.__file__).parent / "bridges" / "java.js").read_text(
            encoding="utf-8"
        )
        source += "\n" + pathlib.Path(__file__).with_name("agent.js").read_text(encoding="utf-8")
        script = session.create_script(source)

        def message(m, data):
            if m.get("type") == "send" and m["payload"].get("type") == "capture":
                messages.append(m["payload"])
                captured.set()
            elif m.get("type") == "error":
                messages.append({"error": m.get("description", "Agent error")})
                captured.set()

        script.on("message", message)
        script.load()
        if request["action"] == "symbols":
            return {"results": script.exports_sync.symbols(request["addresses"])}
        if request["action"] != "capture":
            raise ValueError("Unknown action")
        op = snapshot.get("operation")
        if op not in ("send", "receive"):
            raise ValueError("Select a send or receive event")
        setup = script.exports_sync.prepare(tid)
        script.exports_sync.arm()
        helper = subprocess.Popen(
            prefix
            + [
                "/data/local/tmp/ebpf-analyzer",
                "frida-capture",
                str(pid),
                str(tid),
                op,
                str(snapshot["capturedAtBootNs"]),
            ],
            stdin=subprocess.PIPE,
            stdout=subprocess.PIPE,
            stderr=subprocess.PIPE,
            text=True,
        )

        def ping():
            while not stop.is_set():
                try:
                    helper.stdin.write("ping\n")
                    helper.stdin.flush()
                except (OSError, ValueError):
                    return
                stop.wait(0.5)

        heartbeat = threading.Thread(target=ping, daemon=True)
        heartbeat.start()
        ready = json.loads(helper.stdout.readline())
        if not ready.get("ready"):
            raise RuntimeError(ready.get("error", "Capture helper unavailable"))
        if on_ready is not None:
            on_ready()
        line = helper.stdout.readline()
        outcome = json.loads(line)
        helper.wait(timeout=3)
        if "error" in outcome:
            raise RuntimeError(outcome["error"])
        if outcome["SignalResult"] != 0:
            raise RuntimeError("Signal helper returned " + str(outcome["SignalResult"]))
        if not captured.wait(3):
            raise RuntimeError("Signal queued but no Frida capture received")
        event = messages[0]
        if "error" in event:
            raise RuntimeError(event["error"])
        saved = outcome["Saved"]
        registers = event["registers"]
        if (
            event["tid"] != tid
            or int(registers["pc"], 16) != saved[16]
            or int(registers["sp"], 16) != saved[19]
            or int(registers["rax"], 16) != saved[10]
        ):
            raise RuntimeError("Frida context did not match the syscall-exit event")
        event.update(
            {
                "pid": pid,
                "operation": op,
                "phase": "exit",
                "scope": "Next TCP "
                + op
                + " on selected TID; not the historical event or necessarily the same connection",
                "result": outcome["Result"],
                "resultUnit": "messages" if saved[15] in (299, 307) else "bytes",
                "syscall": saved[15],
                "enteredAtBootNs": str(outcome["Entered"]),
                "exitedAtBootNs": str(outcome["Exited"]),
                "skipped": outcome["Skipped"],
                "capturedAtEpochMs": int(time.time() * 1000),
                "setup": setup,
            }
        )
        response = event
        return response
    finally:
        # Stop kernel production first, then disarm the exception handler and detach.
        stop.set()
        if helper is not None:
            try:
                helper.stdin.close()
            except (OSError, ValueError):
                pass
            try:
                helper.wait(timeout=4)
            except subprocess.TimeoutExpired:
                helper.kill()
                helper.wait()
        if script is not None:
            try:
                script.exports_sync.stop()
            except frida.InvalidOperationError:
                pass
        # Frida may defer unload while an already-entered native call is blocked.
        # Capture is already disarmed; do not hold the UI waiting for cleanup.
        cancellation = frida.Cancellable()
        timer = threading.Timer(2, cancellation.cancel)
        timer.start()
        try:
            with cancellation:
                session.detach()
            if response is not None:
                response["cleanup"] = "Detached"
        except frida.OperationCancelledError:
            if response is not None:
                response["cleanup"] = "Capture disarmed; agent unload deferred"
        except frida.InvalidOperationError:
            pass
        finally:
            timer.cancel()


if __name__ == "__main__":
    try:
        raw = sys.stdin.buffer.readline(1024 * 1024 + 1)
        if len(raw) > 1024 * 1024:
            raise ValueError("Request exceeds limit")
        print(json.dumps(execute(json.loads(raw))))
    except Exception as error:
        print(json.dumps({"error": str(error)}))
        sys.exit(1)

```

`frida/stream.py`:

```py
"""PID-wide capture, JSON lines until stdin closes. No libc hooks."""

import base64, json, pathlib, queue, subprocess, sys, threading, time
import frida, frida_tools


def run(request, emit, on_ready=None, commands=None):
    snapshot = request["snapshot"]
    pid = int(snapshot["pid"])
    if pid <= 0:
        raise ValueError("Invalid PID")
    prefix = [
        request.get("adb", "adb"),
        "-s",
        request.get("serial", "emulator-5554"),
        "shell",
        "-T",
        "su",
        "0",
    ]
    validation = {
        "pid": pid,
        "bootId": snapshot["connectionId"].split("/")[0],
        "eventNs": snapshot["capturedAtBootNs"],
        "addresses": [],
    }
    answer = json.loads(
        subprocess.check_output(
            prefix + ["/data/local/tmp/ebpf-analyzer", "symbols"],
            input=(json.dumps(validation) + "\n").encode(),
            timeout=15,
        )
    )
    if "error" in answer:
        raise RuntimeError(answer["error"])

    def identity():
        return (
            subprocess.check_output(prefix + ["cat", f"/proc/{pid}/stat"], timeout=5)
            .rsplit(b")", 1)[1]
            .split()[19]
        )

    original = identity()
    session = frida.get_device(request.get("serial", "emulator-5554"), timeout=5).attach(pid)
    script = None
    helper = None
    stop = threading.Event()
    inbox = queue.Queue(maxsize=4096)
    inspections = queue.Queue(maxsize=16)
    lock = threading.Lock()

    def put(item):
        while not stop.is_set():
            try:
                inbox.put(item, timeout=0.2)
                return
            except queue.Full:
                pass

    def write(line):
        with lock:
            helper.stdin.write(line + "\n")
            helper.stdin.flush()

    try:
        if identity() != original:
            raise RuntimeError("PID changed during attach")
        source = (
            (pathlib.Path(frida_tools.__file__).parent / "bridges/java.js").read_text(
                encoding="utf-8"
            )
            + "\n"
            + pathlib.Path(__file__).with_name("agent.js").read_text(encoding="utf-8")
        )
        script = session.create_script(source)
        script.on("message", lambda m, d: put(("agent", m, d)))
        script.load()
        script.exports_sync.prepare(0, True)
        script.exports_sync.arm()
        helper = subprocess.Popen(
            prefix + ["/data/local/tmp/ebpf-analyzer", "frida-stream", str(pid)],
            stdin=subprocess.PIPE,
            stdout=subprocess.PIPE,
            stderr=subprocess.DEVNULL,
            text=True,
        )

        def heartbeat():
            while not stop.is_set():
                try:
                    write("ping")
                except (OSError, ValueError):
                    return
                stop.wait(0.5)

        def reader():
            for line in helper.stdout:
                try:
                    put(("kernel", json.loads(line), None))
                except ValueError:
                    put(("error", "Invalid helper response", None))
            put(("error", "Capture helper stopped", None))

        def control():
            for line in commands or sys.stdin:
                if line.strip() == "stop":
                    break
                try:
                    command = json.loads(line)
                    if command.get("action") in ("catalog", "members"):
                        inspections.put_nowait(command)
                except (ValueError, AttributeError, queue.Full):
                    put(("inspection", {"type": "catalogError", "error": "Invalid request or inspection queue is full"}, None))
            put(("stop", None, None))

        def inspect():
            while not stop.is_set():
                try:
                    command = inspections.get(timeout=0.25)
                except queue.Empty:
                    continue
                cancellation = frida.Cancellable()
                timer = threading.Timer(15, cancellation.cancel)
                timer.start()
                try:
                    with cancellation:
                        if command["action"] == "catalog":
                            result = script.exports_sync.catalog()
                            result["type"] = "catalog"
                        else:
                            result = script.exports_sync.members(command.get("kind", ""), command.get("name", ""))
                            result["type"] = "members"
                    put(("inspection", result, None))
                except Exception as error:
                    put(("inspection", {"type": "catalogError", "kind": command.get("kind", ""),
                        "name": command.get("name", ""), "error": str(error)}, None))
                finally:
                    timer.cancel()

        for fn in (heartbeat, reader, control, inspect):
            threading.Thread(target=fn, daemon=True).start()
        pending = {}
        captures = {}
        names = [
            "r15",
            "r14",
            "r13",
            "r12",
            "rbp",
            "rbx",
            "r11",
            "r10",
            "r9",
            "r8",
            "rax_saved",
            "rcx",
            "rdx",
            "rsi",
            "rdi",
            "orig_rax",
            "rip",
            "cs",
            "rflags",
            "rsp",
            "ss",
        ]

        def output(k, capture=None, data=None, reason="Captured"):
            saved = k["Saved"]
            stamp = str(k["Exited"])
            event = dict(capture or {})
            regs = {n: hex(v) for n, v in zip(names, saved)}
            event.update(
                type="event",
                pid=pid,
                tid=k["TID"],
                operation="send" if k["Operation"] == 1 else "receive",
                phase="exit",
                eventId=validation["bootId"] + "/frida/" + str(k["TID"]) + "/" + stamp,
                connectionId=validation["bootId"] + "/frida/" + stamp,
                capturedAtBootNs=stamp,
                capturedAtEpochMs=k.get("AtEpochMs", int(time.time() * 1000)),
                syscallPc=hex(saved[16] - 2),
                registers=regs,
                stack=base64.b64encode(data or b"").decode(),
                stackBase=hex(saved[19]),
                requestedIoBytes=str(k["Requested"]),
                result=k["Result"],
                resultUnit="messages" if saved[15] in (299, 307) else "bytes",
                status=reason,
                scope="All TCP I/O in selected PID",
            )
            event.setdefault("nativeFrames", [])
            event.setdefault("java", {"frames": [], "error": reason, "source": "Exception handler"})
            emit(event)

        while True:
            try:
                kind, value, data = inbox.get(timeout=0.25)
            except queue.Empty:
                kind = None
            if kind == "stop":
                break
            if kind == "error":
                raise RuntimeError(value)
            if kind == "inspection":
                emit(value)
            if kind == "kernel":
                if value.get("type") == "error":
                    raise RuntimeError(value["error"])
                if value.get("type") == "ready":
                    emit(value)
                    try:
                        inspections.put_nowait({"action": "catalog"})
                    except queue.Full:
                        pass
                    if on_ready:
                        on_ready()
                elif value.get("type") == "stats":
                    emit(value)
                elif "TID" in value:
                    if value["Flags"] == 1:
                        pending[value["TID"]] = [value, time.monotonic(), False]
                    else:
                        output(
                            value,
                            reason={
                                2: "Stack capture in flight",
                                3: "SIGTRAP blocked",
                                4: "Would block / restart",
                                5: "Signal failed",
                            }.get(value["Flags"], "Stack unavailable"),
                        )
            if kind == "agent":
                if value.get("type") == "error":
                    raise RuntimeError(value.get("description", "Agent error"))
                if value.get("type") == "send" and value["payload"].get("type") == "capture":
                    captures[value["payload"]["tid"]] = (value["payload"], data)
            for tid, (k, started, reported) in list(pending.items()):
                if tid in captures:
                    c, d = captures.pop(tid)
                    r = c["registers"]
                    s = k["Saved"]
                    if any(int(r[n], 16) != s[i] for n, i in [("pc", 16), ("sp", 19), ("rax", 10)]):
                        raise RuntimeError("Frida context does not match syscall exit")
                    output(k, c, d)
                    write(f"ack {tid} {k['Exited']}")
                    del pending[tid]
                elif not reported and time.monotonic() - started > 3:
                    output(k, reason="Waiting for signal delivery")
                    pending[tid][2] = True
    finally:
        stop.set()
        if helper:
            try:
                helper.stdin.close()
            except (OSError, ValueError):
                pass
            try:
                helper.wait(timeout=4)
            except subprocess.TimeoutExpired:
                helper.kill()
                helper.wait()
        if script:
            try:
                script.exports_sync.stop()
            except frida.InvalidOperationError:
                pass
        cancellation = frida.Cancellable()
        timer = threading.Timer(2, cancellation.cancel)
        timer.start()
        try:
            with cancellation:
                session.detach()
        except (frida.OperationCancelledError, frida.InvalidOperationError):
            pass
        finally:
            timer.cancel()


if __name__ == "__main__":

    def emit(value):
        print(json.dumps(value), flush=True)

    try:
        run(json.loads(sys.stdin.readline()), emit)
    except Exception as error:
        emit({"type": "error", "error": str(error)})
        sys.exit(1)

```

`go/cmd/device/main_linux_amd64.go`:

```go
//go:build linux && amd64

// ebpf-analyzer is the shared Android x86_64 device executable.
package main

import (
	"fmt"
	"os"

	"github.com/DoranekoSystems/ebpf-analyzer/internal/device/collector"
	"github.com/DoranekoSystems/ebpf-analyzer/internal/device/fridacapture"
	"github.com/DoranekoSystems/ebpf-analyzer/internal/device/fridastream"
	"github.com/DoranekoSystems/ebpf-analyzer/internal/device/symbols"
)

func main() {
	if len(os.Args) < 2 {
		usage()
		os.Exit(2)
	}
	command := os.Args[1]
	os.Args = append([]string{os.Args[0] + " " + command}, os.Args[2:]...)
	switch command {
	case "monitor":
		collector.Main()
	case "symbols":
		symbols.Main()
	case "frida-capture":
		fridacapture.Main()
	case "frida-stream":
		fridastream.Main()
	case "help", "-h", "--help":
		usage()
	default:
		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Unknown command: %s\n", command)
		usage()
		os.Exit(2)
	}
}

func usage() {
	fmt.Fprintln(os.Stderr, "Usage: ebpf-analyzer <monitor|symbols|frida-capture|frida-stream> [arguments]")
}

```

`go/cmd/monitor/main.go`:

```go
package main

import (
	"bufio"
	"context"
	"encoding/json"
	"flag"
	"fmt"
	"github.com/DoranekoSystems/ebpf-analyzer/internal/attribution"
	"github.com/DoranekoSystems/ebpf-analyzer/internal/monitor"
	"log"
	"os"
	"os/exec"
	"os/signal"
	"path/filepath"
	"regexp"
	"time"
)

func main() {
	if err := run(); err != nil {
		log.Fatal(err)
	}
}
func run() error {
	serial := flag.String("serial", "emulator-5554", "adb serial")
	adb := flag.String("adb", "adb", "adb executable")
	remote := flag.String("remote", "/data/local/tmp/ebpf-analyzer", "Android collector binary")
	iface := flag.String("interface", "any", "device interface to copy; any = all; empty disables packet copies")
	output := flag.String("output", "captures/monitor.json", "passive extension snapshot")
	duration := flag.Duration("duration", 0, "stop after duration; default Ctrl+C")
	userStack := flag.Bool("user-stack", true, "capture x86_64 connect registers/raw stack; false for unsupported devices")
	ioEvents := flag.Bool("io-events", true, "capture independent x86_64 TCP send/receive entry snapshots")
	flag.Parse()
	if !regexp.MustCompile(`^/[A-Za-z0-9/_.-]+$`).MatchString(*remote) || !regexp.MustCompile(`^[A-Za-z0-9_.-]*$`).MatchString(*iface) {
		return fmt.Errorf("invalid remote path or interface")
	}
	if err := os.MkdirAll(filepath.Dir(*output), 0755); err != nil {
		return err
	}
	ctx, stop := signal.NotifyContext(context.Background(), os.Interrupt)
	defer stop()
	if *duration > 0 {
		var cancel context.CancelFunc
		ctx, cancel = context.WithTimeout(ctx, *duration)
		defer cancel()
	}
	args := []string{"-s", *serial, "shell", "-T", "su", "0", *remote, "monitor", "-control"}
	if *userStack {
		args = append(args, "-user-stack")
	}
	if *ioEvents {
		args = append(args, "-io-events")
	}
	if *iface != "" {
		args = append(args, "-capture-interface", *iface)
	}
	cmd := exec.Command(*adb, args...)
	cmd.Stderr = os.Stderr
	stdin, err := cmd.StdinPipe()
	if err != nil {
		return err
	}
	stdout, err := cmd.StdoutPipe()
	if err != nil {
		return err
	}
	if err = cmd.Start(); err != nil {
		return err
	}
	events := make(chan attribution.Batch, 4)
	done := make(chan error, 1)
	readerCtx, cancelReader := context.WithCancel(context.Background())
	defer cancelReader()
	go func() {
		scanner := bufio.NewScanner(stdout)
		scanner.Buffer(make([]byte, 65536), 16*1024*1024)
		var readErr error
		for scanner.Scan() {
			var b attribution.Batch
			if err := json.Unmarshal(scanner.Bytes(), &b); err != nil {
				readErr = err
				break
			}
			if b.SchemaVersion != 1 {
				readErr = fmt.Errorf("unsupported collector schema")
				break
			}
			select {
			case events <- b:
			case <-readerCtx.Done():
			}
		}
		if readErr == nil {
			readErr = scanner.Err()
		}
		if readErr != nil {
			stdin.Close()
		}
		waitErr := cmd.Wait()
		if readErr != nil {
			done <- readErr
		} else {
			done <- waitErr
		}
	}()
	state := monitor.NewState()
	finished := false
	defer func() {
		cancelReader()
		stdin.Close()
		if !finished {
			select {
			case <-done:
			case <-time.After(6 * time.Second):
				cmd.Process.Kill()
				<-done
			}
		}
		state.Running = false
		state.UpdatedAt = time.Now().UnixMilli()
		if err := monitor.Write(*output, state.Snapshot); err != nil {
			log.Printf("final snapshot: %v", err)
		}
	}()
	absolute, _ := filepath.Abs(*output)
	log.Printf("Passive monitor: %s (no proxy or adb forward required)", absolute)
	ticker := time.NewTicker(time.Second)
	defer ticker.Stop()
	if _, err = fmt.Fprintln(stdin, "ping"); err != nil {
		return err
	}
	for {
		select {
		case <-ctx.Done():
			return nil
		case err := <-done:
			finished = true
			if err == nil {
				return fmt.Errorf("collector stopped unexpectedly")
			}
			return err
		case <-ticker.C:
			if _, err := fmt.Fprintln(stdin, "ping"); err != nil {
				return err
			}
		case batch := <-events:
			var packets []monitor.Packet
			if len(batch.Packets) > 0 {
				if err := json.Unmarshal(batch.Packets, &packets); err != nil {
					return err
				}
			}
			state.Add(batch.Connections, packets)
			state.AddOrigins(batch.Origins)
			state.OriginDrops = batch.OriginDrops
			state.UpdatedAt = time.Now().UnixMilli()
			state.Drops = batch.Drops
			state.CopyDrops = batch.CopyDrops
			if err := monitor.Write(*output, state.Snapshot); err != nil {
				return err
			}
		}
	}
}

```

package attribution

import (
	"encoding/json"
	"github.com/DoranekoSystems/ebpf-analyzer/internal/origin"
)

// Observation endpoints are always device-side. They are not silently NAT-rewritten.
type Observation struct {
	ConnectionID string `json:"connectionId"`
	ClientIP     string `json:"clientIp"`
	ClientPort   int    `json:"clientPort"`
	ServerIP     string `json:"serverIp"`
	ServerPort   int    `json:"serverPort"`
	From         int64  `json:"validFromEpochMs"`
	To           int64  `json:"validToEpochMs"`
	PID          uint32 `json:"pid"`
	TID          uint32 `json:"tid"`
	UID          uint32 `json:"uid"`
	AppName      string `json:"appName"`
	PackageName  string `json:"packageName,omitempty"`
	ProcessName  string `json:"processName"`
	Closed       bool   `json:"closed"`
}

type Batch struct {
	SchemaVersion int               `json:"schemaVersion"`
	SampledAt     int64             `json:"sampledAtEpochMs"`
	Drops         uint64            `json:"drops"`
	Connections   []Observation     `json:"connections"`
	Packets       json.RawMessage   `json:"packets,omitempty"`
	CopyDrops     uint64            `json:"copyDrops,omitempty"`
	Origins       []origin.Snapshot `json:"origins,omitempty"`
	OriginDrops   uint64            `json:"originDrops,omitempty"`
}

`go/internal/attribution/packages.go`:

```go
package attribution

import (
	"fmt"
	"os"
	"sort"
	"strconv"
	"strings"
	"time"
)

// PackageResolver watches Android's UID mapping, including installs during capture.
// A failed read keeps the last good mapping and is retried after a short backoff.
type PackageResolver struct {
	Path     string
	Names    map[uint32][]string
	modified time.Time
	size     int64
	checked  time.Time
	retry    time.Time
}

func (r *PackageResolver) Refresh(now time.Time) error {
	if now.Before(r.retry) {
		return nil
	}
	info, err := os.Stat(r.Path)
	if err != nil {
		r.retry = now.Add(5 * time.Second)
		return err
	}
	if r.Names != nil && info.ModTime().Equal(r.modified) && info.Size() == r.size && now.Sub(r.checked) < 5*time.Second {
		return nil
	}
	data, err := os.ReadFile(r.Path)
	if err != nil {
		r.retry = now.Add(5 * time.Second)
		return err
	}
	names := map[uint32][]string{}
	for _, line := range strings.Split(string(data), "\n") {
		fields := strings.Fields(line)
		if len(fields) < 2 {
			continue
		}
		uid, err := strconv.ParseUint(fields[1], 10, 32)
		if err != nil {
			continue
		}
		names[uint32(uid)] = append(names[uint32(uid)], fields[0])
	}
	if len(names) == 0 {
		r.retry = now.Add(5 * time.Second)
		return fmt.Errorf("empty or invalid package list: %s", r.Path)
	}
	for _, values := range names {
		sort.Strings(values)
	}
	r.Names, r.modified, r.size, r.checked = names, info.ModTime(), info.Size(), now
	r.retry = time.Time{}
	return nil
}

```

`go/internal/attribution/packages_test.go`:

```go
package attribution

import (
	"os"
	"path/filepath"
	"testing"
	"time"
)

func TestPackageRefreshDuringCapture(t *testing.T) {
	path := filepath.Join(t.TempDir(), "packages.list")
	now := time.Now()
	r := PackageResolver{Path: path}
	write := func(data string) {
		t.Helper()
		if err := os.WriteFile(path, []byte(data), 0600); err != nil {
			t.Fatal(err)
		}
	}
	write("old.app 10001 0 /data/user/0/old.app\n")
	if err := r.Refresh(now); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	write("old.app 10001 0 /data/user/0/old.app\nnew.app 10203 0 /data/user/0/new.app\n")
	if err := r.Refresh(now.Add(time.Millisecond)); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if got := r.Names[10203]; len(got) != 1 || got[0] != "new.app" {
		t.Fatalf("new install unresolved: %v", got)
	}
	write("")
	if err := r.Refresh(now.Add(time.Second)); err == nil {
		t.Fatal("empty read accepted")
	}
	if len(r.Names[10203]) != 1 {
		t.Fatal("lost last good mapping")
	}
	write("replacement.app 10204 0 /data/user/0/replacement.app\n")
	if err := r.Refresh(now.Add(7 * time.Second)); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if len(r.Names[10203]) != 0 || len(r.Names[10204]) != 1 {
		t.Fatal("install/uninstall mapping stale")
	}
	if err := os.Remove(path); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if err := r.Refresh(now.Add(8 * time.Second)); err == nil {
		t.Fatal("missing file accepted")
	}
	if len(r.Names[10204]) != 1 {
		t.Fatal("lost mapping after read failure")
	}
}

```

`go/internal/control/control.go`:

```go
package control

import (
	"bufio"
	"context"
	"io"
	"time"
)

// WatchControl bounds the lifetime of an orphaned collector even when adbd fails to
// deliver stdin EOF after a host transport disconnect. The caller owns the reader.
func WatchControl(ctx context.Context, cancel context.CancelFunc, r io.Reader, lease time.Duration) {
	activity := make(chan bool, 1)
	go func() {
		scanner := bufio.NewScanner(r)
		scanner.Buffer(make([]byte, 64), 64)
		for scanner.Scan() {
			if scanner.Text() != "ping" {
				break
			}
			select {
			case activity <- true:
			case <-ctx.Done():
				return
			}
		}
		select {
		case activity <- false:
		case <-ctx.Done():
		}
	}()
	timer := time.NewTimer(lease)
	defer timer.Stop()
	for {
		select {
		case <-ctx.Done():
			return
		case <-timer.C:
			cancel()
			return
		case alive := <-activity:
			if !alive {
				cancel()
				return
			}
			if !timer.Stop() {
				select {
				case <-timer.C:
				default:
				}
			}
			timer.Reset(lease)
		}
	}
}

```

`go/internal/control/control_test.go`:

```go
package control

import (
	"context"
	"io"
	"testing"
	"time"
)

func TestControlLeaseCancelsOrphanWithoutEOF(t *testing.T) {
	ctx, cancel := context.WithCancel(context.Background())
	defer cancel()
	r, w := io.Pipe()
	defer r.Close()
	defer w.Close()
	go WatchControl(ctx, cancel, r, 30*time.Millisecond)
	select {
	case <-ctx.Done():
	case <-time.After(time.Second):
		t.Fatal("orphan lease did not expire")
	}
}

func TestControlEOFCancelsImmediately(t *testing.T) {
	ctx, cancel := context.WithCancel(context.Background())
	defer cancel()
	r, w := io.Pipe()
	defer r.Close()
	go WatchControl(ctx, cancel, r, time.Hour)
	w.Close()
	select {
	case <-ctx.Done():
	case <-time.After(time.Second):
		t.Fatal("EOF did not cancel collector")
	}
}

`go/internal/device/collector/capture_linux.go`:

```go
package collector

import (
	"context"
	"encoding/binary"
	"errors"
	"fmt"
	"github.com/DoranekoSystems/ebpf-analyzer/internal/monitor"
	"github.com/cilium/ebpf"
	"github.com/cilium/ebpf/asm"
	"golang.org/x/sys/unix"
	"net"
	"sync"
	"time"
)

type copies struct {
	sync.Mutex
	packets []monitor.Packet
	drops   uint64
	err     error
	fd      int
}

// This filter is attached only to our AF_PACKET socket. It limits the private
// copy to 65535 bytes; it cannot drop, redirect or modify the original traffic.
// Userspace stores only the first 2048 bytes and reports the original length.
func startCopies(ctx context.Context, iface string) (*copies, func(), error) {
	index := 0
	if iface != "any" {
		dev, err := net.InterfaceByName(iface)
		if err != nil {
			return nil, nil, err
		}
		index = dev.Index
	}
	program, err := ebpf.NewProgram(&ebpf.ProgramSpec{Name: "monitor_copy", Type: ebpf.SocketFilter, License: "GPL", Instructions: asm.Instructions{asm.Mov.Imm(asm.R0, 65535), asm.Return()}})
	if err != nil {
		return nil, nil, err
	}
	defer program.Close()
	// ETH_P_ALL in network byte order, supported collector architectures are little endian.
	fd, err := unix.Socket(unix.AF_PACKET, unix.SOCK_DGRAM|unix.SOCK_CLOEXEC|unix.SOCK_NONBLOCK, 0x0300)
	if err != nil {
		return nil, nil, err
	}
	fail := func(err error) (*copies, func(), error) { unix.Close(fd); return nil, nil, err }
	if err = unix.SetsockoptInt(fd, unix.SOL_SOCKET, unix.SO_ATTACH_BPF, program.FD()); err != nil {
		return fail(err)
	}
	if err = unix.SetsockoptInt(fd, unix.SOL_SOCKET, unix.SO_TIMESTAMPNS, 1); err != nil {
		return fail(err)
	}
	if err = unix.Bind(fd, &unix.SockaddrLinklayer{Protocol: 0x0300, Ifindex: index}); err != nil {
		return fail(err)
	}
	state := &copies{fd: fd}
	child, cancel := context.WithCancel(ctx)
	done := make(chan struct{})
	go func() {
		defer close(done)
		defer unix.Close(fd)
		buf := make([]byte, 2048)
		oob := make([]byte, 256)
		for child.Err() == nil {
			n, oobn, _, from, err := unix.Recvmsg(fd, buf, oob, unix.MSG_TRUNC)
			if errors.Is(err, unix.EAGAIN) || errors.Is(err, unix.EINTR) {
				unix.Poll([]unix.PollFd{{Fd: int32(fd), Events: unix.POLLIN}}, 100)
				continue
			}
			if err != nil {
				state.Lock()
				state.err = fmt.Errorf("packet copy: %w", err)
				state.Unlock()
				return
			}
			length := n
			if length > len(buf) {
				length = len(buf)
			}
			at := time.Now().UnixMilli()
			messages, parseErr := unix.ParseSocketControlMessage(oob[:oobn])
			if parseErr == nil {
				for _, m := range messages {
					if m.Header.Level == unix.SOL_SOCKET && m.Header.Type == unix.SO_TIMESTAMPNS && len(m.Data) >= 16 {
						at = int64(binary.NativeEndian.Uint64(m.Data[:8]))*1000 + int64(binary.NativeEndian.Uint64(m.Data[8:16]))/1e6
					}
				}
			}
			p, ok := monitor.Decode(buf[:length], n, at)
			if address, ok := from.(*unix.SockaddrLinklayer); ok {
				p.InterfaceIndex = address.Ifindex
			}
			if !ok {
				continue
			}
			state.Lock()
			if len(state.packets) < 512 {
				state.packets = append(state.packets, p)
			} else {
				state.drops++
			}
			state.Unlock()
		}
	}()
	return state, func() { cancel(); <-done }, nil
}

func (c *copies) drain() ([]monitor.Packet, uint64, error) {
	c.Lock()
	defer c.Unlock()
	packets := c.packets
	c.packets = nil
	stats, err := unix.GetsockoptTpacketStats(c.fd, unix.SOL_PACKET, unix.PACKET_STATISTICS)
	if err != nil {
		return nil, c.drops, err
	}
	c.drops += uint64(stats.Drops)
	return packets, c.drops, c.err
}

```

`go/internal/device/collector/main_linux.go`:

```go
//go:build linux

package collector

import (
	"bytes"
	"context"
	_ "embed"
	"encoding/json"
	"flag"
	"fmt"
	"io"
	"log"
	"net"
	"os"
	"os/signal"
	"regexp"
	"runtime"
	"strings"
	"syscall"
	"time"

	"github.com/DoranekoSystems/ebpf-analyzer/internal/attribution"
	"github.com/DoranekoSystems/ebpf-analyzer/internal/control"
	"github.com/DoranekoSystems/ebpf-analyzer/internal/origin"
	"github.com/cilium/ebpf"
	"github.com/cilium/ebpf/link"
	"github.com/cilium/ebpf/rlimit"
	"golang.org/x/sys/unix"
)

//go:embed tcp_bpfel.o
var object []byte

type flow struct {
	Start, Established, End                  uint64
	PID, TID, UID, State                     uint32
	SourcePort, DestinationPort, Family, Pad uint16
	Source, Destination, Comm                [16]byte
}

func Main() {
	if err := run(); err != nil {
		log.Fatal(err)
	}
}

func run() error {
	interval := flag.Duration("interval", 500*time.Millisecond, "sampling interval")
	duration := flag.Duration("duration", 0, "exit after this duration (0 = until signal)")
	selfTest := flag.Bool("self-test", false, "create one loopback TCP connection from this collector")
	dial := flag.String("dial", "", "test client only: connect to IP:port without loading BPF")
	payload := flag.String("payload", "", "test client payload; verify byte-for-byte echo after half-close")
	captureInterface := flag.String("capture-interface", "", "passively copy IP packets on this interface (e.g. eth0)")
	controlEnabled := flag.Bool("control", false, "require stdin ping lease; detach when the host disconnects")
	userStack := flag.Bool("user-stack", false, "copy saved x86_64 connect registers and up to 4 KiB user stack at SYN_SENT")
	ioEvents := flag.Bool("io-events", false, "capture per-call TCP send/receive entry registers and raw stacks (x86_64)")
	flag.Parse()
	if *dial != "" {
		conn, err := net.DialTimeout("tcp", *dial, 3*time.Second)
		if err != nil {
			return err
		}
		defer conn.Close()
		conn.SetDeadline(time.Now().Add(10 * time.Second))
		if err := json.NewEncoder(os.Stdout).Encode(map[string]any{"pid": os.Getpid(), "client": conn.LocalAddr().String()}); err != nil {
			return err
		}
		if *payload != "" {
			if _, err := io.WriteString(conn, *payload); err != nil {
				return err
			}
			if err := conn.(*net.TCPConn).CloseWrite(); err != nil {
				return err
			}
			data, err := io.ReadAll(conn)
			if err != nil {
				return err
			}
			if string(data) != *payload {
				return fmt.Errorf("echo payload mismatch")
			}
		} else {
			time.Sleep(3 * time.Second)
		}
		return nil
	}
	if *interval < 100*time.Millisecond {
		return fmt.Errorf("interval must be at least 100ms")
	}
	if err := validateFormat(); err != nil {
		return err
	}
	if err := rlimit.RemoveMemlock(); err != nil {
		log.Printf("memlock: %v", err)
	}
	spec, err := ebpf.LoadCollectionSpecFromReader(bytes.NewReader(object))
	if err != nil {
		return err
	}
	collection, err := ebpf.NewCollection(spec)
	if err != nil {
		return fmt.Errorf("load BPF (SELinux/capabilities may restrict this): %w", err)
	}
	defer collection.Close()
	if *userStack || *ioEvents {
		if runtime.GOARCH != "amd64" || collection.Maps["origin_enabled"] == nil {
			return fmt.Errorf("user-stack requires the x86_64 BPF/collector build")
		}
		var zero uint32
		var enabled uint32
		if *userStack {
			enabled |= 1
		}
		if *ioEvents {
			enabled |= 2
		}
		if err := collection.Maps["origin_enabled"].Update(zero, enabled, ebpf.UpdateAny); err != nil {
			return err
		}
		log.Printf("saved registers/raw stack: connect=%v I/O=%v (4 KiB limit, x86_64)", *userStack, *ioEvents)
	}
	if *ioEvents {
		for _, probe := range []struct{ name, program string }{{"inet_sock_destruct", "trace_destruct"}, {"tcp_sendmsg", "trace_send"}, {"tcp_recvmsg", "trace_receive"}} {
			attachment, err := link.Kprobe(probe.name, collection.Programs[probe.program], nil)
			if err != nil {
				return fmt.Errorf("attach I/O event %s: %w", probe.name, err)
			}
			defer attachment.Close()
		}
		log.Print("attached TCP send/receive entry events; these are not IP packet events")
	}
	attachment, err := link.Tracepoint("sock", "inet_sock_set_state", collection.Programs["trace_state"], nil)
	if err != nil {
		return fmt.Errorf("attach tracepoint: %w", err)
	}
	defer attachment.Close()
	log.Printf("attached sock:inet_sock_set_state; collector PID=%d", os.Getpid())
	ctx, cancel := signal.NotifyContext(context.Background(), os.Interrupt, syscall.SIGTERM)
	defer cancel()
	if *controlEnabled {
		go control.WatchControl(ctx, cancel, os.Stdin, 5*time.Second)
	}
	if *duration > 0 {
		var stop context.CancelFunc
		ctx, stop = context.WithTimeout(ctx, *duration)
		defer stop()
	}
	if *selfTest {
		if err := testConnection(); err != nil {
			return err
		}
	}
	boot, err := os.ReadFile("/proc/sys/kernel/random/boot_id")
	if err != nil {
		return err
	}
	// A run ID prevents accidental collisions across collector restarts within one boot.
	runID := fmt.Sprintf("%s/%d", strings.TrimSpace(string(boot)), time.Now().UnixNano())
	packages := &attribution.PackageResolver{Path: "/data/system/packages.list"}
	var capture *copies
	if *captureInterface != "" {
		var cleanup func()
		capture, cleanup, err = startCopies(ctx, *captureInterface)
		if err != nil {
			return fmt.Errorf("start passive copies: %w", err)
		}
		defer cleanup()
		log.Printf("passive packet copies on %s; original traffic unchanged", *captureInterface)
	}
	// Anchor boot time once: resampling rounded milliseconds makes SYN time jitter.
	var anchor unix.Timespec
	if err := unix.ClockGettime(unix.CLOCK_BOOTTIME, &anchor); err != nil {
		return err
	}
	epochOffset := (time.Now().UnixNano() - anchor.Nano()) / 1e6
	ticker := time.NewTicker(*interval)
	defer ticker.Stop()
	encoder := json.NewEncoder(os.Stdout)
	for {
		if err := sample(collection, encoder, runID, packages, capture, epochOffset); err != nil {
			return err
		}
		select {
		case <-ctx.Done():
			return nil
		case <-ticker.C:
		}
	}
}

func sample(c *ebpf.Collection, encoder *json.Encoder, runID string, packages *attribution.PackageResolver, capture *copies, offset int64) error {
	now := time.Now().UnixMilli()
	if err := packages.Refresh(time.Now()); err != nil {
		log.Printf("package resolution refresh unavailable: %v", err)
	}
	batch := attribution.Batch{SchemaVersion: 1, SampledAt: now, Connections: []attribution.Observation{}}
	var originKeys []uint64
	if origins := c.Maps["origins"]; origins != nil {
		var zero uint32
		if err := c.Maps["origin_drops"].Lookup(zero, &batch.OriginDrops); err != nil {
			return err
		}
		it := origins.Iterate()
		var key uint64
		var raw origin.Raw
		for it.Next(&key, &raw) {
			snapshot, err := origin.Decode(raw, runID)
			if err != nil {
				return err
			}
			batch.Origins = append(batch.Origins, snapshot)
			originKeys = append(originKeys, key)
			if len(originKeys) >= 128 {
				break
			}
		}
		if err := it.Err(); err != nil {
			return err
		}
	}
	if capture != nil {
		packets, drops, err := capture.drain()
		if err != nil {
			return err
		}
		batch.Packets, err = json.Marshal(packets)
		if err != nil {
			return err
		}
		batch.CopyDrops = drops
	}
	var zero uint32
	if err := c.Maps["drops"].Lookup(zero, &batch.Drops); err != nil {
		return err
	}
	iterator := c.Maps["flows"].Iterate()
	var key uint64
	var f flow
	var completed []uint64
	for iterator.Next(&key, &f) {
		if f.End != 0 {
			completed = append(completed, key)
		}
		if f.Established == 0 || f.SourcePort == 0 {
			continue
		}
		end := now
		if f.End != 0 {
			// Include the final millisecond in the exclusive upper bound.
			end = int64(f.End/1e6) + offset + 1
		}
		start := int64(f.Start/1e6) + offset
		if end <= start {
			end = start + 1
		}
		o := observation(f, runID, packages.Names, offset, now)
		o.From, o.To = start, end
		batch.Connections = append(batch.Connections, o)
	}
	if err := iterator.Err(); err != nil {
		return err
	}
	if err := encoder.Encode(batch); err != nil {
		return err
	}
	for _, id := range originKeys {
		if err := c.Maps["origins"].Delete(id); err != nil {
			return err
		}
	}
	for _, id := range completed {
		if err := c.Maps["flows"].Delete(id); err != nil {
			return err
		}
	}
	return nil
}

func observation(f flow, runID string, packages map[uint32][]string, offset, now int64) attribution.Observation {
	app := fmt.Sprintf("Unknown (UID %d)", f.UID)
	pkg := ""
	if names := packages[f.UID]; len(names) == 1 {
		app, pkg = names[0], names[0]
	} else if len(names) > 1 {
		app = fmt.Sprintf("Shared UID %d (%d packages)", f.UID, len(names))
	}
	return attribution.Observation{ConnectionID: fmt.Sprintf("%s/%d", runID, f.Start),
		ClientIP: address(f.Source, f.Family), ClientPort: int(f.SourcePort), ServerIP: address(f.Destination, f.Family), ServerPort: int(f.DestinationPort),
		From: int64(f.Start/1e6) + offset, To: now, PID: f.PID, TID: f.TID, UID: f.UID, AppName: app, PackageName: pkg,
		ProcessName: strings.TrimRight(string(f.Comm[:]), "\x00"), Closed: f.End != 0}
}

func address(raw [16]byte, family uint16) string {
	if family == 2 {
		return net.IP(raw[:4]).String()
	}
	return net.IP(raw[:]).String()
}

func validateFormat() error {
	path := "/sys/kernel/tracing/events/sock/inet_sock_set_state/format"
	data, err := os.ReadFile(path)
	if err != nil {
		return fmt.Errorf("read tracepoint ABI: %w", err)
	}
	for _, field := range []struct {
		name         string
		offset, size int
	}{
		{"skaddr", 8, 8}, {"oldstate", 16, 4}, {"newstate", 20, 4}, {"sport", 24, 2},
		{"dport", 26, 2}, {"family", 28, 2}, {"protocol", 30, 2}, {"saddr", 32, 4},
		{"daddr", 36, 4}, {"saddr_v6", 40, 16}, {"daddr_v6", 56, 16},
	} {
		pattern := fmt.Sprintf(`field:[^;]*\b%s(?:\[\d+\])?;\s*offset:%d;\s*size:%d;`, field.name, field.offset, field.size)
		if !regexp.MustCompile(pattern).Match(data) {
			return fmt.Errorf("unsupported tracepoint ABI for %s", field.name)
		}
	}
	return nil
}

func testConnection() error {
	listener, err := net.Listen("tcp4", "127.0.0.1:0")
	if err != nil {
		return err
	}
	defer listener.Close()
	done := make(chan error, 1)
	go func() {
		conn, err := listener.Accept()
		if err == nil {
			conn.Close()
		}
		done <- err
	}()
	conn, err := net.DialTimeout("tcp4", listener.Addr().String(), time.Second)
	if err != nil {
		return err
	}
	log.Printf("self-test: PID=%d %s -> %s", os.Getpid(), conn.LocalAddr(), conn.RemoteAddr())
	conn.Close()
	return <-done
}

`go/internal/device/fridacapture/main_linux.go`:

```go
//go:build linux && amd64

package fridacapture

import (
	"bufio"
	"bytes"
	_ "embed"
	"encoding/binary"
	"encoding/json"
	"fmt"
	"github.com/cilium/ebpf"
	"github.com/cilium/ebpf/link"
	"github.com/cilium/ebpf/rlimit"
	"golang.org/x/sys/unix"
	"os"
	"strconv"
	"strings"
	"time"
)

//go:embed frida_bpfel.o
var program []byte

type state struct {
	PID, TID, Operation, Armed uint32
	Pending, Done              uint32
	SignalResult               int32
	Skipped                    uint32
	Deadline, Entered, Exited  uint64
	Result                     int64
	Saved                      [21]uint64
}

func Main() {
	if err := run(); err != nil {
		json.NewEncoder(os.Stdout).Encode(map[string]string{"error": err.Error()})
		os.Exit(1)
	}
}
func run() error {
	if len(os.Args) != 5 {
		return fmt.Errorf("expected PID TID send|receive eventBootNs")
	}
	pid, e := strconv.ParseUint(os.Args[1], 10, 32)
	if e != nil || pid == 0 {
		return fmt.Errorf("invalid PID")
	}
	tid, e := strconv.ParseUint(os.Args[2], 10, 32)
	if e != nil || tid == 0 {
		return fmt.Errorf("invalid TID")
	}
	operation := uint32(2)
	if os.Args[3] == "send" {
		operation = 1
	} else if os.Args[3] != "receive" {
		return fmt.Errorf("unsupported operation")
	}
	status, e := os.ReadFile(fmt.Sprintf("/proc/%d/task/%d/status", pid, tid))
	if e != nil {
		return e
	}
	eventNS, e := strconv.ParseUint(os.Args[4], 10, 64)
	if e != nil {
		return e
	}
	stat, e := os.ReadFile(fmt.Sprintf("/proc/%d/task/%d/stat", pid, tid))
	if e != nil {
		return e
	}
	end := strings.LastIndex(string(stat), ")")
	if end < 0 {
		return fmt.Errorf("invalid thread stat")
	}
	fields := strings.Fields(string(stat[end+1:]))
	if len(fields) < 20 {
		return fmt.Errorf("short thread stat")
	}
	started, e := strconv.ParseUint(fields[19], 10, 64)
	if e != nil {
		return e
	}
	aux, e := os.ReadFile("/proc/self/auxv")
	if e != nil {
		return e
	}
	var hz uint64
	for i := 0; i+16 <= len(aux); i += 16 {
		if binary.LittleEndian.Uint64(aux[i:]) == 17 {
			hz = binary.LittleEndian.Uint64(aux[i+8:])
			break
		}
	}
	if hz == 0 || started/hz > eventNS/1e9 || (started/hz == eventNS/1e9 && (started%hz)*1e9/hz > eventNS%1e9) {
		return fmt.Errorf("TID was reused since the selected event")
	}
	for _, line := range strings.Split(string(status), "\n") {
		if strings.HasPrefix(line, "SigBlk:") {
			v, e := strconv.ParseUint(strings.TrimSpace(strings.TrimPrefix(line, "SigBlk:")), 16, 64)
			if e != nil || v&(1<<4) != 0 {
				return fmt.Errorf("target blocks SIGTRAP")
			}
		}
	}
	if e = rlimit.RemoveMemlock(); e != nil {
		return e
	}
	spec, e := ebpf.LoadCollectionSpecFromReader(bytes.NewReader(program))
	if e != nil {
		return e
	}
	c, e := ebpf.NewCollection(spec)
	if e != nil {
		return e
	}
	defer c.Close()
	hook, e := link.AttachRawTracepoint(link.RawTracepointOptions{Name: "sys_exit", Program: c.Programs["io_exit"]})
	if e != nil {
		return e
	}
	defer hook.Close()
	name, prog := "tcp_recvmsg", "receive_entry"
	if operation == 1 {
		name, prog = "tcp_sendmsg", "send_entry"
	}
	entry, e := link.Kprobe(name, c.Programs[prog], nil)
	if e != nil {
		return e
	}
	defer entry.Close()
	var ts unix.Timespec
	if e = unix.ClockGettime(unix.CLOCK_BOOTTIME, &ts); e != nil {
		return e
	}
	s := state{PID: uint32(pid), TID: uint32(tid), Operation: operation, Armed: 1, Deadline: uint64(ts.Nano()) + 15_000_000_000}
	key := uint32(0)
	if e = c.Maps["control"].Put(key, &s); e != nil {
		return e
	}
	defer func() { s.Armed = 0; c.Maps["control"].Put(key, &s) }()
	lease := make(chan struct{}, 1)
	closed := make(chan struct{})
	go func() {
		defer close(closed)
		scanner := bufio.NewScanner(os.Stdin)
		for scanner.Scan() {
			if scanner.Text() == "ping" {
				select {
				case lease <- struct{}{}:
				default:
				}
			}
		}
	}()
	fmt.Println(`{"ready":true}`)
	poll := time.NewTicker(20 * time.Millisecond)
	defer poll.Stop()
	timeout := time.NewTimer(16 * time.Second)
	defer timeout.Stop()
	heartbeat := time.NewTimer(3 * time.Second)
	defer heartbeat.Stop()
	for {
		select {
		case <-closed:
			return fmt.Errorf("controller disconnected")
		case <-timeout.C:
			return fmt.Errorf("no completed TCP %s on TID %d; skipped %d readiness/restart/unsupported calls", os.Args[3], tid, s.Skipped)
		case <-heartbeat.C:
			return fmt.Errorf("controller lease expired")
		case <-lease:
			if !heartbeat.Stop() {
				select {
				case <-heartbeat.C:
				default:
				}
			}
			heartbeat.Reset(3 * time.Second)
		case <-poll.C:
			if e = c.Maps["control"].Lookup(key, &s); e != nil {
				return e
			}
			if s.Done != 0 {
				return json.NewEncoder(os.Stdout).Encode(s)
			}
		}
	}
}
