`go/internal/device/fridastream/main_linux.go`:

```go
//go:build linux && amd64

package fridastream

import (
	"bufio"
	"bytes"
	_ "embed"
	"encoding/binary"
	"encoding/json"
	"fmt"
	"github.com/cilium/ebpf"
	"github.com/cilium/ebpf/btf"
	"github.com/cilium/ebpf/link"
	"github.com/cilium/ebpf/ringbuf"
	"github.com/cilium/ebpf/rlimit"
	"golang.org/x/sys/unix"
	"os"
	"strconv"
	"strings"
	"time"
)

//go:embed fridastream_bpfel.o
var program []byte

type event struct {
	PID, TID, Operation, Flags uint32
	SignalResult               int32
	Pad                        uint32
	Entered, Exited, Requested uint64
	Result                     int64
	Saved                      [21]uint64
}

func Main() {
	if e := run(); e != nil {
		json.NewEncoder(os.Stdout).Encode(map[string]string{"type": "error", "error": e.Error()})
		os.Exit(1)
	}
}
func run() error {
	if len(os.Args) != 2 {
		return fmt.Errorf("expected PID")
	}
	pid, e := strconv.ParseUint(os.Args[1], 10, 32)
	if e != nil || pid == 0 {
		return fmt.Errorf("invalid PID")
	}
	identity, e := os.ReadFile(fmt.Sprintf("/proc/%d/stat", pid))
	if e != nil {
		return e
	}
	start := strings.Fields(string(identity)[strings.LastIndex(string(identity), ")")+1:])[19]
	kernel, e := btf.LoadKernelSpec()
	if e != nil {
		return e
	}
	var task *btf.Struct
	if e = kernel.TypeByName("task_struct", &task); e != nil {
		return e
	}
	var offset uint32
	for _, m := range task.Members {
		if m.Name == "blocked" {
			sig, ok := btf.UnderlyingType(m.Type).(*btf.Struct)
			if !ok {
				return fmt.Errorf("unsupported sigset ABI")
			}
			for _, n := range sig.Members {
				if n.Name == "sig" {
					offset = m.Offset.Bytes() + n.Offset.Bytes()
				}
			}
		}
	}
	if offset == 0 {
		return fmt.Errorf("SIGTRAP mask offset unavailable")
	}
	if e = rlimit.RemoveMemlock(); e != nil {
		return e
	}
	spec, e := ebpf.LoadCollectionSpecFromReader(bytes.NewReader(program))
	if e != nil {
		return e
	}
	c, e := ebpf.NewCollection(spec)
	if e != nil {
		return e
	}
	defer c.Close()
	reader, e := ringbuf.NewReader(c.Maps["events"])
	if e != nil {
		return e
	}
	defer reader.Close()
	h, e := link.AttachRawTracepoint(link.RawTracepointOptions{Name: "sys_exit", Program: c.Programs["io_exit"]})
	if e != nil {
		return e
	}
	defer h.Close()
	for _, pair := range [][2]string{{"tcp_sendmsg", "send_entry"}, {"tcp_recvmsg", "receive_entry"}} {
		h, e := link.Kprobe(pair[0], c.Programs[pair[1]], nil)
		if e != nil {
			return e
		}
		defer h.Close()
	}
	cfg := struct {
		PID, BlockedOffset uint32
		Deadline           uint64
	}{PID: uint32(pid), BlockedOffset: offset}
	key := uint32(0)
	renew := func() error {
		var ts unix.Timespec
		if e := unix.ClockGettime(unix.CLOCK_BOOTTIME, &ts); e != nil {
			return e
		}
		cfg.Deadline = uint64(ts.Nano()) + 3_000_000_000
		return c.Maps["control"].Put(key, &cfg)
	}
	if e = renew(); e != nil {
		return e
	}
	defer func() { cfg.PID = 0; c.Maps["control"].Put(key, &cfg) }()
	encoder := json.NewEncoder(os.Stdout)
	if e = encoder.Encode(map[string]any{"type": "ready", "pid": pid}); e != nil {
		return e
	}
	records := make(chan event, 256)
	done := make(chan struct{})
	defer close(done)
	go func() {
		for {
			record, e := reader.Read()
			if e != nil {
				return
			}
			var v event
			if binary.Read(bytes.NewReader(record.RawSample), binary.LittleEndian, &v) == nil {
				select {
				case records <- v:
				case <-done:
					return
				}
			}
		}
	}()
	commands := make(chan string, 256)
	go func() {
		defer close(commands)
		scan := bufio.NewScanner(os.Stdin)
		for scan.Scan() {
			select {
			case commands <- scan.Text():
			case <-done:
				return
			}
		}
	}()
	tick := time.NewTicker(time.Second)
	defer tick.Stop()
	last := time.Now()
	for {
		select {
		case command, ok := <-commands:
			if !ok {
				return nil
			}
			fields := strings.Fields(command)
			if len(fields) == 1 && fields[0] == "ping" {
				last = time.Now()
				if e = renew(); e != nil {
					return e
				}
			}
			if len(fields) == 3 && fields[0] == "ack" {
				tid, _ := strconv.ParseUint(fields[1], 10, 32)
				stamp, _ := strconv.ParseUint(fields[2], 10, 64)
				var pending uint64
				k := uint32(tid)
				if c.Maps["inflight"].Lookup(k, &pending) == nil && pending == stamp {
					c.Maps["inflight"].Delete(k)
				}
			}
		case v := <-records:
			if e = encoder.Encode(struct {
				event
				AtEpochMs int64
			}{v, time.Now().UnixMilli() + (int64(v.Exited)-bootTime())/1000000}); e != nil {
				return e
			}
		case <-tick.C:
			if time.Since(last) > 3*time.Second {
				return fmt.Errorf("controller disconnected")
			}
			current, e := os.ReadFile(fmt.Sprintf("/proc/%d/stat", pid))
			if e != nil {
				return fmt.Errorf("target exited")
			}
			fields := strings.Fields(string(current)[strings.LastIndex(string(current), ")")+1:])
			if len(fields) < 20 || fields[19] != start {
				return fmt.Errorf("target changed")
			}
			var lost uint64
			c.Maps["dropped"].Lookup(key, &lost)
			if e = encoder.Encode(map[string]any{"type": "stats", "dropped": lost}); e != nil {
				return e
			}
		}
	}
}

func bootTime() int64 {
	var ts unix.Timespec
	unix.ClockGettime(unix.CLOCK_BOOTTIME, &ts)
	return ts.Nano()
}

```

`go/internal/device/symbols/main_linux.go`:

```go
//go:build linux

// One-shot, explicitly requested resolver. No BPF hooks, process injection or ELF transfers.
package symbols

import (
	"bytes"
	"crypto/sha256"
	"encoding/binary"
	"encoding/hex"
	"encoding/json"
	"fmt"
	"github.com/DoranekoSystems/ebpf-analyzer/internal/symbols"
	"golang.org/x/sys/unix"
	"io"
	"os"
	"strconv"
	"strings"
	"time"
)

type request struct {
	PID           int                 `json:"pid"`
	BootID        string              `json:"bootId"`
	EventNS       string              `json:"eventNs"`
	Addresses     []string            `json:"addresses"`
	Disassemble   string              `json:"disassemble,omitempty"`
	ExpectedBytes string              `json:"expectedBytes,omitempty"`
	Stack         *symbols.SavedStack `json:"stack,omitempty"`
	MapsHash      string              `json:"mapsHash,omitempty"`
	CodeMapping   string              `json:"codeMapping,omitempty"`
	FileID        string              `json:"fileId,omitempty"`
	CodeOnly      bool                `json:"codeOnly,omitempty"`
	Before        bool                `json:"before,omitempty"`
}
type resolution struct {
	Address string `json:"address"`
	Module  string `json:"module"`
	Symbol  string `json:"symbol"`
	Status  string `json:"status"`
}
type module struct {
	Path      string           `json:"path"`
	Symbols   []symbols.Symbol `json:"symbols"`
	Truncated bool             `json:"truncated"`
}
type response struct {
	ResolvedAt  int64                `json:"resolvedAtEpochMs"`
	Scope       string               `json:"scope"`
	Results     []resolution         `json:"results"`
	Modules     []module             `json:"modules"`
	Error       string               `json:"error,omitempty"`
	Disassembly *symbols.Disassembly `json:"disassembly,omitempty"`
	StackTrace  *symbols.StackTrace  `json:"stackTrace,omitempty"`
	MapsHash    string               `json:"mapsHash"`
	CodeMapping string               `json:"codeMapping,omitempty"`
}

func Main() {
	time.AfterFunc(12*time.Second, func() { os.Exit(1) })
	result, err := run()
	if err != nil {
		result = response{Error: err.Error()}
	}
	json.NewEncoder(os.Stdout).Encode(result)
}
func read(path string, limit int) ([]byte, error) {
	f, e := os.Open(path)
	if e != nil {
		return nil, e
	}
	defer f.Close()
	b, e := io.ReadAll(io.LimitReader(f, int64(limit)+1))
	if len(b) > limit {
		return nil, fmt.Errorf("file exceeds limit")
	}
	return b, e
}
func start(pid int) (uint64, error) {
	b, e := read(fmt.Sprintf("/proc/%d/stat", pid), 65536)
	if e != nil {
		return 0, e
	}
	s := string(b)
	at := strings.LastIndex(s, ")")
	if at < 0 {
		return 0, fmt.Errorf("invalid process stat")
	}
	fields := strings.Fields(s[at+1:])
	if len(fields) < 20 {
		return 0, fmt.Errorf("short process stat")
	}
	return strconv.ParseUint(fields[19], 10, 64)
}
func run() (response, error) {
	out := response{Scope: "Current process maps (manual lookup); not event-time mappings", Results: []resolution{}, Modules: []module{}}
	var req request
	if err := json.NewDecoder(io.LimitReader(os.Stdin, 65536)).Decode(&req); err != nil {
		return out, err
	}
	if req.PID <= 0 || len(req.Addresses) > 128 {
		return out, fmt.Errorf("invalid PID/address count")
	}
	var disasmAddr uint64
	var expected []byte
	if req.Disassemble != "" {
		var err error
		disasmAddr, err = strconv.ParseUint(strings.TrimPrefix(strings.ToLower(req.Disassemble), "0x"), 16, 64)
		if err != nil {
			return out, err
		}
		expected, err = hex.DecodeString(req.ExpectedBytes)
		if err != nil || len(expected) > 15 {
			return out, fmt.Errorf("invalid captured opcode")
		}
		req.Addresses = append([]string{req.Disassemble}, req.Addresses...)
		out.Disassembly = &symbols.Disassembly{Address: req.Disassemble, Source: "ELF file", Stop: "No readable ELF mapping", Instructions: []symbols.Instruction{}}
	}
	boot, err := read("/proc/sys/kernel/random/boot_id", 128)
	if err != nil {
		return out, err
	}
	if strings.TrimSpace(string(boot)) != req.BootID {
		return out, fmt.Errorf("device boot differs from capture")
	}
	event, err := strconv.ParseUint(req.EventNS, 10, 64)
	if err != nil {
		return out, err
	}
	before, err := start(req.PID)
	if err != nil {
		return out, fmt.Errorf("process no longer available")
	}
	aux, err := read("/proc/self/auxv", 4096)
	if err != nil {
		return out, err
	}
	var hz uint64
	for i := 0; i+16 <= len(aux); i += 16 {
		if binary.LittleEndian.Uint64(aux[i:]) == 17 {
			hz = binary.LittleEndian.Uint64(aux[i+8:])
			break
		}
	}
	if hz == 0 || before/hz > event/1000000000 || (before/hz == event/1000000000 && (before%hz)*1000000000/hz > event%1000000000) {
		return out, fmt.Errorf("PID was reused or capture clock is incompatible")
	}
	mapsPath := fmt.Sprintf("/proc/%d/maps", req.PID)
	mapsData, err := read(mapsPath, 4<<20)
	if err != nil {
		return out, err
	}
	maps, err := symbols.Maps(string(mapsData))
	if err != nil {
		return out, err
	}
	out.MapsHash = fmt.Sprintf("%x", sha256.Sum256(mapsData))
	if req.MapsHash != "" && req.MapsHash != out.MapsHash {
		return out, fmt.Errorf("process mappings changed; resolve again before scrolling")
	}
	cache := map[string]*symbols.ELF{}
	fail := map[string]string{}
	remaining := 5000
	var opened []*os.File
	defer func() {
		for _, f := range opened {
			f.Close()
		}
	}()
	loadELF := func(m symbols.Mapping) (*symbols.ELF, error) {
		key := fmt.Sprintf("%s/%d/%s", m.Device, m.Inode, m.Path)
		obj, exists := cache[key]
		if !exists {
			if len(cache) >= 8 {
				return nil, fmt.Errorf("Module limit reached")
			}
			cache[key] = nil
			file, e := os.Open(fmt.Sprintf("/proc/%d/map_files/%x-%x", req.PID, m.Start, m.End))
			if e != nil && !strings.HasSuffix(m.Path, " (deleted)") {
				file, e = os.Open(fmt.Sprintf("/proc/%d/root%s", req.PID, m.Path))
			}
			if e != nil {
				fail[key] = "ELF unavailable"
			} else {
				var st unix.Stat_t
				e = unix.Fstat(int(file.Fd()), &st)
				device := fmt.Sprintf("%02x:%02x", unix.Major(uint64(st.Dev)), unix.Minor(uint64(st.Dev)))
				if e != nil || st.Ino != m.Inode || device != m.Device {
					fail[key] = "Mapped file identity changed"
				} else {
					obj, e = symbols.Read(file)
					if e != nil {
						fail[key] = "No supported ELF symbols"
						if strings.HasSuffix(m.Path, ".apk") {
							fail[key] = "APK-backed ELF unwinding is not supported yet"
						}
					} else {
						cache[key] = obj
						obj.FileID = fmt.Sprintf("%s/%d/%d/%d/%d", device, st.Ino, st.Size, st.Mtim.Sec, st.Mtim.Nsec)
						n := len(obj.Symbols)
						if n > remaining {
							n = remaining
						}
						if !req.CodeOnly {
							out.Modules = append(out.Modules, module{m.Path, obj.Symbols[:n], n < len(obj.Symbols)})
						}
						remaining -= n
					}
				}
				opened = append(opened, file)
			}
		}
		if obj == nil {
			return nil, fmt.Errorf("%s", fail[key])
		}
		return obj, nil
	}
	seen := map[uint64]bool{}
	for _, text := range req.Addresses {
		addr, e := strconv.ParseUint(strings.TrimPrefix(strings.ToLower(text), "0x"), 16, 64)
		if e != nil {
			return out, e
		}
		if seen[addr] {
			continue
		}
		seen[addr] = true
		r := resolution{Address: fmt.Sprintf("0x%016x", addr), Status: "Unmapped"}
		for _, m := range maps {
			if addr < m.Start || addr >= m.End {
				continue
			}
			r.Module = fmt.Sprintf("%s + file 0x%x", m.Path, m.Offset+addr-m.Start)
			r.Status = "Module only"
			if !strings.HasPrefix(m.Path, "/") || m.Inode == 0 {
				break
			}
			obj, err := loadELF(m)
			if err != nil {
				r.Status = err.Error()
				break
			}
			if req.Disassemble != "" && addr == disasmAddr {
				out.CodeMapping = mappingID(m)
				if req.CodeMapping != "" && req.CodeMapping != out.CodeMapping {
					return out, fmt.Errorf("code mapping changed; resolve again before scrolling")
				}
				if req.FileID != "" && req.FileID != obj.FileID {
					return out, fmt.Errorf("ELF changed; resolve again before scrolling")
				}
				d := obj.Disassemble(m, addr, expected)
				if req.Before {
					d = obj.DisassembleBefore(m, addr)
				} else if !req.CodeOnly && len(d.Instructions) > 0 {
					before := obj.DisassembleBefore(m, addr)
					d.Instructions = append(before.Instructions, d.Instructions...)
					d.Previous = before.Previous
					d.Boundary += "; earlier context: " + before.Stop
				}
				out.Disassembly = &d
			}
			value, ok := obj.Virtual(m, addr)
			if !ok {
				r.Status = "Outside ELF load segment"
				break
			}
			r.Module = fmt.Sprintf("%s + ELF 0x%x", m.Path, value)
			if sym := obj.Lookup(value); sym != nil {
				r.Symbol = fmt.Sprintf("%s+0x%x", sym.Name, value-sym.Value)
				r.Status = "Resolved"
			}
			break
		}
		out.Results = append(out.Results, r)
	}
	if req.Stack != nil {
		trace := symbols.Unwind(*req.Stack, func(pc uint64) (*symbols.ELF, symbols.Mapping, error) {
			for _, m := range maps {
				if pc >= m.Start && pc < m.End && strings.Contains(m.Perms, "x") && strings.HasPrefix(m.Path, "/") {
					e, err := loadELF(m)
					return e, m, err
				}
			}
			return nil, symbols.Mapping{}, fmt.Errorf("Caller has no executable ELF mapping")
		})
		out.StackTrace = &trace
	}
	after, err := start(req.PID)
	if err != nil || before != after {
		return out, fmt.Errorf("process changed during lookup")
	}
	finalMaps, err := read(mapsPath, 4<<20)
	if err != nil {
		return out, err
	}
	if req.CodeOnly && req.CodeMapping != "" {
		current, e := symbols.Maps(string(finalMaps))
		if e != nil {
			return out, e
		}
		match := false
		for _, m := range current {
			if disasmAddr >= m.Start && disasmAddr < m.End && mappingID(m) == req.CodeMapping {
				match = true
				break
			}
		}
		if !match || out.CodeMapping != req.CodeMapping {
			return out, fmt.Errorf("code mapping changed during disassembly")
		}
	} else if !bytes.Equal(mapsData, finalMaps) {
		return out, fmt.Errorf("process mappings changed; retry manual lookup")
	}
	out.ResolvedAt = time.Now().UnixMilli()
	return out, nil
}

func mappingID(m symbols.Mapping) string {
	return fmt.Sprintf("%x", sha256.Sum256([]byte(fmt.Sprintf("%#v", m))))
}

```

`go/internal/monitor/model.go`:

```go
// Package monitor contains the passive capture format, separate from proxy history.
package monitor

import (
	"encoding/binary"
	"encoding/json"
	"fmt"
	"github.com/DoranekoSystems/ebpf-analyzer/internal/attribution"
	"github.com/DoranekoSystems/ebpf-analyzer/internal/origin"
	"net/netip"
	"os"
	"path/filepath"
	"sort"
	"strconv"
	"time"
)

type Packet struct {
	InterfaceIndex int    `json:"interfaceIndex"`
	At             int64  `json:"atEpochMs"`
	Source         string `json:"source"`
	Destination    string `json:"destination"`
	Protocol       string `json:"protocol"`
	Data           []byte `json:"data"`
	OriginalLength int    `json:"originalLength"`
	ConnectionID   string `json:"connectionId,omitempty"`
}

// Decode only assigns ports for unfragmented IPv4/IPv6 with a direct TCP/UDP header.
// Unsupported extension headers and fragments remain unattributed raw packets.
func Decode(data []byte, original int, at int64) (Packet, bool) {
	p := Packet{At: at, OriginalLength: original, Data: append([]byte(nil), data...), Protocol: "IP"}
	var src, dst netip.Addr
	var proto byte
	var offset int
	fragmented := false
	if len(data) >= 20 && data[0]>>4 == 4 {
		offset = int(data[0]&15) * 4
		if offset < 20 || offset > len(data) {
			return p, false
		}
		src = netip.AddrFrom4([4]byte(data[12:16]))
		dst = netip.AddrFrom4([4]byte(data[16:20]))
		proto = data[9]
		fragmented = binary.BigEndian.Uint16(data[6:8])&0x3fff != 0
	} else if len(data) >= 40 && data[0]>>4 == 6 {
		offset = 40
		proto = data[6]
		src = netip.AddrFrom16([16]byte(data[8:24]))
		dst = netip.AddrFrom16([16]byte(data[24:40]))
	} else {
		return p, false
	}
	p.Source, p.Destination = src.String(), dst.String()
	if !fragmented && (proto == 6 || proto == 17) && len(data) >= offset+8 {
		p.Protocol = "TCP"
		if proto == 17 {
			p.Protocol = "UDP"
		}
		p.Source = netip.AddrPortFrom(src, binary.BigEndian.Uint16(data[offset:offset+2])).String()
		p.Destination = netip.AddrPortFrom(dst, binary.BigEndian.Uint16(data[offset+2:offset+4])).String()
	}
	return p, true
}

type Snapshot struct {
	SchemaVersion      int                       `json:"schemaVersion"`
	Mode               string                    `json:"mode"`
	UpdatedAt          int64                     `json:"updatedAtEpochMs"`
	Running            bool                      `json:"running"`
	Drops              uint64                    `json:"drops"`
	CopyDrops          uint64                    `json:"copyDrops"`
	EvictedConnections uint64                    `json:"evictedConnections"`
	Connections        []attribution.Observation `json:"connections"`
	Packets            []Packet                  `json:"packets"`
	Origins            []origin.Snapshot         `json:"origins"`
	OriginDrops        uint64                    `json:"originDrops"`
	EvictedOrigins     uint64                    `json:"evictedOrigins"`
	IOEvents           []origin.Snapshot         `json:"ioEvents"`
	EvictedIOEvents    uint64                    `json:"evictedIoEvents"`
}

type State struct {
	Snapshot
	flows map[string]attribution.Observation
}

func NewState() *State {
	return &State{Snapshot: Snapshot{SchemaVersion: 1, Mode: "passive", Running: true, Connections: []attribution.Observation{}, Packets: []Packet{}, Origins: []origin.Snapshot{}, IOEvents: []origin.Snapshot{}}, flows: map[string]attribution.Observation{}}
}

// Origin snapshots arrive once, sometimes before a connection is established.
// Keep them independently of later connection updates; cap raw stack memory.
func (s *State) AddOrigins(incoming []origin.Snapshot) {
	for _, o := range incoming {
		if o.EventID != "" {
			found := false
			for i := range s.IOEvents {
				if s.IOEvents[i].EventID == o.EventID {
					s.IOEvents[i] = o
					found = true
					break
				}
			}
			if !found {
				s.IOEvents = append(s.IOEvents, o)
			}
			continue
		}
		found := false
		for i := range s.Origins {
			if s.Origins[i].ConnectionID == o.ConnectionID {
				s.Origins[i] = o
				found = true
				break
			}
		}
		if !found {
			s.Origins = append(s.Origins, o)
		}
	}
	sort.Slice(s.IOEvents, func(i, j int) bool {
		a, _ := strconv.ParseUint(s.IOEvents[i].CapturedAtBootNS, 10, 64)
		b, _ := strconv.ParseUint(s.IOEvents[j].CapturedAtBootNS, 10, 64)
		return a < b
	})
	if len(s.IOEvents) > 128 {
		s.EvictedIOEvents += uint64(len(s.IOEvents) - 128)
		s.IOEvents = append([]origin.Snapshot(nil), s.IOEvents[len(s.IOEvents)-128:]...)
	}
	if len(s.Origins) > 128 {
		s.EvictedOrigins += uint64(len(s.Origins) - 128)
		s.Origins = append([]origin.Snapshot(nil), s.Origins[len(s.Origins)-128:]...)
	}
}

func (s *State) Add(connections []attribution.Observation, packets []Packet) {
	for _, c := range connections {
		s.flows[c.ConnectionID] = c
	}
	s.Connections = s.Connections[:0]
	for _, c := range s.flows {
		s.Connections = append(s.Connections, c)
	}
	sort.Slice(s.Connections, func(i, j int) bool { return s.Connections[i].From > s.Connections[j].From })
	if len(s.Connections) > 2000 {
		for _, c := range s.Connections[2000:] {
			delete(s.flows, c.ConnectionID)
			s.EvictedConnections++
		}
		s.Connections = s.Connections[:2000]
	}
	s.Packets = append(s.Packets, packets...)
	if len(s.Packets) > 512 {
		s.Packets = append([]Packet(nil), s.Packets[len(s.Packets)-512:]...)
	}
	type endpoints struct{ from, to string }
	index := map[endpoints][]attribution.Observation{}
	for _, c := range s.Connections {
		a, err := netip.ParseAddr(c.ClientIP)
		if err != nil {
			continue
		}
		b, err := netip.ParseAddr(c.ServerIP)
		if err != nil {
			continue
		}
		from := netip.AddrPortFrom(a, uint16(c.ClientPort)).String()
		to := netip.AddrPortFrom(b, uint16(c.ServerPort)).String()
		index[endpoints{from, to}] = append(index[endpoints{from, to}], c)
		index[endpoints{to, from}] = append(index[endpoints{to, from}], c)
	}
	// Revisit copies when a late flow arrives, including previously matched copies:
	// another matching lifetime must revoke attribution, not leave a guessed PID.
	for i := range s.Packets {
		p := &s.Packets[i]
		p.ConnectionID = ""
		if p.Protocol != "TCP" {
			continue
		}
		match := ""
		for _, c := range index[endpoints{p.Source, p.Destination}] {
			if p.At < c.From || p.At >= c.To {
				continue
			}
			if match != "" {
				match = ""
				break
			}
			match = c.ConnectionID
		}
		p.ConnectionID = match
	}
}

func Write(path string, s Snapshot) error {
	data, err := json.Marshal(s)
	if err != nil {
		return err
	}
	if len(data) > 16*1024*1024 {
		return fmt.Errorf("monitor snapshot too large")
	}
	f, err := os.CreateTemp(filepath.Dir(path), ".monitor-*.json")
	if err != nil {
		return err
	}
	defer os.Remove(f.Name())
	if _, err = f.Write(data); err != nil {
		f.Close()
		return err
	}
	if err = f.Close(); err != nil {
		return err
	}
	// Windows readers (including Java) may briefly open without delete sharing.
	// Keep the previous valid snapshot visible and retry the atomic replacement.
	for attempt := 0; attempt < 40; attempt++ {
		err = os.Rename(f.Name(), path)
		if err == nil || !os.IsPermission(err) {
			return err
		}
		time.Sleep(25 * time.Millisecond)
	}
	return err
}

```

`go/internal/monitor/model_test.go`:

```go
package monitor

import (
	"encoding/binary"
	"fmt"
	"github.com/DoranekoSystems/ebpf-analyzer/internal/attribution"
	"github.com/DoranekoSystems/ebpf-analyzer/internal/origin"
	"testing"
)

func TestDecodeAndOwnership(t *testing.T) {
	data := make([]byte, 40)
	data[0] = 0x45
	data[9] = 6
	copy(data[12:16], []byte{10, 0, 2, 15})
	copy(data[16:20], []byte{1, 2, 3, 4})
	binary.BigEndian.PutUint16(data[20:22], 12345)
	binary.BigEndian.PutUint16(data[22:24], 443)
	p, ok := Decode(data, 100, 150)
	if !ok || p.Source != "10.0.2.15:12345" || p.Destination != "1.2.3.4:443" || p.OriginalLength != 100 {
		t.Fatalf("decode: %+v", p)
	}
	c := attribution.Observation{ConnectionID: "a", ClientIP: "10.0.2.15", ClientPort: 12345, ServerIP: "1.2.3.4", ServerPort: 443, From: 100, To: 200, PID: 12}
	s := NewState()
	s.Add(nil, []Packet{p})
	if s.Packets[0].ConnectionID != "" {
		t.Fatal("invented owner")
	}
	s.Add([]attribution.Observation{c}, nil)
	if s.Packets[0].ConnectionID != "a" {
		t.Fatal("late owner missing")
	}
	other := c
	other.ConnectionID = "late-overlap"
	s.Add([]attribution.Observation{other}, nil)
	if s.Packets[0].ConnectionID != "" {
		t.Fatal("late ambiguity did not revoke attribution")
	}
	p.At = 200
	s.Add(nil, []Packet{p})
	if s.Packets[1].ConnectionID != "" {
		t.Fatal("end boundary reused")
	}
	c.ConnectionID = "b"
	s = NewState()
	a := c
	a.ConnectionID = "a"
	p.At = 150
	s.Add([]attribution.Observation{a, c}, []Packet{p})
	if s.Packets[0].ConnectionID != "" {
		t.Fatal("ambiguous owner guessed")
	}
	data[6] = 0x20
	p, ok = Decode(data, 40, 150)
	if !ok || p.Protocol != "IP" {
		t.Fatal("fragment assigned transport ports")
	}
}

func TestBoundedSnapshot(t *testing.T) {
	s := NewState()
	s.Add(nil, make([]Packet, 600))
	if len(s.Packets) != 512 {
		t.Fatal("packet history unbounded")
	}
	if err := Write(t.TempDir()+"/monitor.json", s.Snapshot); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
}

func TestOriginsPersistAcrossFlowUpdatesAndStayBounded(t *testing.T) {
	s := NewState()
	s.AddOrigins([]origin.Snapshot{{ConnectionID: "known", Stack: []byte{1, 2, 3}}})
	s.Add([]attribution.Observation{{ConnectionID: "known"}}, nil)
	s.AddOrigins(nil)
	s.Add([]attribution.Observation{{ConnectionID: "known", Closed: true}}, nil)
	if len(s.Origins) != 1 || len(s.Origins[0].Stack) != 3 {
		t.Fatal("one-shot stack lost on update")
	}
	for i := 0; i < 128; i++ {
		s.AddOrigins([]origin.Snapshot{{ConnectionID: fmt.Sprint(i)}})
	}
	if len(s.Origins) != 128 || s.EvictedOrigins != 1 {
		t.Fatal("stack retention is not bounded")
	}
}

func TestIOEventsDoNotOverwriteConnectionOrOtherThreads(t *testing.T) {
	s := NewState()
	s.AddOrigins([]origin.Snapshot{{ConnectionID: "flow", TID: 1}})
	for i := 1; i <= 130; i++ {
		s.AddOrigins([]origin.Snapshot{{ConnectionID: "flow", EventID: fmt.Sprint(i), CapturedAtBootNS: fmt.Sprint(i), TID: uint32(i + 10), Stack: []byte{byte(i)}}})
	}
	if len(s.IOEvents) != 128 || s.EvictedIOEvents != 2 || len(s.Origins) != 1 || s.Origins[0].TID != 1 {
		t.Fatal("independent retention failed")
	}
	if s.IOEvents[0].EventID != "3" || s.IOEvents[0].TID != 13 || s.IOEvents[127].Stack[0] != 130 {
		t.Fatal("event identity/context lost")
	}
	s.AddOrigins([]origin.Snapshot{s.IOEvents[0]})
	if len(s.IOEvents) != 128 || s.EvictedIOEvents != 2 {
		t.Fatal("duplicate event counted twice")
	}
}

```

`go/internal/origin/origin.go`:

```go
// Package origin decodes bounded snapshots copied by BPF at TCP_SYN_SENT.
// No target-process memory is read from userspace.
package origin

import (
	"encoding/hex"
	"fmt"
)

const StackBytes = 4096

// Raw is the x86_64 BPF map value (232 byte header + 4 KiB stack).
type Raw struct {
	Start                                   uint64
	PID, TID                                uint32
	RegsError, StackError, InstructionError int32
	Status, Captured, Kind                  uint32
	Registers                               [21]uint64
	Instruction                             [2]byte
	Padding                                 [6]byte
	ConnectionStart, IORequested            uint64
	Stack                                   [StackBytes]byte
}

type Snapshot struct {
	EventID          string            `json:"eventId,omitempty"`
	Operation        string            `json:"operation,omitempty"`
	Phase            string            `json:"phase,omitempty"`
	RequestedIOBytes uint64            `json:"requestedIoBytes,omitempty"`
	ConnectionID     string            `json:"connectionId"`
	PID              uint32            `json:"pid"`
	TID              uint32            `json:"tid"`
	Architecture     string            `json:"architecture"`
	Source           string            `json:"source"`
	CapturedAtBootNS string            `json:"capturedAtBootNs"`
	Status           string            `json:"status"`
	Registers        map[string]string `json:"registers"`
	RegisterError    int32             `json:"registerError"`
	InstructionError int32             `json:"instructionError"`
	InstructionHex   string            `json:"instructionHex"`
	SyscallPC        string            `json:"syscallPc,omitempty"`
	StackBase        string            `json:"stackBase"`
	RequestedBytes   int               `json:"requestedBytes"`
	CapturedBytes    uint32            `json:"capturedBytes"`
	FailureOffset    int               `json:"failureOffset"`
	StackError       int32             `json:"stackError"`
	Stack            []byte            `json:"stack"`
}

var names = [...]string{"r15", "r14", "r13", "r12", "rbp", "rbx", "r11", "r10", "r9", "r8", "rax_saved", "rcx", "rdx", "rsi", "rdi", "orig_rax", "rip", "cs", "rflags", "rsp", "ss"}
var statuses = map[uint32]string{1: "captured-limit", 2: "partial-read", 3: "unsupported-user-frame", 4: "unsupported-syscall", 5: "register-read-failed", 6: "unverified-syscall-instruction", 7: "stack-read-failed"}

func syscallAllowed(kind uint32, nr uint64) bool {
	switch kind {
	case 0:
		return nr == 42
	case 1:
		return nr == 1 || nr == 20 || nr == 44 || nr == 46 || nr == 307
	case 2:
		return nr == 0 || nr == 19 || nr == 45 || nr == 47 || nr == 299
	}
	return false
}

func Decode(raw Raw, runID string) (Snapshot, error) {
	status, ok := statuses[raw.Status]
	if !ok || raw.Kind > 2 || raw.ConnectionStart == 0 || raw.Captured > StackBytes || raw.Captured%256 != 0 {
		return Snapshot{}, fmt.Errorf("invalid BPF origin header")
	}
	if raw.Status == 1 && raw.Captured != StackBytes {
		return Snapshot{}, fmt.Errorf("incomplete full stack")
	}
	if raw.Status >= 3 && raw.Captured != 0 {
		return Snapshot{}, fmt.Errorf("invalid unsupported stack payload")
	}
	s := Snapshot{ConnectionID: fmt.Sprintf("%s/%d", runID, raw.Start), PID: raw.PID, TID: raw.TID, Architecture: "x86_64", Source: "sock:inet_sock_set_state/TCP_SYN_SENT/current-task", CapturedAtBootNS: fmt.Sprint(raw.Start), Status: status, Registers: map[string]string{}, RegisterError: raw.RegsError, InstructionError: raw.InstructionError, StackBase: fmt.Sprintf("0x%016x", raw.Registers[19]), RequestedBytes: StackBytes, CapturedBytes: raw.Captured, FailureOffset: -1, StackError: raw.StackError, Stack: append([]byte{}, raw.Stack[:raw.Captured]...)}
	s.ConnectionID = fmt.Sprintf("%s/%d", runID, raw.ConnectionStart)
	if raw.Kind != 0 {
		s.EventID = fmt.Sprintf("%s/io/%d", runID, raw.Start)
		s.Phase = "entry"
		s.RequestedIOBytes = raw.IORequested
		if raw.Kind == 1 {
			s.Operation = "send"
			s.Source = "kprobe/tcp_sendmsg"
		} else {
			s.Operation = "receive"
			s.Source = "kprobe/tcp_recvmsg"
		}
	}
	if raw.RegsError == 0 {
		for i, name := range names {
			s.Registers[name] = fmt.Sprintf("0x%016x", raw.Registers[i])
		}
	}
	if raw.Status == 1 || raw.Status == 2 || raw.Status == 7 {
		if !syscallAllowed(raw.Kind, raw.Registers[15]) || raw.Registers[17] != 0x33 || raw.Registers[16] < 2 || raw.Instruction != [2]byte{0x0f, 0x05} || raw.InstructionError != 0 || raw.RegsError != 0 {
			return Snapshot{}, fmt.Errorf("invalid verified syscall frame")
		}
		s.SyscallPC = fmt.Sprintf("0x%016x", raw.Registers[16]-2)
		s.InstructionHex = hex.EncodeToString(raw.Instruction[:])
	}
	if raw.StackError != 0 {
		s.FailureOffset = int(raw.Captured)
	}
	return s, nil
}

```

```go
package origin

import (
	"encoding/binary"
	"testing"
)

func fixture() Raw {
	r := Raw{Start: 123, ConnectionStart: 123, PID: 12, TID: 13, Status: 2, Captured: 256, StackError: -14, Instruction: [2]byte{0x0f, 0x05}}
	r.Registers[15] = 42
	r.Registers[16] = 0x1002
	r.Registers[17] = 0x33
	r.Registers[19] = 0x2000
	for i := range r.Stack {
		r.Stack[i] = 0xab
	}
	return r
}
func TestDecodeOnlyValidPrefixAndExactAddresses(t *testing.T) {
	r := fixture()
	if binary.Size(r) != 4328 {
		t.Fatal("BPF ABI size mismatch")
	}
	s, err := Decode(r, "boot/run")
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if len(s.Stack) != 256 || s.FailureOffset != 256 || s.StackError != -14 || s.SyscallPC != "0x0000000000001000" || s.StackBase != "0x0000000000002000" || s.ConnectionID != "boot/run/123" {
		t.Fatalf("invalid snapshot metadata: %+v", s)
	}
	r.Stack[0] = 0
	if s.Stack[0] != 0xab {
		t.Fatal("stack aliases reused BPF buffer")
	}
}

func TestIOEventsKeepTheirOwnIdentityAndThread(t *testing.T) {
	r := fixture()
	r.Kind = 1
	r.Start = 456
	r.TID = 99
	r.IORequested = 128
	r.Registers[15] = 1
	s, err := Decode(r, "boot/run")
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if s.EventID != "boot/run/io/456" || s.ConnectionID != "boot/run/123" || s.TID != 99 || s.Operation != "send" || s.RequestedIOBytes != 128 || s.Phase != "entry" {
		t.Fatal("I/O identity replaced by connection metadata")
	}
	r.Kind = 2
	if _, err := Decode(r, "boot/run"); err == nil {
		t.Fatal("write syscall accepted as receive")
	}
	r.Registers[15] = 0
	s, err = Decode(r, "boot/run")
	if err != nil || s.Operation != "receive" {
		t.Fatal("read syscall rejected")
	}
}
func TestRejectInvalidCapture(t *testing.T) {
	for _, change := range []func(*Raw){func(r *Raw) { r.Captured = StackBytes + 256 }, func(r *Raw) { r.Captured = 1 }, func(r *Raw) { r.Status = 1 }, func(r *Raw) { r.Instruction[0] = 0 }, func(r *Raw) { r.Registers[15] = 1 }, func(r *Raw) { r.Status = 3 }} {
		r := fixture()
		change(&r)
		if _, err := Decode(r, "run"); err == nil {
			t.Fatal("invalid snapshot accepted")
		}
	}
}
func TestFailedRegistersExportNoScratchMemory(t *testing.T) {
	r := fixture()
	r.Status = 5
	r.Captured = 0
	r.RegsError = -14
	r.StackError = 0
	s, err := Decode(r, "run")
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if len(s.Stack) != 0 || len(s.Registers) != 0 || s.SyscallPC != "" {
		t.Fatal("invalid registers or stale memory exported")
	}
}
```

`go/internal/symbols/disasm.go`:

```go
package symbols

import (
	"bytes"
	"debug/elf"
	"encoding/hex"
	"fmt"
	"golang.org/x/arch/x86/x86asm"
	"strconv"
	"strings"
)

type Instruction struct {
	Address string `json:"address"`
	Bytes   string `json:"bytes"`
	Text    string `json:"text"`
	Symbol  string `json:"symbol"`
}
type Disassembly struct {
	Address      string        `json:"address"`
	Source       string        `json:"source"`
	Boundary     string        `json:"boundary"`
	Stop         string        `json:"stop"`
	Next         string        `json:"next,omitempty"`
	Previous     string        `json:"previous,omitempty"`
	FileID       string        `json:"fileId"`
	Instructions []Instruction `json:"instructions"`
}

// DisassembleBefore decodes forward from a known symbol boundary. x86 instructions
// cannot safely be decoded by subtracting an arbitrary byte count from the PC.
func (e *ELF) DisassembleBefore(m Mapping, addr uint64) Disassembly {
	out := Disassembly{Address: fmt.Sprintf("0x%016x", addr), Source: "ELF file: " + m.Path, FileID: e.FileID,
		Boundary: "Decoded from ELF symbol start", Stop: "No earlier verified symbol boundary", Instructions: []Instruction{}}
	value, ok := e.Virtual(m, addr)
	if !ok {
		return out
	}
	symbol := e.Lookup(value)
	if symbol == nil || value <= symbol.Value || value-symbol.Value > 65536 || value-symbol.Value > addr-m.Start {
		return out
	}
	start := addr - (value - symbol.Value)
	at := start
	var lines []Instruction
	for at < addr {
		page := e.Disassemble(m, at, nil)
		if len(page.Instructions) == 0 {
			return out
		}
		for _, line := range page.Instructions {
			pc, err := strconv.ParseUint(strings.TrimPrefix(line.Address, "0x"), 16, 64)
			if err != nil || pc != at {
				return out
			}
			end := pc + uint64(len(line.Bytes)/2)
			if end > addr {
				return out
			}
			lines = append(lines, line)
			if len(lines) > 64 {
				lines = lines[1:]
			}
			at = end
			if at == addr {
				out.Instructions = lines
				out.Stop = "Start of symbol"
				if lines[0].Address != fmt.Sprintf("0x%016x", start) {
					out.Previous = lines[0].Address
					out.Stop = "Scroll up for earlier instructions"
				}
				return out
			}
		}
	}
	return out
}

func (e *ELF) Disassemble(m Mapping, addr uint64, expected []byte) Disassembly {
	out := Disassembly{Address: fmt.Sprintf("0x%016x", addr), Source: "ELF file: " + m.Path, Boundary: "Selected address; instruction boundary not independently verified", Instructions: []Instruction{}}
	out.FileID = e.FileID
	if e.Machine != elf.EM_X86_64 || e.Class != elf.ELFCLASS64 {
		out.Stop = "Only x86_64 ELF is supported"
		return out
	}
	value, ok := e.Virtual(m, addr)
	if !ok || !strings.Contains(m.Perms, "x") {
		out.Stop = "Not in an executable file-backed mapping"
		return out
	}
	fileOffset := m.Offset + addr - m.Start
	limit := uint64(271) // One maximum-length instruction of lookahead across a page boundary.
	if m.End-addr < limit {
		limit = m.End - addr
	}
	found := false
	for _, p := range e.Programs {
		if p.Type == elf.PT_LOAD && p.Flags&elf.PF_X != 0 && fileOffset >= p.Off && fileOffset-p.Off < p.Filesz && p.Vaddr+(fileOffset-p.Off) == value {
			if p.Filesz-(fileOffset-p.Off) < limit {
				limit = p.Filesz - (fileOffset - p.Off)
			}
			found = true
			break
		}
	}
	if !found {
		out.Stop = "Not in an executable ELF load segment"
		return out
	}
	if fileOffset > 1<<63-1 || e.Reader == nil {
		out.Stop = "Invalid file offset"
		return out
	}
	code := make([]byte, int(limit))
	n, err := e.Reader.ReadAt(code, int64(fileOffset))
	code = code[:n]
	if len(expected) > 0 {
		if len(code) < len(expected) || !bytes.Equal(code[:len(expected)], expected) {
			out.Stop = "ELF bytes differ from the captured instruction"
			return out
		}
		out.Boundary = "Start bytes match captured syscall opcode; remaining bytes are from ELF"
	}
	out.Stop = "End of file-backed range"
	if limit == 271 {
		out.Stop = "Scroll for more"
	}
	if err != nil {
		out.Stop = "File read boundary"
	}
	for at := 0; at < len(code); {
		if len(out.Instructions) >= 64 || at >= 256 {
			out.Stop = "Scroll for more"
			out.Next = fmt.Sprintf("0x%016x", addr+uint64(at))
			break
		}
		inst, e2 := x86asm.Decode(code[at:], 64)
		if e2 != nil || inst.Op == 0 || inst.Len <= 0 || inst.Len > len(code)-at {
			out.Stop = fmt.Sprintf("Cannot decode instruction at 0x%016x", addr+uint64(at))
			break
		}
		symbol := ""
		if s := e.Lookup(value + uint64(at)); s != nil {
			symbol = fmt.Sprintf("%s+0x%x", s.Name, value+uint64(at)-s.Value)
		}
		out.Instructions = append(out.Instructions, Instruction{fmt.Sprintf("0x%016x", addr+uint64(at)), hex.EncodeToString(code[at : at+inst.Len]), x86asm.IntelSyntax(inst, addr+uint64(at), nil), symbol})
		at += inst.Len
	}
	return out
}

```

`go/internal/symbols/disasm_test.go`:

```go
package symbols

import (
	"bytes"
	"debug/elf"
	"strings"
	"testing"
)

func fixture(code []byte) (*ELF, Mapping) {
	raw := append(make([]byte, 32), code...)
	return &ELF{Reader: bytes.NewReader(raw), Machine: elf.EM_X86_64, Class: elf.ELFCLASS64, Programs: []*elf.Prog{{ProgHeader: elf.ProgHeader{Type: elf.PT_LOAD, Flags: elf.PF_X, Off: 32, Vaddr: 0x1000, Filesz: uint64(len(code))}}}, Symbols: []Symbol{{Name: "syscall_wrapper", Value: 0x1000, Size: 6}}}, Mapping{Start: 0x7000, End: 0x8000, Offset: 32, Perms: "r-xp", Path: "/lib/test.so"}
}
func TestForwardDisassembly(t *testing.T) {
	e, m := fixture([]byte{0x0f, 0x05, 0x48, 0x89, 0xc7, 0xc3, 0x0f})
	d := e.Disassemble(m, m.Start, []byte{0x0f, 0x05})
	if len(d.Instructions) != 3 || d.Instructions[0].Bytes != "0f05" || d.Instructions[0].Text != "syscall" || d.Instructions[1].Address != "0x0000000000007002" || d.Instructions[2].Symbol != "syscall_wrapper+0x5" || !strings.Contains(d.Stop, "7006") {
		t.Fatalf("unexpected decode: %+v", d)
	}
	if d = e.Disassemble(m, m.Start, []byte{0xcc}); len(d.Instructions) != 0 || !strings.Contains(d.Stop, "differ") {
		t.Fatal("accepted changed anchor")
	}
	m.Perms = "r--p"
	if d = e.Disassemble(m, m.Start, nil); len(d.Instructions) != 0 {
		t.Fatal("decoded non executable mapping")
	}
}
func TestDisassemblyLimits(t *testing.T) {
	e, m := fixture(bytes.Repeat([]byte{0x90}, 300))
	d := e.Disassemble(m, m.Start, nil)
	if len(d.Instructions) != 64 || d.Next != "0x0000000000007040" {
		t.Fatal("instruction limit")
	}
	m.End = m.Start + 1
	d = e.Disassemble(m, m.Start, nil)
	if len(d.Instructions) != 1 {
		t.Fatal("read across mapping boundary")
	}
	e.Machine = elf.EM_AARCH64
	if len(e.Disassemble(m, m.Start, nil).Instructions) != 0 {
		t.Fatal("decoded wrong ISA")
	}
}

func TestContinuationKeepsInstructionBoundary(t *testing.T) {
	// Five-byte MOV instructions straddle the nominal byte boundary.
	code := bytes.Repeat([]byte{0xb8, 1, 0, 0, 0}, 100)
	e, m := fixture(code)
	first := e.Disassemble(m, m.Start, nil)
	if len(first.Instructions) != 52 || first.Next != "0x0000000000007104" {
		t.Fatalf("bad first page: %d %s", len(first.Instructions), first.Next)
	}
	second := e.Disassemble(m, m.Start+260, nil)
	if second.Instructions[0].Address != first.Next || second.Instructions[0].Bytes != "b801000000" || len(second.Instructions) != 48 || second.Next != "" {
		t.Fatal("continuation lost instruction boundary")
	}
}

func TestEarlierDisassemblyUsesSymbolBoundary(t *testing.T) {
	code := bytes.Repeat([]byte{0xb8, 1, 0, 0, 0}, 150)
	e, m := fixture(code)
	e.Symbols[0].Size = uint64(len(code))
	d := e.DisassembleBefore(m, m.Start+700)
	if len(d.Instructions) != 64 || d.Previous != "0x000000000000717c" || d.Instructions[63].Address != "0x00000000000072b7" {
		t.Fatalf("bad earlier page: %+v", d)
	}
	d = e.DisassembleBefore(m, m.Start+380)
	if len(d.Instructions) != 64 || d.Previous != "0x000000000000703c" {
		t.Fatal("bad previous page")
	}
	if len(e.DisassembleBefore(m, m.Start+699).Instructions) != 0 {
		t.Fatal("accepted middle of instruction")
	}
	e.Symbols = nil
	if len(e.DisassembleBefore(m, m.Start+700).Instructions) != 0 {
		t.Fatal("guessed instruction boundary without symbol")
	}
}
```

`go/internal/symbols/elf.go`:

```go
// Package symbols resolves only defined ELF symbols whose ranges contain an address.
package symbols

import (
	"bufio"
	"debug/elf"
	"encoding/binary"
	"fmt"
	"github.com/go-delve/delve/pkg/dwarf/frame"
	"io"
	"strconv"
	"strings"
)

type Mapping struct {
	Start, End, Offset, Inode uint64
	Device, Perms, Path       string
}

func Maps(text string) ([]Mapping, error) {
	var result []Mapping
	scan := bufio.NewScanner(strings.NewReader(text))
	scan.Buffer(make([]byte, 4096), 1024*1024)
	for scan.Scan() {
		fields := strings.Fields(scan.Text())
		if len(fields) < 5 {
			return nil, fmt.Errorf("invalid maps record")
		}
		span := strings.Split(fields[0], "-")
		if len(span) != 2 {
			return nil, fmt.Errorf("invalid map range")
		}
		start, e1 := strconv.ParseUint(span[0], 16, 64)
		end, e2 := strconv.ParseUint(span[1], 16, 64)
		off, e3 := strconv.ParseUint(fields[2], 16, 64)
		ino, e4 := strconv.ParseUint(fields[4], 10, 64)
		if e1 != nil || e2 != nil || e3 != nil || e4 != nil || end <= start {
			return nil, fmt.Errorf("invalid map values")
		}
		// Split only the first five fields; preserve spaces inside paths.
		rest := scan.Text()
		for i := 0; i < 5; i++ {
			rest = strings.TrimLeft(rest, " \t")
			at := strings.IndexAny(rest, " \t")
			if at < 0 {
				rest = ""
				break
			}
			rest = rest[at:]
		}
		result = append(result, Mapping{start, end, off, ino, fields[3], fields[1], strings.TrimLeft(rest, " \t")})
	}
	return result, scan.Err()
}

type Symbol struct {
	Name    string `json:"name"`
	Value   uint64 `json:"-"`
	Address string `json:"address"`
	Size    uint64 `json:"size"`
}
type ELF struct {
	Programs     []*elf.Prog
	Symbols      []Symbol
	Reader       io.ReaderAt
	Machine      elf.Machine
	Class        elf.Class
	cfi          *elf.Section
	order        binary.ByteOrder
	frames       frame.FrameDescriptionEntries
	frameError   error
	framesLoaded bool
	FileID       string
}

func Read(reader io.ReaderAt) (*ELF, error) {
	f, err := elf.NewFile(reader)
	if err != nil {
		return nil, err
	}
	defer f.Close()
	if f.Type != elf.ET_DYN && f.Type != elf.ET_EXEC {
		return nil, fmt.Errorf("unsupported ELF type")
	}
	var total uint64
	for _, s := range f.Sections {
		if s.Type == elf.SHT_SYMTAB || s.Type == elf.SHT_DYNSYM || s.Type == elf.SHT_STRTAB {
			total += s.Size
			if s.Size > 32<<20 || total > 64<<20 {
				return nil, fmt.Errorf("symbol tables exceed limit")
			}
		}
	}
	result := &ELF{Programs: f.Progs, Reader: reader, Machine: f.Machine, Class: f.Class}
	result.cfi = f.Section(".eh_frame")
	result.order = f.ByteOrder
	seen := map[string]bool{}
	for _, read := range []func() ([]elf.Symbol, error){f.Symbols, f.DynamicSymbols} {
		list, err := read()
		if err == elf.ErrNoSymbols {
			continue
		}
		if err != nil {
			return nil, err
		}
		if len(list) > 500000 {
			return nil, fmt.Errorf("too many ELF symbols")
		}
		for _, s := range list {
			t := elf.ST_TYPE(s.Info)
			if s.Section == elf.SHN_UNDEF || s.Section >= elf.SHN_LORESERVE || s.Name == "" || (t != elf.STT_FUNC && t != elf.STT_OBJECT && t != elf.STT_NOTYPE) {
				continue
			}
			key := fmt.Sprintf("%x/%s", s.Value, s.Name)
			if seen[key] {
				continue
			}
			seen[key] = true
			name := s.Name
			if len(name) > 512 {
				name = name[:512]
			}
			result.Symbols = append(result.Symbols, Symbol{name, s.Value, fmt.Sprintf("0x%016x", s.Value), s.Size})
		}
	}
	return result, nil
}

// Translate the mapping's file offset through PT_LOAD, not address-mapStart.
func (e *ELF) Virtual(m Mapping, address uint64) (uint64, bool) {
	if address < m.Start || address >= m.End {
		return 0, false
	}
	delta := address - m.Start
	if m.Offset > ^uint64(0)-delta {
		return 0, false
	}
	fileOffset := m.Offset + delta
	for _, p := range e.Programs {
		if p.Type == elf.PT_LOAD && fileOffset >= p.Off && fileOffset-p.Off < p.Filesz {
			return p.Vaddr + (fileOffset - p.Off), true
		}
	}
	return 0, false
}
func (e *ELF) Lookup(value uint64) *Symbol {
	var best *Symbol
	for i := range e.Symbols {
		s := &e.Symbols[i]
		if value < s.Value || !(value == s.Value || (s.Size > 0 && value-s.Value < s.Size)) {
			continue
		}
		if best == nil || s.Value > best.Value {
			best = s
		}
	}
	return best
}

```

`go/internal/symbols/elf_test.go`:

```go
package symbols

import (
	"bytes"
	"debug/elf"
	"os"
	"testing"
)

func TestMapsAndLoadOffset(t *testing.T) {
	list, err := Maps("7f001000-7f002000 r-xp 00002000 08:01 123 /path/with spaces/lib.so\n")
	if err != nil || len(list) != 1 || list[0].Path != "/path/with spaces/lib.so" {
		t.Fatalf("maps: %v %v", list, err)
	}
	e := ELF{Programs: []*elf.Prog{{ProgHeader: elf.ProgHeader{Type: elf.PT_LOAD, Off: 0x2000, Vaddr: 0x5000, Filesz: 0x1000}}}, Symbols: []Symbol{{Name: "function", Value: 0x5010, Size: 16}, {Name: "zero", Value: 0x5030}}}
	value, ok := e.Virtual(list[0], 0x7f001018)
	if !ok || value != 0x5018 || e.Lookup(value).Name != "function" {
		t.Fatal("load bias translation failed")
	}
	if e.Lookup(0x5020) != nil || e.Lookup(0x5031) != nil || e.Lookup(0x5030) == nil {
		t.Fatal("guessed symbol outside its range")
	}
	if _, ok := e.Virtual(list[0], list[0].End); ok {
		t.Fatal("matched end boundary")
	}
	if _, err := Maps("bad record"); err == nil {
		t.Fatal("accepted invalid maps")
	}
	if _, err := Read(bytes.NewReader([]byte("not ELF"))); err == nil {
		t.Fatal("accepted non ELF")
	}
}
func TestBuiltProbeELF(t *testing.T) {
	f, err := os.Open("../../bin/stackprobe-amd64")
	if err != nil {
		t.Skip("build stackprobe first")
	}
	defer f.Close()
	e, err := Read(f)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	for _, s := range e.Symbols {
		if s.Name == "main.stackConnect.abi0" {
			if e.Lookup(s.Value) == nil {
				t.Fatal("defined function unresolved")
			}
			return
		}
	}
	t.Fatal("known assembly symbol missing")
}
```

`go/internal/symbols/unwind.go`:

```go
package symbols

import (
	"debug/elf"
	"encoding/binary"
	"fmt"
	"github.com/go-delve/delve/pkg/dwarf/frame"
	"strconv"
	"strings"
)

type StackFrame struct {
	PC     string `json:"pc"`
	SP     string `json:"sp"`
	FP     string `json:"fp"`
	Symbol string `json:"symbol"`
	Module string `json:"module"`
}
type StackTrace struct {
	Frames []StackFrame `json:"frames"`
	Stop   string       `json:"stop"`
}
type SavedStack struct {
	Base      string            `json:"base"`
	Data      []byte            `json:"data"`
	Registers map[string]string `json:"registers"`
	PC        string            `json:"pc"`
}

func (e *ELF) frameAt(pc uint64) (ctx *frame.FrameContext, err error) {
	defer func() {
		if recover() != nil {
			ctx = nil
			err = fmt.Errorf("unsupported or malformed CFI")
		}
	}()
	if !e.framesLoaded {
		e.framesLoaded = true
		if e.cfi == nil {
			e.frameError = fmt.Errorf("ELF has no .eh_frame")
		} else if e.cfi.Size > 8<<20 {
			e.frameError = fmt.Errorf("CFI section exceeds limit")
		} else {
			var data []byte
			data, e.frameError = e.cfi.Data()
			if e.frameError == nil {
				e.frames, e.frameError = frame.Parse(data, e.order, 0, 8, e.cfi.Addr)
			}
		}
	}
	if e.frameError != nil {
		return nil, e.frameError
	}
	fde, err := e.frames.FDEForPC(pc)
	if err != nil {
		return nil, fmt.Errorf("no CFI for this address")
	}
	return fde.EstablishFrame(pc), nil
}
func unsigned(s string) (uint64, error) {
	return strconv.ParseUint(strings.TrimPrefix(strings.ToLower(s), "0x"), 16, 64)
}
func addOffset(v uint64, n int64) (uint64, bool) {
	if n >= 0 {
		r := v + uint64(n)
		return r, r >= v
	}
	m := uint64(-(n + 1)) + 1
	return v - m, v >= m
}

// Only saved event memory is used. Never read a running thread's current stack.
func Unwind(s SavedStack, load func(uint64) (*ELF, Mapping, error)) StackTrace {
	out := StackTrace{Frames: []StackFrame{}}
	if len(s.Data) > 16384 {
		out.Stop = "Saved stack exceeds limit"
		return out
	}
	base, err := unsigned(s.Base)
	if err != nil {
		out.Stop = "Invalid stack base"
		return out
	}
	pc, err := unsigned(s.PC)
	if err != nil {
		out.Stop = "No verified PC"
		return out
	}
	names := []string{"rax_saved", "rdx", "rcx", "rbx", "rsi", "rdi", "rbp", "rsp", "r8", "r9", "r10", "r11", "r12", "r13", "r14", "r15", "rip"}
	regs := map[uint64]uint64{}
	for i, name := range names {
		if v, e := unsigned(s.Registers[name]); e == nil {
			regs[uint64(i)] = v
		}
	}
	read := func(addr uint64) (uint64, bool) {
		if addr < base || addr-base > uint64(len(s.Data)) || uint64(len(s.Data))-(addr-base) < 8 {
			return 0, false
		}
		return binary.LittleEndian.Uint64(s.Data[addr-base:]), true
	}
	for depth := 0; depth < 64; depth++ {
		sp, ok := regs[7]
		if !ok {
			out.Stop = "Stack pointer unavailable"
			break
		}
		entry := StackFrame{PC: fmt.Sprintf("0x%016x", pc), SP: fmt.Sprintf("0x%016x", sp), FP: fmt.Sprintf("0x%016x", regs[6])}
		lookupPC := pc
		if depth > 0 && pc > 0 {
			lookupPC--
		}
		e, m, err := load(lookupPC)
		entry.Module = m.Path
		if err != nil {
			out.Frames = append(out.Frames, entry)
			out.Stop = err.Error()
			break
		}
		if e.Machine != elf.EM_X86_64 || e.Class != elf.ELFCLASS64 {
			out.Frames = append(out.Frames, entry)
			out.Stop = "Only x86_64 CFI is supported"
			break
		}
		value, ok := e.Virtual(m, lookupPC)
		if !ok {
			out.Frames = append(out.Frames, entry)
			out.Stop = "PC outside ELF load segment"
			break
		}
		entry.Module = m.Path
		if sym := e.Lookup(value); sym != nil {
			entry.Symbol = fmt.Sprintf("%s+0x%x", sym.Name, value-sym.Value)
		}
		out.Frames = append(out.Frames, entry)
		ctx, err := e.frameAt(value)
		if err != nil {
			out.Stop = err.Error()
			break
		}
		next, err := unwindRow(ctx, regs, read)
		if err != nil {
			out.Stop = err.Error()
			break
		}
		if next[7] <= sp {
			out.Stop = "Non-increasing stack pointer"
			break
		}
		if next[16] == 0 {
			out.Stop = "End of stack"
			break
		}
		pc = next[16]
		regs = next
		if depth == 63 {
			out.Stop = "64-frame limit"
		}
	}
	return out
}
func unwindRow(ctx *frame.FrameContext, regs map[uint64]uint64, read func(uint64) (uint64, bool)) (map[uint64]uint64, error) {
	if ctx.CFA.Rule != frame.RuleCFA {
		return nil, fmt.Errorf("CFI expression for CFA is not supported")
	}
	v, ok := regs[ctx.CFA.Reg]
	if !ok {
		return nil, fmt.Errorf("CFI register unavailable")
	}
	cfa, ok := addOffset(v, ctx.CFA.Offset)
	if !ok {
		return nil, fmt.Errorf("CFI address overflow")
	}
	next := map[uint64]uint64{7: cfa}
	for _, r := range []uint64{3, 6, 12, 13, 14, 15} {
		if v, ok := regs[r]; ok {
			next[r] = v
		}
	}
	for r, rule := range ctx.Regs {
		var value uint64
		valid := true
		switch rule.Rule {
		case frame.RuleUndefined:
			delete(next, r)
			continue
		case frame.RuleSameVal:
			value, valid = regs[r]
		case frame.RuleRegister:
			value, valid = regs[rule.Reg]
		case frame.RuleOffset:
			var addr uint64
			addr, valid = addOffset(cfa, rule.Offset)
			if valid {
				value, valid = read(addr)
			}
		case frame.RuleValOffset:
			value, valid = addOffset(cfa, rule.Offset)
		default:
			return nil, fmt.Errorf("Unsupported CFI register rule")
		}
		if !valid {
			return nil, fmt.Errorf("Required register or saved stack bytes unavailable")
		}
		next[r] = value
	}
	ret, ok := next[ctx.RetAddrReg]
	if !ok {
		return nil, fmt.Errorf("Return address unavailable")
	}
	next[16] = ret
	next[7] = cfa
	return next, nil
}
```

`go/internal/symbols/unwind_test.go`:

```go
package symbols

import (
	"github.com/go-delve/delve/pkg/dwarf/frame"
	"testing"
)

func TestCFIWithoutFramePointer(t *testing.T) {
	ctx := &frame.FrameContext{CFA: frame.DWRule{Rule: frame.RuleCFA, Reg: 7, Offset: 8}, RetAddrReg: 16, Regs: map[uint64]frame.DWRule{16: {Rule: frame.RuleOffset, Offset: -8}}}
	regs := map[uint64]uint64{7: 0x1000, 6: 5}
	next, err := unwindRow(ctx, regs, func(addr uint64) (uint64, bool) { return 0x400123, addr == 0x1000 })
	if err != nil || next[16] != 0x400123 || next[7] != 0x1008 || next[6] != 5 {
		t.Fatalf("CFI without RBP failed: %v %v", next, err)
	}
	if _, err = unwindRow(ctx, regs, func(uint64) (uint64, bool) { return 0, false }); err == nil {
		t.Fatal("missing saved memory guessed")
	}
	ctx.CFA.Rule = frame.RuleExpression
	if _, err = unwindRow(ctx, regs, nil); err == nil {
		t.Fatal("unsupported CFI guessed")
	}
}
```

```ps1
param([string]$PacketProxyJar = "$PSScriptRoot/../../PacketProxy/build/libs/PacketProxy.jar")
$ErrorActionPreference = 'Stop'
$out = "$PSScriptRoot/build"
New-Item -ItemType Directory -Force "$out/classes" | Out-Null
javac -encoding UTF-8 --release 17 -cp $PacketProxyJar -d "$out/classes" "$PSScriptRoot/src/CommunicationOrigins.java" "$PSScriptRoot/src/DebuggerView.java" "$PSScriptRoot/src/SymbolLookup.java" "$PSScriptRoot/src/FridaLookup.java" "$PSScriptRoot/src/FridaView.java" "$PSScriptRoot/src/FridaStream.java" "$PSScriptRoot/src/AnalyzerSettings.java" "$PSScriptRoot/src/JavaSourceView.java" "$PSScriptRoot/src/ProcessBrowser.java" "$PSScriptRoot/src/UiLayout.java"
if ($LASTEXITCODE -ne 0) { throw 'Plugin compilation failed' }
jar --create --file "$out/CommunicationOrigins.jar" -C "$out/classes" ebpfproxy
if ($LASTEXITCODE -ne 0) { throw 'Plugin packaging failed' }
Write-Output "$out/CommunicationOrigins.jar"
```

`plugin/src/AnalyzerSettings.java`:

```java
package ebpfproxy;

import java.awt.*;
import java.nio.file.*;
import java.util.prefs.Preferences;
import javax.swing.*;

final class AnalyzerSettings {
  static String jadxPath() {
    return System.getProperty("packetproxy.jadx.path",
        Preferences.userRoot().node("ebpfproxy").get("jadxPath", ""));
  }
  static void show(Component parent) {
    Object host = parent;
    while (host instanceof Component component) {
      if (component instanceof JComponent jc && jc.getClientProperty("ebpf.eventMode") instanceof JComboBox<?> mode) {
        @SuppressWarnings("unchecked") JComboBox<String> current = (JComboBox<String>) mode;
        show(parent, current);
        return;
      }
      host = component.getParent();
    }
    show(parent, null);
  }
  static void show(Component parent, JComboBox<String> currentMode) {
    SettingsForm form = new SettingsForm(currentMode);
    JTextField path = form.path;
    JComboBox<String> analysis = form.analysis;
    while (JOptionPane.showConfirmDialog(parent, form, "eBPF Analyzer Settings",
        JOptionPane.OK_CANCEL_OPTION, JOptionPane.PLAIN_MESSAGE) == JOptionPane.OK_OPTION) {
      String value = path.getText().trim();
      boolean valid;
      try { valid = value.isEmpty() || Files.isRegularFile(Path.of(value)); }
      catch (InvalidPathException ex) { valid = false; }
      if (!valid) {
        JOptionPane.showMessageDialog(parent, "Select an existing jadx CLI file.");
        continue;
      }
      Preferences prefs = Preferences.userRoot().node("ebpfproxy");
      prefs.put("jadxPath", value);
      try { prefs.flush(); }
      catch (java.util.prefs.BackingStoreException ex) {
        JOptionPane.showMessageDialog(parent, "Unable to save settings: " + ex.getMessage());
        continue;
      }
      System.setProperty("packetproxy.jadx.path", value);
      if (currentMode != null) currentMode.setSelectedIndex(analysis.getSelectedIndex());
      return;
    }
  }
  static final class SettingsForm extends JPanel {
    final JComboBox<String> analysis = new JComboBox<>(new String[] {"None", "Manual", "Frida"});
    final JTextField path = new JTextField(jadxPath(), 36);
    final JButton browse = UiLayout.button("Browse...");
    int row;
    SettingsForm(JComboBox<String> currentMode) {
      super(new GridBagLayout());
      setBorder(BorderFactory.createEmptyBorder(12, 12, 12, 12));
      UiLayout.combo(analysis);
      analysis.setSelectedIndex(currentMode == null ? 0 : currentMode.getSelectedIndex());
      if (currentMode != null) {
        heading("I/O analysis", 0);
        JPanel mode = new JPanel(new FlowLayout(FlowLayout.LEFT, 0, 0));
        mode.add(analysis); full(mode, 8);
        hint("Applies to this session. Frida collects future I/O stacks for the selected process and enables Modules / Java.");
      }
      heading("Java source", currentMode == null ? 0 : 18);
      full(new JLabel("jadx CLI executable"), 8);
      GridBagConstraints field = constraints(6);
      field.gridwidth = 1; field.weightx = 1; field.gridx = 0;
      path.setMinimumSize(new Dimension(160, path.getPreferredSize().height));
      add(path, field);
      GridBagConstraints button = constraints(6);
      button.gridx = 1; button.gridwidth = 1; button.weightx = 0;
      button.insets.left = 8; button.fill = GridBagConstraints.NONE;
      add(browse, button); row++;
      hint("Select bin/jadx.bat on Windows or bin/jadx on Linux/macOS. Use the CLI executable, not jadx-gui.");
      hint("Java Stack > Source reads the captured APK/DEX via ADB. You can also select a local file.");
      browse.addActionListener(e -> {
        JFileChooser chooser = new JFileChooser();
        chooser.setDialogTitle("Select jadx CLI (jadx / jadx.bat)");
        if (chooser.showOpenDialog(this) == JFileChooser.APPROVE_OPTION)
          path.setText(chooser.getSelectedFile().getAbsolutePath());
      });
    }
    GridBagConstraints constraints(int top) {
      GridBagConstraints c = new GridBagConstraints();
      c.gridx = 0; c.gridy = row; c.gridwidth = 2; c.weightx = 1;
      c.fill = GridBagConstraints.HORIZONTAL; c.anchor = GridBagConstraints.WEST;
      c.insets = new Insets(top, 0, 0, 0); return c;
    }
    void full(Component component, int top) { add(component, constraints(top)); row++; }
    void heading(String text, int top) {
      JLabel label = new JLabel(text);
      label.setFont(label.getFont().deriveFont(Font.BOLD)); full(label, top);
    }
    void hint(String text) {
      JTextArea area = new JTextArea(text, 2, 46);
      area.setFont(UIManager.getFont("Label.font"));
      area.setForeground(UIManager.getColor("Label.foreground"));
      area.setEditable(false); area.setFocusable(false); area.setOpaque(false);
      area.setLineWrap(true); area.setWrapStyleWord(true);
      area.setBorder(null); full(area, 8);
    }
  }

}
```
