`plugin/src/CommunicationOrigins.java`:

```java
package ebpfproxy;

import com.google.gson.*;
import packetproxy.model.Extension;
import packetproxy.gui.TableHeaderStyle;
import javax.swing.*;
import javax.swing.event.*;
import javax.swing.table.*;
import java.awt.*;
import java.nio.file.*;
import java.io.*;
import java.time.*;
import java.util.*;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.regex.Pattern;

/** Passive observations only: never inserts History records or sends network traffic. */
public final class CommunicationOrigins extends Extension {
  private JPanel panel;
  private Path file;
  private JLabel status;
  private JLabel packetScope;
  private JTextField path;
  private JTable connections, packets;
  private DefaultTableModel connectionRows, packetRows;
  private JTextArea details, bytes;
  private DebuggerView connectView;
  private JTable ioEvents;
  private DefaultTableModel eventRows;
  private DebuggerView eventView;
  private JLabel eventScope;
  private JTabbedPane tabs;
  private JComponent eventPanel;
  private ProcessBrowser processBrowser;
  private final JComboBox<String> eventMode =
      new JComboBox<>(new String[] {"None", "Manual", "Frida"});
  private final List<JsonObject> capturedEvents = new ArrayList<>();
  private FridaStream stream;
  private long streamPid;
  private String streamStatus = "";
  private void updateStream() {
    long pid = 0;
    JsonObject target = null;
    for (JsonElement entry : flows) {
      JsonObject flow = entry.getAsJsonObject();
      if (Objects.equals(selected, str(flow, "connectionId"))) {
        pid = number(flow, "pid");
        target = origins.get(selected);
        if (target == null) {
          target = new JsonObject();
          target.addProperty("pid", pid);
          target.addProperty("connectionId", selected);
          target.addProperty("capturedAtBootNs", selected.substring(selected.lastIndexOf('/') + 1));
        }
        break;
      }
    }
    if (eventMode.getSelectedIndex() != 2) {
      if (stream != null)
        stream.stop();
      stream = null;
      streamPid = 0;
      return;
    }
    if (pid == 0 || pid == streamPid)
      return;
    if (stream != null)
      stream.stop();
    streamPid = pid;
    capturedEvents.clear();
    if (processBrowser != null) processBrowser.reset(pid);
    streamStatus = "Attaching to PID " + pid + "...";
    FridaStream active = new FridaStream();
    stream = active;
    active.start(target.deepCopy(), event -> {
      if (stream != active)
        return;
      String type = str(event, "type");
      if (type.equals("event")) {
        capturedEvents.removeIf(old -> str(old, "eventId").equals(str(event, "eventId")));
        capturedEvents.add(event);
        while (capturedEvents.size() > 1000) capturedEvents.remove(0);
        syncBrowserEvents();
      } else if (type.equals("ready"))
        streamStatus = "Collecting PID " + streamPid + " / all TIDs";
      else if (type.equals("error"))
        streamStatus = "Stopped: " + str(event, "error");
      else if (type.equals("stats"))
        streamStatus =
            "Collecting PID " + streamPid + " / all TIDs / dropped " + str(event, "dropped");
      else if (Set.of("catalog", "members", "catalogError").contains(type)) {
        processBrowser.accept(event);
        return;
      }
      renderEvents();
    });
  }
  private JsonArray events = new JsonArray();
  private final List<JsonObject> visibleEvents = new ArrayList<>();
  private String selectedEvent, renderedEvent;
  private boolean updatingEvents;
  private final Map<String, JsonObject> origins = new HashMap<>();
  private String renderedOrigin;
  private JsonArray flows = new JsonArray(), copies = new JsonArray();
  private final List<JsonObject> visibleCopies = new ArrayList<>();
  private javax.swing.Timer timer;
  private boolean busy, updating;
  private String selected;
  private long generation;
  private final Map<String, Long> synTimes = new HashMap<>();
  private final Set<String> clearedConnections = new HashSet<>(), clearedPackets = new HashSet<>(),
                            clearedEvents = new HashSet<>();
  private JsonObject lastRoot;
  private static void clearMenu(JTable table, Runnable action) {
    JPopupMenu menu = new JPopupMenu();
    JMenuItem clear = new JMenuItem("Clear");
    clear.addActionListener(e -> action.run());
    menu.add(clear);
    table.setComponentPopupMenu(menu);
  }
  private void clearConnections() {
    for (JsonElement e : flows) clearedConnections.add(str(e.getAsJsonObject(), "connectionId"));
    selected = null;
    if (lastRoot != null)
      render(lastRoot);
  }
  private void clearPackets() {
    for (JsonObject p : visibleCopies) clearedPackets.add(p.toString());
    renderDetails();
  }
  private void clearEvents() {
    for (JsonObject e : visibleEvents) clearedEvents.add(str(e, "eventId"));
    selectedEvent = null;
    renderedEvent = null;
    syncBrowserEvents();
    renderEvents();
  }
  private void syncBrowserEvents() {
    if (processBrowser != null)
      processBrowser.setEvents(capturedEvents.stream().filter(event -> !clearedEvents.contains(str(event, "eventId"))).toList());
  }
  private void requestInspection(JsonObject command) {
    try {
      if (stream == null) throw new IOException("Select Frida in Settings and a connection first");
      stream.request(command);
    } catch (IOException ex) {
      JsonObject error = command.deepCopy();
      error.addProperty("type", "catalogError"); error.addProperty("error", ex.getMessage());
      processBrowser.accept(error);
    }
  }
  private void openCapturedEvent(String id) {
    selectedEvent = id;
    renderEvents();
    tabs.setSelectedComponent(eventPanel);
  }
  private void updateBrowserTab() {
    if (tabs == null || processBrowser == null) return;
    if (eventMode.getSelectedIndex() == 2) {
      if (tabs.indexOfComponent(processBrowser) < 0) tabs.addTab("Modules / Java", processBrowser);
    } else {
      tabs.remove(processBrowser);
      processBrowser.reset(0);
    }
  }

  public CommunicationOrigins() {
    setName("eBPF Analyzer");
    setEnabled(true);
  }
  public CommunicationOrigins(String name, String path) {
    this();
    setName(name);
    setPath(path);
  }

  @Override
  public JComponent createPanel() {
    // Old database registrations remain inert after renaming the extension.
    if ("\u901a\u4fe1\u7b87\u6240".equals(getName()))
      return null;
    if (panel != null)
      return panel; // Host may call createPanel twice.
    panel = new JPanel(new BorderLayout());
    panel.putClientProperty("ebpf.eventMode", eventMode);
    Path defaultFile =
        Path.of("../ebpf-analyzer/captures/monitor.json").toAbsolutePath().normalize();
    file = Path.of(System.getProperty("packetproxy.monitor.file", defaultFile.toString()));
    JPanel top = new JPanel(new BorderLayout(4, 4));
    path = new JTextField(file.toString());
    path.setEditable(false);
    JButton open = UiLayout.button("Open Monitor File...");
    open.addActionListener(e -> {
      JFileChooser chooser = new JFileChooser(file.toFile());
      chooser.setLocale(Locale.ENGLISH);
      chooser.updateUI();
      chooser.setDialogTitle("Open Monitor File");
      if (chooser.showOpenDialog(panel) == JFileChooser.APPROVE_OPTION) {
        file = chooser.getSelectedFile().toPath();
        path.setText(file.toString());
        generation++;
        synTimes.clear();
        clearedConnections.clear();
        clearedPackets.clear();
        clearedEvents.clear();
        if (stream != null)
          stream.stop();
        stream = null;
        streamPid = 0;
        if (processBrowser != null) processBrowser.reset(0);
        clear();
        refresh();
      }
    });
    open.setToolTipText("Select captures/monitor.json created by the host monitor");
    status = new JLabel("Start the host monitor, then open captures/monitor.json.");
    JPanel fileRow = new JPanel(new BorderLayout(4, 0));
    fileRow.add(new JLabel("Monitor file:"), BorderLayout.WEST);
    fileRow.add(path, BorderLayout.CENTER);
    top.add(fileRow, BorderLayout.NORTH);
    top.add(status, BorderLayout.CENTER);
    JPanel fileButtons = new JPanel(new FlowLayout(FlowLayout.RIGHT, 4, 0));
    fileButtons.add(open);
    JButton settings = UiLayout.button("Settings");
    settings.addActionListener(e -> AnalyzerSettings.show(panel, eventMode));
    fileButtons.add(settings);
    top.add(UiLayout.horizontal(fileButtons), BorderLayout.SOUTH);
    panel.add(top, BorderLayout.SOUTH);
    JLabel guide = new JLabel("<html>Start the host monitor, then click <b>Open Monitor File...</b> "
        + "at the bottom right to select <b>captures/monitor.json</b>.<br>"
        + "Generate a new TCP connection in the app, then select its row below to inspect packets and stacks.</html>");
    guide.setBorder(BorderFactory.createEmptyBorder(6, 6, 6, 6));
    panel.add(guide, BorderLayout.NORTH);
    connectionRows = rows(
        "SYN Time", "PID", "UID", "App", "Process", "Client", "Server", "Status", "PC", "Updated");
    connections = new JTable(connectionRows);
    connections.setSelectionMode(ListSelectionModel.SINGLE_SELECTION);
    styleTable(connections);
    clearMenu(connections, this::clearConnections);
    connections.setToolTipText("SYN Time: connection start. Updated: latest observation of the "
                               + "connection, not the last payload transfer.");
    // Keep technical fields available in Details, rather than crowding the list.
    for (int column : new int[] {8, 4, 2})
      connections.removeColumn(connections.getColumnModel().getColumn(column));
    TableRowSorter<DefaultTableModel> sorter = new TableRowSorter<>(connectionRows);
    connections.setRowSorter(sorter);
    JTextField filter = new JTextField() {
      @Override
      protected void paintComponent(Graphics g) {
        super.paintComponent(g);
        if (getText().isEmpty()) {
          Graphics copy = g.create();
          copy.setColor(UIManager.getColor("TextField.inactiveForeground"));
          Insets in = getInsets();
          copy.drawString("Filter by app, PID or address", in.left + 2,
              (getHeight() - getFontMetrics(getFont()).getHeight()) / 2
                  + getFontMetrics(getFont()).getAscent());
          copy.dispose();
        }
      }
    };
    filter.getDocument().addDocumentListener(new DocumentListener() {
      private void update() {
        sorter.setRowFilter(filter.getText().isEmpty()
                ? null
                : RowFilter.regexFilter("(?i)" + Pattern.quote(filter.getText())));
      }
      public void insertUpdate(DocumentEvent e) {
        update();
      }
      public void removeUpdate(DocumentEvent e) {
        update();
      }
      public void changedUpdate(DocumentEvent e) {
        update();
      }
    });
    JPanel list = new JPanel(new BorderLayout());
    JPanel search = new JPanel(new BorderLayout(6, 0));
    search.add(new JLabel("Filter: "), BorderLayout.WEST);
    search.add(filter);
    filter.setToolTipText("Filter by app, PID or address");
    list.add(search, BorderLayout.NORTH);
    list.add(new JScrollPane(connections));
    details = textArea();
    bytes = textArea();
    connectView = new DebuggerView();
    packetRows = rows("Time", "Type", "Source", "Destination", "Length", "Direction");
    packets = new JTable(packetRows);
    packets.setSelectionMode(ListSelectionModel.SINGLE_SELECTION);
    styleTable(packets);
    clearMenu(packets, this::clearPackets);
    packets.getColumnModel().getColumn(5).setPreferredWidth(65);
    packets.setToolTipText("\u2192 Client request   /   \u2190 Server response");
    packets.getSelectionModel().addListSelectionListener(e -> {
      if (e.getValueIsAdjusting())
        return;
      int row = packets.getSelectedRow();
      bytes.setText(row < 0 || row >= visibleCopies.size() ? "" : hex(visibleCopies.get(row)));
      bytes.setCaretPosition(0);
    });
    JSplitPane packetSplit =
        new JSplitPane(JSplitPane.VERTICAL_SPLIT, new JScrollPane(packets), new JScrollPane(bytes));
    packetSplit.setResizeWeight(.5);
    JPanel packetPanel = new JPanel(new BorderLayout());
    packetScope = new JLabel("Select a connection");
    JPanel packetToolbar = new JPanel(new BorderLayout());
    packetToolbar.add(packetScope);
    packetPanel.add(packetToolbar, BorderLayout.NORTH);
    packetPanel.add(packetSplit);
    tabs = new JTabbedPane();
    processBrowser = new ProcessBrowser(this::requestInspection, this::openCapturedEvent);
    tabs.addTab("Details", new JScrollPane(details));
    tabs.addTab("Packets", packetPanel);
    eventPanel = createEventsPanel();
    tabs.addTab("I/O Events", eventPanel);
    tabs.addTab("Connect", connectView);
    JSplitPane split = new JSplitPane(JSplitPane.VERTICAL_SPLIT, list, tabs);
    split.setResizeWeight(.5);
    panel.add(split);
    connections.getSelectionModel().addListSelectionListener(e -> {
      if (updating || e.getValueIsAdjusting())
        return;
      int row = connections.getSelectedRow();
      selected = row < 0 ? null
                         : str(flows.get(connections.convertRowIndexToModel(row)).getAsJsonObject(),
                               "connectionId");
      renderDetails();
    });
    timer = new javax.swing.Timer(1000, e -> refresh());
    panel.addHierarchyListener(e -> {
      if (panel.isShowing()) {
        timer.start();
        refresh();
      } else {
        timer.stop();
      }
    });
    refresh();
    return panel;
  }

  private static DefaultTableModel rows(String... columns) {
    return new DefaultTableModel(columns, 0) {
      @Override
      public boolean isCellEditable(int r, int c) {
        return false;
      }
    };
  }
  static void styleTable(JTable table) {
    table.setFillsViewportHeight(true);
    table.setRowHeight(table.getFontMetrics(table.getFont()).getHeight());
    table.setDefaultRenderer(Object.class, new DefaultTableCellRenderer() {
      @Override
      public Component getTableCellRendererComponent(
          JTable t, Object value, boolean selected, boolean focus, int row, int column) {
        Component c = super.getTableCellRendererComponent(t, value, selected, focus, row, column);
        if (!selected)
          c.setBackground(row % 2 == 0 ? Color.WHITE : new Color(0xf0, 0xf0, 0xf0));
        return c;
      }
    });
    TableHeaderStyle.apply(table, table.getColumnCount());
  }
  private static JTextArea textArea() {
    JTextArea a = new JTextArea();
    a.setEditable(false);
    a.setFont(new Font(Font.MONOSPACED, Font.PLAIN, 12));
    return a;
  }
  private static String str(JsonObject o, String k) {
    return o.has(k) && !o.get(k).isJsonNull() ? o.get(k).getAsString() : "";
  }
  private static long number(JsonObject o, String k) {
    return o.has(k) ? o.get(k).getAsLong() : 0;
  }
  private static String time(long value) {
    return java.time.format.DateTimeFormatter.ofPattern("HH:mm:ss.SSS")
        .format(Instant.ofEpochMilli(value).atZone(ZoneId.systemDefault()));
  }
  private static String endpoint(JsonObject o, String ip, String port) {
    String a = displayIP(str(o, ip));
    return (a.contains(":") ? "[" + a + "]" : a) + ":" + str(o, port);
  }

  static String displayIP(String value) {
    // Parse numeric IPv6 only; never resolve hostnames or turn native IPv6 into IPv4.
    if (!value.contains(":"))
      return value;
    try {
      java.net.InetAddress address = java.net.InetAddress.getByName(value);
      if (address instanceof java.net.Inet4Address)
        return address.getHostAddress();
    } catch (java.net.UnknownHostException ignored) {
    }
    return value;
  }
  static String displayEndpoint(String value) {
    if (!value.startsWith("["))
      return value;
    int end = value.indexOf(']');
    if (end < 0)
      return value;
    String ip = displayIP(value.substring(1, end));
    return (ip.contains(":") ? "[" + ip + "]" : ip) + value.substring(end + 1);
  }

  static JsonObject read(Path file) throws IOException {
    byte[] data;
    try (InputStream in = Files.newInputStream(file)) {
      data = in.readNBytes(16 * 1024 * 1024 + 1);
    }
    if (data.length > 16 * 1024 * 1024)
      throw new IOException("snapshot exceeds 16 MiB");
    try {
      JsonObject root =
          JsonParser.parseString(new String(data, java.nio.charset.StandardCharsets.UTF_8))
              .getAsJsonObject();
      if (number(root, "schemaVersion") != 1 || !str(root, "mode").equals("passive"))
        throw new IOException("passive schema required");
      if (root.getAsJsonArray("connections").size() > 2000
          || root.getAsJsonArray("packets").size() > 512)
        throw new IOException("snapshot count limit exceeded");
      Set<String> ids = new HashSet<>();
      for (JsonElement entry : root.getAsJsonArray("connections")) {
        JsonObject c = entry.getAsJsonObject();
        String id = str(c, "connectionId");
        if (id.isEmpty() || !ids.add(id) || number(c, "pid") <= 0
            || number(c, "validToEpochMs") <= number(c, "validFromEpochMs"))
          throw new IOException("invalid connection lifetime");
      }
      for (JsonElement entry : root.getAsJsonArray("packets")) {
        JsonObject p = entry.getAsJsonObject();
        byte[] b = Base64.getDecoder().decode(str(p, "data"));
        if (b.length > 2048 || number(p, "originalLength") < b.length)
          throw new IOException("invalid packet length");
      }
      for (String group : new String[] {"origins", "ioEvents"})
        if (root.has(group) && !root.get(group).isJsonNull()) {
          JsonArray entries = root.getAsJsonArray(group);
          if (entries.size() > 128)
            throw new IOException("origin snapshot count limit exceeded");
          Set<String> originIds = new HashSet<>();
          for (JsonElement entry : entries) {
            JsonObject o = entry.getAsJsonObject();
            String id = str(o, group.equals("ioEvents") ? "eventId" : "connectionId");
            byte[] raw = Base64.getDecoder().decode(str(o, "stack"));
            if (id.isEmpty() || !originIds.add(id) || !str(o, "architecture").equals("x86_64")
                || raw.length > 16384 || raw.length != number(o, "capturedBytes")
                || raw.length > number(o, "requestedBytes")
                || (number(o, "requestedBytes") != 4096 && number(o, "requestedBytes") != 16384))
              throw new IOException("invalid raw stack snapshot");
            if (!str(o, "stackBase").matches("0x[0-9a-fA-F]{16}"))
              throw new IOException("invalid stack address");
            if (!o.get("registers").isJsonObject() || o.getAsJsonObject("registers").size() > 21)
              throw new IOException("invalid registers");
            if (group.equals("ioEvents")
                && (!Set.of("send", "receive").contains(str(o, "operation"))
                    || !str(o, "phase").equals("entry") || number(o, "tid") <= 0))
              throw new IOException("invalid I/O event");
          }
        }
      return root;
    } catch (RuntimeException e) {
      throw new IOException("invalid monitor JSON", e);
    }
  }
  private void refresh() {
    if (busy)
      return;
    busy = true;
    Path current = file;
    long version = generation;
    new SwingWorker<JsonObject, Void>() {
      protected JsonObject doInBackground() throws Exception {
        return read(current);
      }
      protected void done() {
        busy = false;
        if (version != generation) {
          refresh();
          return;
        }
        try {
          render(get());
        } catch (Exception e) {
          clear();
          Throwable cause = e.getCause() == null ? e : e.getCause();
          status.setText(cause instanceof NoSuchFileException
                  ? "File not found. Start the monitor, then click Open Monitor File... at the bottom right."
                  : "Unable to read monitor file. Select a valid monitor.json with Open Monitor File...");
          status.setToolTipText(current + " | " + cause.getMessage());
        }
      }
    }.execute();
  }
  private void clear() {
    if (connectionRows == null)
      return;
    updating = true;
    connectionRows.setRowCount(0);
    updating = false;
    flows = new JsonArray();
    copies = new JsonArray();
    events = new JsonArray();
    origins.clear();
    selected = null;
    renderDetails();
  }
  private void render(JsonObject root) {
    lastRoot = root;
    Set<String> current = new HashSet<>();
    flows = new JsonArray();
    for (JsonElement entry : root.getAsJsonArray("connections")) {
      JsonObject c = entry.getAsJsonObject().deepCopy();
      String id = str(c, "connectionId");
      current.add(id);
      long start = synTimes.computeIfAbsent(id, k -> number(c, "validFromEpochMs"));
      c.addProperty("validFromEpochMs", start);
      if (!clearedConnections.contains(id))
        flows.add(c);
    }
    synTimes.keySet().retainAll(current);
    clearedConnections.retainAll(current);
    copies = root.getAsJsonArray("packets");
    Set<String> packetKeys = new HashSet<>();
    for (JsonElement entry : copies) packetKeys.add(entry.toString());
    clearedPackets.retainAll(packetKeys);
    events = root.has("ioEvents") && !root.get("ioEvents").isJsonNull()
        ? root.getAsJsonArray("ioEvents")
        : new JsonArray();
    Set<String> eventKeys = new HashSet<>();
    for (JsonElement entry : events) eventKeys.add(str(entry.getAsJsonObject(), "eventId"));
    for (JsonObject entry : capturedEvents) eventKeys.add(str(entry, "eventId"));
    clearedEvents.retainAll(eventKeys);
    origins.clear();
    if (root.has("origins"))
      for (JsonElement e : root.getAsJsonArray("origins")) {
        JsonObject o = e.getAsJsonObject();
        origins.put(str(o, "connectionId"), o);
      }
    updating = true;
    connectionRows.setRowCount(0);
    int selectedIndex = -1;
    for (int i = 0; i < flows.size(); i++) {
      JsonObject c = flows.get(i).getAsJsonObject();
      JsonObject origin = origins.get(str(c, "connectionId"));
      connectionRows.addRow(new Object[] {time(number(c, "validFromEpochMs")), number(c, "pid"),
          number(c, "uid"), str(c, "appName"), str(c, "processName"),
          endpoint(c, "clientIp", "clientPort"), endpoint(c, "serverIp", "serverPort"),
          str(c, "closed").equals("true") ? "Closed" : "Observed open",
          origin == null                           ? "Not captured / Evicted"
              : str(origin, "syscallPc").isEmpty() ? str(origin, "status")
                                                   : str(origin, "syscallPc"),
          time(number(c, "validToEpochMs") - (str(c, "closed").equals("true") ? 1 : 0))});
      if (str(c, "connectionId").equals(selected))
        selectedIndex = i;
    }
    if (selectedIndex >= 0) {
      int view = connections.convertRowIndexToView(selectedIndex);
      if (view >= 0)
        connections.setRowSelectionInterval(view, view);
      else
        selected = null;
    } else
      selected = null;
    updating = false;
    boolean stale = System.currentTimeMillis() - number(root, "updatedAtEpochMs") > 5000;
    status.setText((!str(root, "running").equals("true") ? "Stopped"
                           : stale                       ? "Disconnected"
                                                         : "Monitoring")
        + "   |   " + flows.size() + " connections   |   " + copies.size() + " packets");
    status.setToolTipText(file.toString() + " | Dropped snapshots: " + number(root, "originDrops")
        + " | Dropped packets: " + number(root, "copyDrops"));
    renderDetails();
  }
  private void renderDetails() {
    JsonObject owner = null;
    for (JsonElement e : flows) {
      JsonObject c = e.getAsJsonObject();
      if (str(c, "connectionId").equals(selected))
        owner = c;
    }
    details.setText(owner == null ? "Select a connection"
                                  : "App       " + str(owner, "appName") + "\nProcess   "
                + str(owner, "processName") + "\nPID       " + str(owner, "pid") + "\nUID       "
                + str(owner, "uid") + "\nTID       " + str(owner, "tid")
                + " (connect)\n\nClient    " + endpoint(owner, "clientIp", "clientPort")
                + "\nServer    " + endpoint(owner, "serverIp", "serverPort"));
    details.setCaretPosition(0);
    renderOrigin();
    renderEvents();
    // Preserve packet selection across live refresh by packet identity.
    int old = packets.getSelectedRow();
    String key = old >= 0 && old < visibleCopies.size() ? visibleCopies.get(old).toString() : null;
    visibleCopies.clear();
    packetRows.setRowCount(0);
    bytes.setText("");
    int choose = -1;
    packetScope.setText(owner == null ? "Select a connection"
                                      : endpoint(owner, "clientIp", "clientPort") + " <-> "
                + endpoint(owner, "serverIp", "serverPort"));
    packetScope.setToolTipText(selected);
    for (JsonElement e : copies) {
      JsonObject p = e.getAsJsonObject();
      String id = str(p, "connectionId");
      if (selected == null || !selected.equals(id) || clearedPackets.contains(p.toString()))
        continue;
      visibleCopies.add(p);
      byte[] b = Base64.getDecoder().decode(str(p, "data"));
      String direction = "";
      if (owner != null && id.equals(selected))
        direction =
            displayEndpoint(str(p, "source")).equals(endpoint(owner, "clientIp", "clientPort"))
            ? "\u2192"
            : "\u2190";
      packetRows.addRow(new Object[] {time(number(p, "atEpochMs")), str(p, "protocol"),
          displayEndpoint(str(p, "source")), displayEndpoint(str(p, "destination")),
          number(p, "originalLength"), direction});
      if (p.toString().equals(key))
        choose = visibleCopies.size() - 1;
    }
    if (choose >= 0)
      packets.setRowSelectionInterval(choose, choose);
  }
  private JComponent createEventsPanel() {
    eventRows = rows("Time", "Operation", "TID", "PC", "Length", "Status");
    ioEvents = new JTable(eventRows);
    ioEvents.setSelectionMode(ListSelectionModel.SINGLE_SELECTION);
    ioEvents.setAutoCreateRowSorter(true);
    styleTable(ioEvents);
    clearMenu(ioEvents, this::clearEvents);
    eventView = new DebuggerView();
    eventView.setEventMode(0);
    FridaStream.initialize();
    ioEvents.getSelectionModel().addListSelectionListener(e -> {
      if (updatingEvents || e.getValueIsAdjusting())
        return;
      int row = ioEvents.getSelectedRow();
      selectedEvent =
          row < 0 ? null : str(visibleEvents.get(ioEvents.convertRowIndexToModel(row)), "eventId");
      renderSelectedEvent();
    });
    JComponent snapshots = eventView;
    JSplitPane split =
        new JSplitPane(JSplitPane.VERTICAL_SPLIT, new JScrollPane(ioEvents), snapshots);
    split.setResizeWeight(.45);
    JPanel result = new JPanel(new BorderLayout());
    eventScope = new JLabel("Select a connection, then an I/O event");
    JPanel toolbar = new JPanel(new BorderLayout());
    JPanel settings = new JPanel(new FlowLayout(FlowLayout.LEFT));
    settings.add(new JLabel("Analysis mode: Settings"));
    JButton stopCapture = UiLayout.button("Stop");
    settings.add(stopCapture);
    stopCapture.addActionListener(e -> {
      if (stream != null)
        stream.stop();
      streamStatus = "Stopped";
      renderEvents();
    });
    toolbar.add(settings, BorderLayout.WEST);
    toolbar.add(eventScope);
    result.add(toolbar, BorderLayout.NORTH);
    result.add(split);
    eventMode.addActionListener(e -> {
      eventView.setEventMode(eventMode.getSelectedIndex());
      updateBrowserTab();
      renderedEvent = null;
      updateStream();
      renderEvents();
    });
    ioEvents.setToolTipText(
        "None / Manual shows TCP call entry. Frida shows syscall completion for all TIDs in the "
        + "selected PID. Length is the TCP call length; select an event for its result.");
    return result;
  }
  private String eventTime(JsonObject event) {
    if (event.has("capturedAtEpochMs"))
      return time(number(event, "capturedAtEpochMs"));
    String id = str(event, "connectionId");
    for (JsonElement entry : flows) {
      JsonObject c = entry.getAsJsonObject();
      if (!id.equals(str(c, "connectionId")))
        continue;
      try {
        long startNs = Long.parseLong(id.substring(id.lastIndexOf('/') + 1));
        long eventNs = Long.parseLong(str(event, "capturedAtBootNs"));
        return time(number(c, "validFromEpochMs") + eventNs / 1000000 - startNs / 1000000);
      } catch (NumberFormatException ignored) {
        return "";
      }
    }
    return "";
  }
  private void renderEvents() {
    updateStream();
    updatingEvents = true;
    eventRows.setRowCount(0);
    visibleEvents.clear();
    int choose = -1;
    boolean live = eventMode.getSelectedIndex() == 2;
    for (int i = (live ? capturedEvents.size() : events.size()) - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
      JsonObject event = live ? capturedEvents.get(i) : events.get(i).getAsJsonObject();
      if (!live && (selected == null || !selected.equals(str(event, "connectionId"))))
        continue;
      if (clearedEvents.contains(str(event, "eventId")))
        continue;
      visibleEvents.add(event);
      eventRows.addRow(
          new Object[] {eventTime(event), str(event, "operation"), number(event, "tid"),
              str(event, "syscallPc").isEmpty() ? "N/A"
                                                : DebuggerView.displayAddress(str(event, "syscallPc")),
              str(event, "requestedIoBytes"), str(event, "status")});
      if (str(event, "eventId").equals(selectedEvent))
        choose = visibleEvents.size() - 1;
    }
    if (choose >= 0) {
      int view = ioEvents.convertRowIndexToView(choose);
      if (view >= 0)
        ioEvents.setRowSelectionInterval(view, view);
      else
        selectedEvent = null;
    } else
      selectedEvent = null;
    updatingEvents = false;
    eventScope.setText(live    ? streamStatus + " / " + visibleEvents.size() + " events"
            : selected == null ? "Select a connection"
                               : visibleEvents.size() + " events");
    renderSelectedEvent();
  }
  private void renderSelectedEvent() {
    JsonObject chosen = null;
    for (JsonObject event : visibleEvents)
      if (str(event, "eventId").equals(selectedEvent)) {
        chosen = event;
        break;
      }
    String key = selectedEvent + "/" + (chosen == null ? "missing" : chosen.toString());
    if (key.equals(renderedEvent))
      return;
    renderedEvent = key;
    if (eventMode.getSelectedIndex() == 2)
      eventView.showRecorded(chosen);
    else
      eventView.showSnapshot(chosen);
  }
  private void renderOrigin() {
    JsonObject o = origins.get(selected);
    String key = selected + "/" + (o == null ? "missing" : o.toString());
    if (key.equals(renderedOrigin))
      return;
    renderedOrigin = key;
    connectView.showSnapshot(o);
  }
  private static String hex(JsonObject packet) {
    byte[] data = Base64.getDecoder().decode(str(packet, "data"));
    StringBuilder out = new StringBuilder();
    if (number(packet, "originalLength") > data.length)
      out.append(
          "Showing " + data.length + " of " + number(packet, "originalLength") + " bytes.\n\n");
    for (int i = 0; i < data.length; i += 16) {
      out.append(String.format("%04x  ", i));
      for (int j = 0; j < 16; j++)
        out.append(i + j < data.length ? String.format("%02x ", data[i + j] & 255) : "   ");
      out.append(" ");
      for (int j = i; j < Math.min(i + 16, data.length); j++) {
        int b = data[j] & 255;
        out.append(b >= 32 && b < 127 ? (char) b : '.');
      }
      out.append('\n');
    }
    return out.toString();
  }
}

```

`plugin/src/DebuggerView.java`:

```java
package ebpfproxy;

import com.google.gson.*;
import java.awt.*;
import java.awt.event.*;
import java.nio.*;
import java.util.*;
import javax.swing.*;
import javax.swing.table.*;

/** Offline debugger view. Dereferences only bytes belonging to this event. */
public final class DebuggerView extends JPanel {
  final DefaultTableModel registerRows = model("Register", "Value");
  final DefaultTableModel memoryRows =
      model("Address", "Offset", "Value (64-bit)", "References", "Text");
  final DefaultTableModel frameRows =
      model("Frame", "PC / Return Address", "Stack Address", "Source");
  final JTable registers = table(registerRows), memory = table(memoryRows),
               frames = table(frameRows);
  final JLabel title = new JLabel("Select an event"),
               unwindStatus = new JLabel("No stack available");
  final JTabbedPane stackTabs = new JTabbedPane();
  byte[] data = new byte[0];
  long base;
  final JComboBox<String> symbolMode = new JComboBox<>(new String[] {"None", "Manual"});
  final JLabel symbolLabel = new JLabel("Symbols:");
  final JButton resolve = UiLayout.button("Resolve");
  final JLabel symbolStatus = new JLabel(" ");
  JsonObject snapshot;
  boolean resolving;
  boolean externalMode;
  void setEventMode(int mode) {
    externalMode = true;
    symbolMode.setVisible(false);
    symbolLabel.setVisible(false);
    symbolMode.setSelectedIndex(mode == 0 ? 0 : 1);
    fridaView.capture.setVisible(false);
  }
  Component recordedTab;
  void showRecorded(JsonObject event) {
    if (stackTabs.indexOfComponent(fridaView) >= 0) recordedTab = stackTabs.getSelectedComponent();
    showSnapshot(event);
    if (event != null) {
      if (stackTabs.indexOfComponent(fridaView) < 0) stackTabs.addTab("Captured Stacks", fridaView);
      fridaView.display(event);
      Component preferred = recordedTab == null ? fridaView : recordedTab;
      if (stackTabs.indexOfComponent(preferred) >= 0) stackTabs.setSelectedComponent(preferred);
    }
  }

  final DefaultTableModel instructionRows = model("Address", "Bytes", "Instruction", "Comment");
  final JTable instructions = table(instructionRows);
  final JPanel disassemblyPanel = new JPanel(new BorderLayout());
  final JLabel instructionSource = new JLabel("ELF file"),
               instructionStatus = new JLabel("Select a Call Stack frame, then open Disassembly");
  final JScrollPane codeScroll = scroll(instructions);
  final JButton earlierCode = UiLayout.button("Earlier"), moreCode = UiLayout.button("More"), goPC = UiLayout.button("Go to PC");
  final java.util.List<Object[]> localFrames = new ArrayList<>();
  String localStop = "", nextCode = "", previousCode = "";
  boolean paging;
  final FridaView fridaView = new FridaView();
  String lookupKey() {
    return snapshotKey() + (symbolMode.getSelectedIndex() == 2 ? "/frida" : "");
  }

  public DebuggerView() {
    super(new BorderLayout(4, 4));
    JPanel heading = new JPanel(new BorderLayout());
    heading.add(title, BorderLayout.NORTH);
    JPanel toolbar = new JPanel(new FlowLayout(FlowLayout.LEFT, 4, 0));
    toolbar.add(symbolLabel);
    UiLayout.combo(symbolMode);
    toolbar.add(symbolMode);
    toolbar.add(resolve);
    toolbar.add(symbolStatus);
    heading.add(UiLayout.horizontal(toolbar));
    add(heading, BorderLayout.NORTH);
    resolve.setEnabled(false);
    resolve.addActionListener(e -> resolveSymbols());
    JPanel codeTop = new JPanel(new BorderLayout());
    codeTop.add(instructionSource);
    JPanel codeButtons = new JPanel(new FlowLayout(FlowLayout.RIGHT, 4, 0));
    codeButtons.add(earlierCode);
    codeButtons.add(goPC);
    codeButtons.add(moreCode);
    codeTop.add(codeButtons, BorderLayout.EAST);
    disassemblyPanel.add(codeTop, BorderLayout.NORTH);
    disassemblyPanel.add(codeScroll);
    disassemblyPanel.add(instructionStatus, BorderLayout.SOUTH);
    earlierCode.setEnabled(false);
    earlierCode.addActionListener(e -> loadCode(true));
    moreCode.setEnabled(false);
    moreCode.addActionListener(e -> loadMoreCode());
    goPC.addActionListener(e -> {
      if (snapshot == null)
        return;
      String pc = str(snapshot, "syscallPc");
      for (int i = 0; i < instructionRows.getRowCount(); i++)
        if (instructionRows.getValueAt(i, 0).toString().equalsIgnoreCase(pc)) {
          instructions.setRowSelectionInterval(i, i);
          instructions.scrollRectToVisible(instructions.getCellRect(i, 0, true));
          return;
        }
    });
    codeScroll.addMouseWheelListener(e -> {
      if (e.getWheelRotation() < 0 && nearCodeStart())
        loadCode(true);
      if (e.getWheelRotation() > 0 && nearCodeEnd())
        loadMoreCode();
    });
    codeScroll.getVerticalScrollBar().addAdjustmentListener(e -> {
      if (e.getValueIsAdjusting() && nearCodeStart()) loadCode(true);
      if (e.getValueIsAdjusting() && nearCodeEnd())
        loadMoreCode();
    });
    instructions.addKeyListener(new KeyAdapter() {
      public void keyPressed(KeyEvent e) {
        if ((e.getKeyCode() == KeyEvent.VK_UP || e.getKeyCode() == KeyEvent.VK_PAGE_UP || e.getKeyCode() == KeyEvent.VK_HOME) && nearCodeStart()) loadCode(true);
        if ((e.getKeyCode() == KeyEvent.VK_DOWN || e.getKeyCode() == KeyEvent.VK_PAGE_DOWN
                || e.getKeyCode() == KeyEvent.VK_END)
            && nearCodeEnd())
          loadMoreCode();
      }
    });
    instructions.setShowHorizontalLines(false);
    instructions.setRowHeight(Math.max(20, instructions.getRowHeight()));
    instructions.setDefaultRenderer(Object.class, new DefaultTableCellRenderer() {
      public Component getTableCellRendererComponent(
          JTable t, Object value, boolean selected, boolean focus, int row, int col) {
        super.getTableCellRendererComponent(t, value, selected, focus, row, col);
        setFont(t.getFont());
        setForeground(selected ? t.getSelectionForeground() : Color.BLACK);
        setBackground(selected ? t.getSelectionBackground() : Color.WHITE);
        boolean pc = snapshot != null
            && sameAddress(t.getValueAt(row, 0).toString(), str(snapshot, "syscallPc"));
        if (pc && col == 0) {
          setBackground(new Color(225, 242, 224));
          setForeground(new Color(25, 80, 45));
        }
        if (col == 0) {
          setText((pc ? "\u25b6 " : "  ") + displayAddress(value.toString()));
          if (!selected)
            setForeground(new Color(65, 90, 130));
        }
        if (col == 1) {
          setText(value.toString().replaceAll("(..)(?!$)", "$1 ").toUpperCase(Locale.ROOT));
          if (!selected)
            setForeground(Color.GRAY);
        }
        if (col == 2 && !selected) {
          String op = value.toString().split(" ")[0];
          setForeground(op.startsWith("j") || op.equals("call")  ? new Color(30, 75, 185)
                  : op.startsWith("ret") || op.equals("syscall") ? new Color(150, 50, 130)
                                                                 : Color.BLACK);
        }
        if (col == 3 && !selected)
          setForeground(new Color(70, 120, 75));
        return this;
      }
    });
    instructions.getColumnModel().getColumn(0).setPreferredWidth(170);
    instructions.getColumnModel().getColumn(1).setPreferredWidth(170);
    instructions.getColumnModel().getColumn(2).setPreferredWidth(280);
    symbolMode.addActionListener(e -> {
      applySymbols();
      autoResolvePending = symbolMode.getSelectedIndex() != 0;
      autoLiveKey = !externalMode && symbolMode.getSelectedIndex() == 2 ? snapshotKey() : "";
      autoResolve();
    });
    JPanel callStack = new JPanel(new BorderLayout());
    callStack.add(scroll(frames));
    callStack.add(unwindStatus, BorderLayout.SOUTH);
    stackTabs.addTab("Call Stack", callStack);
    stackTabs.addTab("Stack Memory", scroll(memory));
    stackTabs.addChangeListener(e -> SwingUtilities.invokeLater(this::refreshSelectedDisassembly));
    JSplitPane split = new JSplitPane(JSplitPane.HORIZONTAL_SPLIT, scroll(registers), stackTabs);
    split.setResizeWeight(.38);
    add(split);
    registers.setToolTipText("Double-click a value to follow it in this event's stack memory");
    memory.setToolTipText("64-bit little-endian words. Double-click a value to follow a pointer "
                          + "within this snapshot");
    frames.setToolTipText(
        "Double-click a frame to inspect its saved frame pointer in stack memory");
    follow(registers, 1);
    follow(memory, 2);
    follow(frames, 2);
    DefaultTableCellRenderer values = new DefaultTableCellRenderer() {
      public Component getTableCellRendererComponent(JTable t, Object value, boolean selected,
          boolean focus, int row, int col) {
        super.getTableCellRendererComponent(t, value, selected, focus, row, col);
        String name = t.getColumnName(col);
        Color color = name.equals("Symbol") || name.equals("References") || name.equals("Text")
            ? new Color(35, 125, 85)
            : name.contains("Address") ? new Color(45, 85, 175)
            : name.startsWith("Value") ? new Color(155, 80, 25)
            : name.equals("Register") ? new Color(125, 45, 155) : t.getForeground();
        setForeground(selected ? t.getSelectionForeground() : color);
        return this;
      }
    };
    for (JTable t : new JTable[] {registers, memory, frames}) {
      t.setDefaultRenderer(Object.class, values);
      t.setAutoResizeMode(JTable.AUTO_RESIZE_OFF);
      for (int i = 0; i < t.getColumnCount(); i++)
        t.getColumnModel().getColumn(i).setPreferredWidth(i == 0 ? 100 : 170);
    }
    memory.getColumnModel().getColumn(0).setPreferredWidth(170);
    memory.getColumnModel().getColumn(1).setPreferredWidth(80);
    memory.getColumnModel().getColumn(3).setPreferredWidth(230);
    memory.getColumnModel().getColumn(4).setPreferredWidth(90);
  }
  static JScrollPane scroll(JTable table) {
    JScrollPane pane = new JScrollPane(table);
    pane.setColumnHeaderView(table.getTableHeader());
    return pane;
  }
  static DefaultTableModel model(String... names) {
    return new DefaultTableModel(names, 0) {
      public boolean isCellEditable(int r, int c) {
        return false;
      }
    };
  }
  static JTable table(DefaultTableModel model) {
    JTable t = new JTable(model);
    CommunicationOrigins.styleTable(t);
    t.setFont(new Font(Font.MONOSPACED, Font.PLAIN, t.getFont().getSize()));
    t.setSelectionMode(ListSelectionModel.SINGLE_SELECTION);
    return t;
  }
  static String str(JsonObject o, String key) {
    return o.has(key) && !o.get(key).isJsonNull() ? o.get(key).getAsString() : "";
  }
  static boolean sameAddress(String a, String b) {
    try {
      return Long.parseUnsignedLong(a.replaceFirst("(?i)^0x", ""), 16)
          == Long.parseUnsignedLong(b.replaceFirst("(?i)^0x", ""), 16);
    } catch (NumberFormatException e) { return false; }
  }
  static long address(String s) {
    try {
      return Long.parseUnsignedLong(s.replaceFirst("^0x", ""), 16);
    } catch (NumberFormatException e) {
      return 0;
    }
  }
  static String hex(long n) {
    return String.format("0x%016x", n);
  }
  static String registerValue(String value) {
    try { return hex(Long.parseUnsignedLong(value.replaceFirst("(?i)^0x", ""), 16)); }
    catch (NumberFormatException ignored) { return value; }
  }
  void refreshSelectedDisassembly() {
    if (snapshot == null || stackTabs.getSelectedComponent() != disassemblyPanel) return;
    if (resolving || paging) { autoResolvePending = true; return; }
    if (!resolve.isEnabled()) return;
    JsonObject cached = SymbolLookup.get(lookupKey());
    if (cached == null || !cached.has("disassembly")
        || !sameAddress(str(cached.getAsJsonObject("disassembly"), "address"), selectedCodeAddress()))
      resolveAt(selectedCodeAddress());
  }
  static String displayAddress(String value) {
    return value.toUpperCase(Locale.ROOT).replaceFirst("^0X", "0x");
  }
  int offset(long pointer, int bytes) {
    long d = pointer - base;
    return Long.compareUnsigned(pointer, base) >= 0 && Long.compareUnsigned(d, data.length) <= 0
            && bytes <= data.length - d
        ? (int) d
        : -1;
  }
  long word(int offset) {
    return ByteBuffer.wrap(data, offset, 8).order(ByteOrder.LITTLE_ENDIAN).getLong();
  }
  void follow(JTable t, int column) {
    t.addMouseListener(new MouseAdapter() {
      public void mouseClicked(MouseEvent e) {
        if (e.getClickCount() == 2) {
          int row = t.rowAtPoint(e.getPoint());
          if (row >= 0)
            selectAddress(address(t.getValueAt(row, column).toString()));
        }
      }
    });
  }
  void selectAddress(long pointer) {
    int at = offset(pointer, 1);
    if (at < 0)
      return;
    int row = at / 8;
    stackTabs.setSelectedIndex(1);
    memory.setRowSelectionInterval(row, row);
    memory.scrollRectToVisible(memory.getCellRect(row, 0, true));
  }

  public void showSnapshot(JsonObject o) {
    snapshot = o;
    fridaView.select(o);
    localFrames.clear();
    localStop = "No stack available";
    registerRows.setRowCount(0);
    memoryRows.setRowCount(0);
    frameRows.setRowCount(0);
    data = new byte[0];
    if (o == null) {
      title.setText("Select an event");
      unwindStatus.setText("No stack available");
      applySymbols();
      return;
    }
    data = Base64.getDecoder().decode(str(o, "stack"));
    memory.setToolTipText("Captured stack: " + data.length
        + " bytes. New captures are limited to 4 KiB; deeper frames may be unavailable. Double-click to follow a saved pointer.");
    base = address(str(o, "stackBase"));
    JsonObject regs = o.getAsJsonObject("registers");
    title.setText("PID " + str(o, "pid") + "   TID " + str(o, "tid") + "   " + str(o, "operation")
        + "   " + str(o, "status"));
    Map<Long, String> markers = new HashMap<>();
    for (String key : new String[] {"rsp", "rbp"})
      if (regs.has(key))
        markers.put(address(str(regs, key)), key.toUpperCase(Locale.ROOT));
    String pc = str(o, "syscallPc");
    if (!pc.isEmpty())
      registerRows.addRow(new Object[] {"PC", registerValue(pc)});
    String[] order = {"rip", "rsp", "rbp", "rax_saved", "rbx", "rcx", "rdx", "rsi", "rdi", "r8",
        "r9", "r10", "r11", "r12", "r13", "r14", "r15", "rflags", "orig_rax", "cs", "ss"};
    for (String key : order)
      if (regs.has(key)) {
        String value = str(regs, key), note = switch (key) {case "rip"->"Syscall return address";case "rsp"->"Stack pointer";case "rbp"->"Frame pointer (if used)";case "orig_rax"->"Syscall number";case "rax_saved"->"Kernel-saved RAX";case "rflags"->flags(address(value));default->"";};
            int at=offset(address(value),1);if(at>=0)note+=(note.isEmpty()?"":" · ")+"Stack +0x"+Integer.toHexString(at);
            registerRows.addRow(new Object[] {key.toUpperCase(Locale.ROOT), registerValue(value)});
        }
        if (!pc.isEmpty())
          frameRows.addRow(new Object[] {0, pc, str(regs, "rbp"), "Current syscall"});
        // RBP records are candidates, not a DWARF unwind or a scan of arbitrary words.
        long fp = address(str(regs, "rbp"));
        String stop = "Frame pointer is outside this snapshot";
        int count = 0;
        while (count < 64) {
          int at = offset(fp, 16);
          if (at < 0)
            break;
          if ((fp & 7) != 0) {
            stop = "Unaligned frame pointer";
            break;
          }
          long next = word(at), ret = word(at + 8);
          if (ret == 0) {
            stop = "No saved return address";
            break;
          }
          markers.put(fp, "RBP chain");
          markers.put(fp + 8, "Saved return address");
          frameRows.addRow(new Object[] {++count, hex(ret), hex(fp), "RBP candidate"});
          if (next == 0) {
            stop = "End of frame-pointer chain";
            break;
          }
          if (Long.compareUnsigned(next, fp) <= 0) {
            stop = "Non-increasing frame pointer";
            break;
          }
          fp = next;
          if (count == 64)
            stop = "Frame limit reached";
        }
        unwindStatus.setText(stop + " · RBP candidates; CFI unavailable");
        localStop = stop + " · Select Manual / Resolve for CFI unwinding";
        for (int i = 0; i < frameRows.getRowCount(); i++)
          localFrames.add(new Object[] {frameRows.getValueAt(i, 0), frameRows.getValueAt(i, 1),
              frameRows.getValueAt(i, 2), frameRows.getValueAt(i, 3)});
        unwindStatus.setToolTipText(
            "Functions may omit frame pointers. Candidate return addresses have not been checked "
            + "against executable mappings; this is not a complete call stack.");
        for (int i = 0; i < data.length; i += 8) {
          int length = Math.min(8, data.length - i);
          String value;
          String note = markers.getOrDefault(base + i, "");
          if (length == 8) {
            long v = word(i);
            value = hex(v);
            int to = offset(v, 1);
            if (to >= 0)
              note += (note.isEmpty() ? "" : " · ") + "→ Stack +0x" + Integer.toHexString(to);
          } else {
            StringBuilder tail = new StringBuilder();
            for (int j = 0; j < length; j++) tail.append(String.format("%02x ", data[i + j] & 255));
            value = tail.toString().trim() + " (partial)";
          }
          StringBuilder ascii = new StringBuilder();
          for (int j = 0; j < length; j++) {
            int c = data[i + j] & 255;
            ascii.append(c >= 32 && c < 127 ? (char) c : '.');
          }
          memoryRows.addRow(new Object[] {
              hex(base + i), String.format("+0x%04x", i), value, note, ascii.toString()});
        }
        applySymbols();
        if (externalMode) SwingUtilities.invokeLater(this::refreshSelectedDisassembly);
      }
    String snapshotKey() {
      try {
        return snapshot == null ? "" : SymbolLookup.key(snapshot);
      } catch (RuntimeException e) {
        return "";
      }
    }
    void applySymbols() {
      frameRows.setRowCount(0);
      for (Object[] row : localFrames) frameRows.addRow(row);
      unwindStatus.setText(localStop);
      boolean manual = symbolMode.getSelectedIndex() != 0;
      if (externalMode && snapshot != null && snapshot.has("nativeFrames")) {
        if (stackTabs.indexOfComponent(fridaView) < 0)
          stackTabs.addTab("Captured Stacks", fridaView);
      } else if (symbolMode.getSelectedIndex() == 2) {
        if (stackTabs.indexOfComponent(fridaView) < 0)
          stackTabs.addTab("Live I/O", fridaView);
      } else
        stackTabs.remove(fridaView);
      resolve.setEnabled(
          manual && snapshot != null && !resolving && !paging && !snapshotKey().isEmpty());
      if (registerRows.getColumnCount() != (manual ? 3 : 2)) {
        registerRows.setColumnIdentifiers(manual ? new String[] {"Register", "Value", "Symbol"}
                                                 : new String[] {"Register", "Value"});
        frameRows.setColumnIdentifiers(manual
                ? new String[] {"Frame", "PC / Return Address", "Stack Address", "Source", "Symbol", "Path"}
                : new String[] {"Frame", "PC / Return Address", "Stack Address", "Source"});
        for (JTable table : new JTable[] {registers, frames})
          for (int i = 0; i < table.getColumnCount(); i++)
            table.getColumnModel().getColumn(i).setPreferredWidth(i == 0 ? 100
                    : i == table.getColumnCount() - 1 && manual          ? 400
                                                                         : 170);
      }
      if (manual) {
        if (stackTabs.indexOfComponent(disassemblyPanel) < 0)
          stackTabs.addTab("Disassembly", disassemblyPanel);
      } else {
        stackTabs.remove(disassemblyPanel);
      }
      instructionRows.setRowCount(0);
      nextCode = "";
      previousCode = "";
      earlierCode.setEnabled(false);
      moreCode.setEnabled(false);
      instructionSource.setText("ELF file");
      instructionStatus.setText("Select a Call Stack frame, then open Disassembly");
      if (!manual) {
        symbolStatus.setText(" ");
        return;
      }
      for (int i = 0; i < registerRows.getRowCount(); i++) registerRows.setValueAt("", i, 2);
      for (int i = 0; i < frameRows.getRowCount(); i++) { frameRows.setValueAt("", i, 4); frameRows.setValueAt("", i, 5); }
      applyCapturedSymbols();
      JsonObject result = SymbolLookup.get(lookupKey());
      if (result == null) {
        symbolStatus.setText(
            resolving ? "Resolving..." : "Click Resolve to read current process maps");
        return;
      }
      Map<String, String> labels = new HashMap<>();
      for (JsonElement entry : result.getAsJsonArray("results")) {
        JsonObject r = entry.getAsJsonObject();
        String name = str(r, "symbol");
        labels.put(registerValue(str(r, "address")),
            name.isEmpty() ? str(r, "module") + " [" + str(r, "status") + "]"
                           : name + " · " + str(r, "module"));
      }
      for (int i = 0; i < registerRows.getRowCount(); i++)
        registerRows.setValueAt(
            labels.getOrDefault(
                registerRows.getValueAt(i, 1).toString().toLowerCase(Locale.ROOT), ""),
            i, 2);
      for (JsonElement element : result.getAsJsonArray("results")) {
        JsonObject resolved = element.getAsJsonObject();
        for (int i = 0; i < frameRows.getRowCount(); i++) {
          if (!sameAddress(frameRows.getValueAt(i, 1).toString(), str(resolved, "address"))) continue;
          frameRows.setValueAt(FridaView.nativeSymbol(resolved), i, 4);
          frameRows.setValueAt(str(resolved, "module"), i, 5);
        }
      }
      boolean truncated = false;
      for (JsonElement entry : result.getAsJsonArray("modules")) {
        JsonObject m = entry.getAsJsonObject();
        truncated |= m.get("truncated").getAsBoolean();
      }
      symbolStatus.setText("Current maps · "
          + java.time.Instant.ofEpochMilli(result.get("resolvedAtEpochMs").getAsLong())
                .atZone(java.time.ZoneId.systemDefault())
                .toLocalTime()
                .withNano(0)
          + (truncated ? " · Symbol list limited" : ""));
      symbolStatus.setToolTipText(str(result, "scope"));
      if (result.has("stackTrace")) {
        JsonObject trace = result.getAsJsonObject("stackTrace");
        frameRows.setRowCount(0);
        int index = 0;
        for (JsonElement entry : trace.getAsJsonArray("frames")) {
          JsonObject f = entry.getAsJsonObject();
          frameRows.addRow(new Object[] {index++, str(f, "pc"), str(f, "sp"),
              index == 1 ? "Current syscall" : "CFI", str(f, "symbol") + " · " + str(f, "module")});
        }
        unwindStatus.setText("CFI: " + str(trace, "stop"));
        unwindStatus.setToolTipText("Uses ELF unwind information and this event's saved stack. "
                                    + "Current maps may differ from the capture-time layout.");
      }
      applyCapturedSymbols();
      if (result.has("disassembly")) {
        JsonObject d = result.getAsJsonObject("disassembly");
        instructionSource.setText(str(d, "source"));
        instructionSource.setToolTipText(
            str(d, "boundary") + ". Linear decoding; not an execution trace or live memory.");
        instructionStatus.setText(str(d, "stop"));
        for (JsonElement entry : d.getAsJsonArray("instructions")) {
          JsonObject i = entry.getAsJsonObject();
          instructionRows.addRow(
              new Object[] {str(i, "address"), str(i, "bytes"), str(i, "text"), str(i, "symbol")});
        }
        if (instructionRows.getRowCount() > 0) {
          int row = 0;
          for (int i = 0; i < instructionRows.getRowCount(); i++)
            if (sameAddress(instructionRows.getValueAt(i, 0).toString(), str(d, "address"))) row = i;
          instructions.setRowSelectionInterval(row, row);
          final int targetRow = row;
          SwingUtilities.invokeLater(() -> {
            if (targetRow < instructions.getRowCount())
              instructions.scrollRectToVisible(instructions.getCellRect(targetRow, 0, true));
          });
        }
        previousCode = str(d, "previous");
        earlierCode.setEnabled(!previousCode.isEmpty() && !paging && instructionRows.getRowCount() < 2048);
        nextCode = str(d, "next");
        moreCode.setEnabled(!nextCode.isEmpty() && !paging && instructionRows.getRowCount() < 2048);
      }
    }
    void applyCapturedSymbols() {
      if (snapshot == null || !snapshot.has("nativeFrames") || frameRows.getColumnCount() < 6) return;
      for (JsonElement element : snapshot.getAsJsonArray("nativeFrames")) {
        JsonObject frame = element.getAsJsonObject();
        for (int i = 0; i < frameRows.getRowCount(); i++) {
          if (!sameAddress(frameRows.getValueAt(i, 1).toString(), str(frame, "address"))) continue;
          frameRows.setValueAt(FridaView.nativeSymbol(frame), i, 4);
          frameRows.setValueAt(str(frame, "module"), i, 5);
        }
      }
    }
    boolean nearCodeEnd() {
      JScrollBar bar = codeScroll.getVerticalScrollBar();
      return bar.getValue() + bar.getVisibleAmount()
          >= bar.getMaximum() - instructions.getRowHeight() * 4;
    }
    boolean nearCodeStart() { return codeScroll.getVerticalScrollBar().getValue() < instructions.getRowHeight() * 4; }
    void loadMoreCode() { loadCode(false); }
    void loadCode(boolean before) {
      if (paging || resolving || snapshot == null || symbolMode.getSelectedIndex() == 0
          || (before ? previousCode : nextCode).isEmpty() || instructionRows.getRowCount() >= 2048)
        return;
      String key = lookupKey(), next = before ? previousCode : nextCode;
      JsonObject previous = SymbolLookup.get(key);
      if (previous == null)
        return;
      JsonObject target = snapshot.deepCopy();
      paging = true;
      earlierCode.setEnabled(false);
      moreCode.setEnabled(false);
      instructionStatus.setText("Loading...");
      new SwingWorker<JsonObject, Void>() {
        protected JsonObject doInBackground() throws Exception {
          return SymbolLookup.resolve(target, Collections.emptySet(), next, previous, before);
        }
        protected void done() {
          paging = false;
          try {
            JsonObject result = get();
            if (SymbolLookup.get(key) != previous)
              return;
            JsonObject page = result.getAsJsonObject("disassembly");
            if (page == null || !str(page, "address").equals(next))
              throw new IllegalStateException("Invalid continuation");
            JsonObject merged = previous.deepCopy(), d = merged.getAsJsonObject("disassembly");
            JsonArray old = d.getAsJsonArray("instructions"), added = page.getAsJsonArray("instructions"), lines = new JsonArray();
            int room = 2048 - old.size();
            if (before) {
              for (int i = Math.max(0, added.size() - room); i < added.size(); i++) lines.add(added.get(i));
              for (JsonElement line : old) lines.add(line);
              d.addProperty("previous", lines.size() >= 2048 ? "" : str(page, "previous"));
            } else {
              for (JsonElement line : old) lines.add(line);
              for (int i = 0; i < added.size() && i < room; i++) lines.add(added.get(i));
              d.addProperty("next", lines.size() >= 2048 ? "" : str(page, "next"));
            }
            d.add("instructions", lines);
            d.addProperty("stop", lines.size() >= 2048 ? "2048-instruction view limit" : str(page, "stop"));
            SymbolLookup.put(key, merged);
            if (!key.equals(lookupKey()) || symbolMode.getSelectedIndex() == 0) return;
            Point position = codeScroll.getViewport().getViewPosition();
            int selectedRow = instructions.getSelectedRow(), inserted = lines.size() - old.size();
            instructionRows.setRowCount(0);
            for (JsonElement element : lines) {
              JsonObject line = element.getAsJsonObject();
              instructionRows.addRow(new Object[] {str(line, "address"), str(line, "bytes"), str(line, "text"), str(line, "symbol")});
            }
            if (selectedRow >= 0) instructions.setRowSelectionInterval(selectedRow + (before ? inserted : 0), selectedRow + (before ? inserted : 0));
            if (before) position.y += inserted * instructions.getRowHeight();
            SwingUtilities.invokeLater(() -> codeScroll.getViewport().setViewPosition(position));
            previousCode = str(d, "previous");
            nextCode = str(d, "next");
            instructionStatus.setText(str(d, "stop"));
            earlierCode.setEnabled(!previousCode.isEmpty() && lines.size() < 2048);
            moreCode.setEnabled(!nextCode.isEmpty() && lines.size() < 2048);
          } catch (Exception e) {
            if (key.equals(lookupKey()) && symbolMode.getSelectedIndex() != 0) {
              instructionStatus.setText("Unable to continue. Resolve again to refresh mappings.");
              instructionStatus.setToolTipText(
                  e.getCause() == null ? e.getMessage() : e.getCause().getMessage());
              if (before) previousCode = ""; else nextCode = "";
            }
          } finally {
            autoResolve();
          }
        }
      }.execute();
    }
    boolean autoResolvePending;
    String autoLiveKey = "";
    void autoResolve() {
      if (resolving || paging)
        return;
      if (autoResolvePending) {
        autoResolvePending = false;
        if (symbolMode.getSelectedIndex() != 0 && snapshot != null && !snapshotKey().isEmpty()) {
          if (SymbolLookup.get(lookupKey()) == null)
            resolveSymbols();
          else if (!externalMode && instructionRows.getRowCount() > 0)
            stackTabs.setSelectedComponent(disassemblyPanel);
        }
      }
      if (resolving || autoLiveKey.isEmpty())
        return;
      String requested = autoLiveKey;
      autoLiveKey = "";
      if (symbolMode.getSelectedIndex() == 2 && requested.equals(snapshotKey()) && snapshot != null
          && Set.of("send", "receive").contains(str(snapshot, "operation"))) {
        stackTabs.setSelectedComponent(fridaView);
        fridaView.start();
      }
    }
    String selectedCodeAddress() {
      int row = frames.getSelectedRow();
      return row >= 0 ? frames.getValueAt(row, 1).toString() : snapshot == null ? "" : str(snapshot, "syscallPc");
    }
    void resolveSymbols() {
      resolveAt(snapshot == null ? "" : str(snapshot, "syscallPc"));
    }
    void resolveAt(String disassemblyAddress) {
      if (snapshot == null || resolving || paging)
        return;
      JsonObject target = snapshot.deepCopy();
      String key = lookupKey();
      boolean frida = symbolMode.getSelectedIndex() == 2;
      Set<String> addresses = new LinkedHashSet<>();
      for (int i = 0; i < frameRows.getRowCount(); i++)
        addresses.add(frameRows.getValueAt(i, 1).toString());
      JsonObject regs = target.getAsJsonObject("registers");
      if (regs.has("rip"))
        addresses.add(str(regs, "rip"));
      resolving = true;
      resolve.setEnabled(false);
      symbolStatus.setText("Resolving...");
      new SwingWorker<JsonObject, Void>() {
        protected JsonObject doInBackground() throws Exception {
          return frida ? FridaLookup.resolve(target, addresses, disassemblyAddress)
                       : SymbolLookup.resolve(target, addresses, disassemblyAddress);
        }
        protected void done() {
          resolving = false;
          try {
            SymbolLookup.put(key, get());
            applySymbols();
            if (!externalMode && key.equals(lookupKey()) && symbolMode.getSelectedIndex() != 0
                && !disassemblyAddress.isEmpty())
              stackTabs.setSelectedComponent(disassemblyPanel);
          } catch (Exception e) {
            applySymbols();
            if (key.equals(lookupKey()) && symbolMode.getSelectedIndex() != 0) {
              Throwable cause = e.getCause() == null ? e : e.getCause();
              symbolStatus.setText("Unable to resolve");
              symbolStatus.setToolTipText(cause.getMessage());
            }
          } finally {
            autoResolve();
          }
        }
      }.execute();
    }
    static String flags(long value) {
      String[] names = {"CF", "PF", "AF", "ZF", "SF", "TF", "IF", "DF", "OF"};
      int[] bits = {0, 2, 4, 6, 7, 8, 9, 10, 11};
      StringJoiner out = new StringJoiner(" ");
      for (int i = 0; i < bits.length; i++)
        if ((value & (1L << bits[i])) != 0)
          out.add(names[i]);
      return out.toString();
    }
  }

```

`plugin/src/FridaLookup.java`:

```java
package ebpfproxy;

import com.google.gson.*;
import java.nio.charset.StandardCharsets;
import java.nio.file.*;
import java.util.*;
import java.util.concurrent.*;

final class FridaLookup {
  private static final java.util.concurrent.locks.ReentrantLock sessionLock =
      new java.util.concurrent.locks.ReentrantLock();
  static void initialize() {} // Load while the extension class loader is open.
  static JsonObject request(JsonObject snapshot, String action, Set<String> addresses)
      throws Exception {
    if (!sessionLock.tryLock())
      throw new IllegalStateException("Another Frida operation is running");
    try {
      return perform(snapshot, action, addresses);
    } finally {
      sessionLock.unlock();
    }
  }
  private static JsonObject perform(JsonObject snapshot, String action, Set<String> addresses)
      throws Exception {
    JsonObject request = new JsonObject();
    request.addProperty("action", action);
    request.add("snapshot", snapshot);
    request.addProperty("adb", System.getProperty("packetproxy.adb", "adb"));
    request.addProperty("serial", System.getProperty("packetproxy.adb.serial", "emulator-5554"));
    JsonArray list = new JsonArray();
    addresses.stream().limit(128).forEach(list::add);
    request.add("addresses", list);
    String script = System.getProperty("packetproxy.frida.script",
        Path.of(System.getProperty("user.home"), ".packetproxy", "extensions", "ebpf-frida",
                "bridge.py")
            .toString());
    if (!Files.isRegularFile(Path.of(script)))
      throw new IllegalStateException("Install the Frida bridge and Python frida-tools package");
    Process process =
        new ProcessBuilder(System.getProperty("packetproxy.frida.python", "python"), script)
            .redirectError(ProcessBuilder.Redirect.DISCARD)
            .start();
    CompletableFuture.delayedExecutor(60, TimeUnit.SECONDS).execute(() -> {
      if (process.isAlive())
        process.destroyForcibly();
    });
    try {
      try (var input = process.getOutputStream()) {
        input.write((request + "\n").getBytes(StandardCharsets.UTF_8));
      }
      byte[] output = process.getInputStream().readNBytes(4 * 1024 * 1024 + 1);
      if (output.length > 4 * 1024 * 1024)
        throw new IllegalStateException("Frida response exceeds limit");
      JsonObject result;
      try {
        result =
            JsonParser.parseString(new String(output, StandardCharsets.UTF_8)).getAsJsonObject();
      } catch (RuntimeException e) {
        throw new IllegalStateException(
            "Frida bridge unavailable; check Python, frida-tools and frida-server");
      }
      if (result.has("error"))
        throw new IllegalStateException(result.get("error").getAsString());
      return result;
    } finally {
      if (process.isAlive())
        process.destroyForcibly();
    }
  }
  static JsonObject resolve(JsonObject snapshot, Set<String> addresses, String disassemble)
      throws Exception {
    Set<String> all = new LinkedHashSet<>(addresses);
    if (snapshot.has("syscallPc"))
      all.add(snapshot.get("syscallPc").getAsString());
    JsonArray resolved = request(snapshot, "symbols", all).getAsJsonArray("results");
    // Attach/detach changes mappings. Establish the ELF continuation baseline afterwards.
    JsonObject result = SymbolLookup.resolve(snapshot, addresses, disassemble);
    Map<String, JsonObject> labels = new HashMap<>();
    for (JsonElement e : resolved) {
      JsonObject r = e.getAsJsonObject();
      labels.put(r.get("address").getAsString().toLowerCase(Locale.ROOT), r);
    }
    for (JsonElement e : result.getAsJsonArray("results")) {
      JsonObject r = e.getAsJsonObject(),
                 f = labels.get(r.get("address").getAsString().toLowerCase(Locale.ROOT));
      if (f != null && !f.get("symbol").getAsString().isEmpty()) {
        r.add("symbol", f.get("symbol"));
        r.add("module", f.get("module"));
      }
    }
    if (result.has("stackTrace"))
      for (JsonElement e : result.getAsJsonObject("stackTrace").getAsJsonArray("frames")) {
        JsonObject r = e.getAsJsonObject(),
                   f = labels.get(r.get("pc").getAsString().toLowerCase(Locale.ROOT));
        if (f != null && !f.get("symbol").getAsString().isEmpty()) {
          r.add("symbol", f.get("symbol"));
          r.add("module", f.get("module"));
        }
      }
    result.addProperty("scope",
        "Frida symbols from current process; historical stack uses saved bytes and ELF CFI; "
        + "disassembly reads ELF files");
    return result;
  }
}
```

`plugin/src/FridaStream.java`:

```java
package ebpfproxy;
import com.google.gson.*;
import java.io.*;
import java.nio.charset.StandardCharsets;
import java.nio.file.*;
import java.util.concurrent.*;
import java.util.function.Consumer;
import javax.swing.SwingUtilities;

final class FridaStream {
  private volatile Process process;
  private volatile boolean stopped;
  private boolean initialized;
  static void initialize() {}
  synchronized void request(JsonObject request) throws IOException {
    if (stopped || !initialized || process == null || !process.isAlive()) throw new IOException("Frida collection is not running or is still attaching");
    process.getOutputStream().write((request + "\n").getBytes(StandardCharsets.UTF_8));
    process.getOutputStream().flush();
  }
  void start(JsonObject snapshot, Consumer<JsonObject> receive) {
    Thread worker = new Thread(() -> {
      try {
        String bridge = System.getProperty("packetproxy.frida.script",
            Path.of(System.getProperty("user.home"), ".packetproxy", "extensions", "ebpf-frida",
                    "bridge.py")
                .toString());
        process = new ProcessBuilder(System.getProperty("packetproxy.frida.python", "python"),
            Path.of(bridge).resolveSibling("stream.py").toString())
                      .redirectError(ProcessBuilder.Redirect.DISCARD)
                      .start();
        JsonObject request = new JsonObject();
        request.add("snapshot", snapshot);
        request.addProperty("adb", System.getProperty("packetproxy.adb", "adb"));
        request.addProperty(
            "serial", System.getProperty("packetproxy.adb.serial", "emulator-5554"));
        synchronized (this) {
          process.getOutputStream().write((request + "\n").getBytes(StandardCharsets.UTF_8));
          process.getOutputStream().flush();
          initialized = true;
        }
        if (stopped)
          stop();
        try (var reader = new BufferedReader(
                 new InputStreamReader(process.getInputStream(), StandardCharsets.UTF_8))) {
          String line;
          while ((line = reader.readLine()) != null) {
            JsonObject event = JsonParser.parseString(line).getAsJsonObject();
            if (!stopped)
              SwingUtilities.invokeAndWait(() -> {
                if (!stopped)
                  receive.accept(event);
              });
          }
        }
        if (!stopped)
          throw new IOException("Frida collection stopped");
      } catch (Exception e) {
        if (!stopped) {
          JsonObject error = new JsonObject();
          error.addProperty("type", "error");
          error.addProperty("error", e.getMessage());
          SwingUtilities.invokeLater(() -> {
            if (!stopped)
              receive.accept(error);
          });
        }
      } finally {
        stop();
      }
    }, "ebpf-frida-stream");
    worker.setDaemon(true);
    worker.start();
  }
  synchronized void stop() {
    stopped = true;
    Process p = process;
    if (p == null)
      return;
    try {
      p.getOutputStream().close();
    } catch (IOException ignored) {
    }
    CompletableFuture.delayedExecutor(10, TimeUnit.SECONDS).execute(() -> {
      if (p.isAlive())
        p.destroyForcibly();
    });
  }
}
```
