`plugin/src/FridaView.java`:

```java
package ebpfproxy;

import com.google.gson.*;
import java.awt.*;
import java.util.*;
import javax.swing.*;
import javax.swing.table.*;

final class FridaView extends JPanel {
  final JButton capture = UiLayout.button("Capture next I/O");
  final JLabel status = new JLabel("Select an I/O event");
  final DefaultTableModel nativeFrames = DebuggerView.model("Frame", "Address", "Symbol", "Path");
  final DefaultTableModel javaFrames = new DefaultTableModel(new String[] {"Frame", "Method", "File", "Line", "Source"}, 0) {
    public boolean isCellEditable(int row, int column) { return column == 4; }
  };
  final java.util.List<JsonObject> javaDetails = new ArrayList<>();
  final JTable javaTable = DebuggerView.table(javaFrames);
  final JLabel javaStatus =
      new JLabel("Java stack is collected on the target TID in the exception handler");
  JsonObject snapshot;
  boolean busy;
  FridaView() {
    super(new BorderLayout(4, 4));
    FridaLookup.initialize();
    JPanel header = new JPanel(new BorderLayout());
    header.add(capture, BorderLayout.WEST);
    header.add(status);
    add(header, BorderLayout.NORTH);
    JTabbedPane stacks = new JTabbedPane();
    stacks.addTab("Native Stack", columns(nativeFrames, 55, 180, 450, 500));
    JPanel java = new JPanel(new BorderLayout());
    javaTable.setAutoResizeMode(JTable.AUTO_RESIZE_OFF);
    javaTable.setRowHeight(Math.max(24, javaTable.getRowHeight()));
    int[] widths = {55, 500, 220, 70, 75};
    for (int i = 0; i < widths.length; i++)
      javaTable.getColumnModel().getColumn(i).setPreferredWidth(widths[i]);
    javaTable.setDefaultRenderer(Object.class, new DefaultTableCellRenderer() {
      public Component getTableCellRendererComponent(JTable table, Object value, boolean selected,
          boolean focus, int row, int col) {
        super.getTableCellRendererComponent(table, value, selected, focus, row, col);
        setForeground(selected ? table.getSelectionForeground()
            : col == 1 ? new Color(45, 85, 175) : col == 2 ? new Color(35, 125, 85) : table.getForeground());
        return this;
      }
    });
    JButton sourceButton = sourceButton();
    javaTable.getColumnModel().getColumn(4).setCellRenderer((table, value, selected, focus, row, col) -> sourceButton);
    class ShowEditor extends AbstractCellEditor implements TableCellEditor {
      final JButton button = sourceButton();
      JsonObject frame;
      ShowEditor() {
        button.addActionListener(e -> {
          JsonObject target = frame.deepCopy();
          fireEditingStopped();
          JavaSourceView.show(FridaView.this, target);
        });
      }
      public Object getCellEditorValue() { return "Show"; }
      public Component getTableCellEditorComponent(JTable table, Object value, boolean selected, int row, int col) {
        frame = javaDetails.get(table.convertRowIndexToModel(row));
        return button;
      }
    }
    javaTable.getColumnModel().getColumn(4).setCellEditor(new ShowEditor());
    java.add(DebuggerView.scroll(javaTable));
    java.add(javaStatus, BorderLayout.SOUTH);
    stacks.addTab("Java Stack", java);
    add(stacks);
    capture.setToolTipText("Wait up to 15 seconds for the next completed TCP I/O on this TID. This "
                           + "actively attaches Frida and sends one SIGTRAP; it does not recover "
                           + "an old event or follow async scheduling.");
    capture.addActionListener(e -> start());
    capture.setEnabled(false);
  }
  static JButton sourceButton() {
    JButton button = new JButton(new Icon() {
      public int getIconWidth() { return 18; }
      public int getIconHeight() { return 18; }
      public void paintIcon(Component c, Graphics graphics, int x, int y) {
        Graphics2D g = (Graphics2D) graphics.create();
        g.translate(x, y);
        g.setRenderingHint(RenderingHints.KEY_ANTIALIASING, RenderingHints.VALUE_ANTIALIAS_ON);
        g.setColor(new Color(45, 85, 175));
        g.setStroke(new BasicStroke(1.3f));
        g.drawPolyline(new int[] {11, 3, 3, 15, 15, 11, 11, 15}, new int[] {1, 1, 17, 17, 5, 1, 5, 5}, 8);
        g.drawPolyline(new int[] {7, 5, 7}, new int[] {8, 10, 12}, 3);
        g.drawPolyline(new int[] {11, 13, 11}, new int[] {8, 10, 12}, 3);
        g.dispose();
      }
    });
    button.setMargin(new Insets(1, 4, 1, 4));
    button.setToolTipText("Show decompiled source");
    button.getAccessibleContext().setAccessibleName("Show decompiled source");
    return button;
  }
  static JScrollPane columns(DefaultTableModel model, int... widths) {
    JTable table = DebuggerView.table(model);
    table.setAutoResizeMode(JTable.AUTO_RESIZE_OFF);
    for (int i = 0; i < widths.length; i++)
      table.getColumnModel().getColumn(i).setPreferredWidth(widths[i]);
    return DebuggerView.scroll(table);
  }
  String key() {
    return snapshot == null ? "" : SymbolLookup.key(snapshot);
  }
  void select(JsonObject value) {
    String old = key();
    snapshot = value;
    if (!old.equals(key())) {
      if (javaTable.isEditing()) javaTable.getCellEditor().cancelCellEditing();
      nativeFrames.setRowCount(0);
      javaFrames.setRowCount(0);
      javaDetails.clear();
      status.setText("Next I/O on selected TID; separate from historical event");
      javaStatus.setText(
          "Java stack at syscall exit; requires a Java environment on the target TID");
    }
    capture.setEnabled(!busy && snapshot != null
        && Set.of("send", "receive").contains(DebuggerView.str(snapshot, "operation")));
  }
  void start() {
    if (busy || snapshot == null)
      return;
    JsonObject target = snapshot.deepCopy();
    String selected = key();
    busy = true;
    capture.setEnabled(false);
    status.setText("Waiting for next TCP " + DebuggerView.str(target, "operation") + " on TID "
        + DebuggerView.str(target, "tid") + "...");
    new SwingWorker<JsonObject, Void>() {
      protected JsonObject doInBackground() throws Exception {
        return FridaLookup.request(target, "capture", Collections.emptySet());
      }
      protected void done() {
        busy = false;
        select(snapshot);
        if (!selected.equals(key()))
          return;
        try {
          display(get());
        } catch (Exception e) {
          Throwable cause = e.getCause() == null ? e : e.getCause();
          status.setText("Capture unavailable: " + cause.getMessage());
          status.setToolTipText(cause.getMessage());
        }
      }
    }.execute();
  }
  void display(JsonObject event) {
    if (javaTable.isEditing()) javaTable.getCellEditor().cancelCellEditing();
    nativeFrames.setRowCount(0);
    javaFrames.setRowCount(0);
    javaDetails.clear();
    int index = 0;
    for (JsonElement e : event.getAsJsonArray("nativeFrames")) {
      JsonObject f = e.getAsJsonObject();
      nativeFrames.addRow(new Object[] {index++,
          DebuggerView.displayAddress(DebuggerView.str(f, "address")),
          nativeSymbol(f), DebuggerView.str(f, "module")});
    }
    JsonObject javaData = event.getAsJsonObject("java");
    index = 0;
    for (JsonElement e : javaData.getAsJsonArray("frames")) {
      JsonObject f = e.getAsJsonObject();
      javaDetails.add(f.deepCopy());
      javaFrames.addRow(new Object[] {index++,
          DebuggerView.str(f, "className") + "." + DebuggerView.str(f, "methodName"),
          DebuggerView.str(f, "fileName"), DebuggerView.str(f, "lineNumber"), "Show"});
    }
    javaStatus.setText(DebuggerView.str(javaData, "source") + " · "
        + (javaData.has("error") ? DebuggerView.str(javaData, "error")
                                 : DebuggerView.str(javaData, "method")));
    long result = event.get("result").getAsLong();
    String outcome =
        result < 0 ? "Error " + result : result + " " + DebuggerView.str(event, "resultUnit");
    String time = java.time.Instant.ofEpochMilli(event.get("capturedAtEpochMs").getAsLong())
                      .atZone(java.time.ZoneId.systemDefault())
                      .format(java.time.format.DateTimeFormatter.ofPattern("HH:mm:ss.SSS"));
    status.setText(time + " · TID " + DebuggerView.str(event, "tid") + " · " + outcome);
    status.setToolTipText(DebuggerView.str(event, "scope")
        + "; skipped readiness/restart/unsupported calls: " + DebuggerView.str(event, "skipped"));
  }
  static String nativeSymbol(JsonObject frame) {
    return DebuggerView.str(frame, "symbol").replaceFirst("(?i)^0x[0-9a-f]+(?:\\s+|$)", "");
  }
}
```

`plugin/src/JavaSourceView.java`:

```java
package ebpfproxy;

import com.google.gson.JsonObject;
import java.awt.*;
import java.awt.event.*;
import java.io.*;
import java.nio.charset.StandardCharsets;
import java.nio.file.*;
import java.util.*;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.concurrent.TimeUnit;
import java.util.regex.*;
import javax.swing.*;
import javax.swing.text.*;

final class JavaSourceView {
  static final Pattern TOKENS = Pattern.compile(
      "(?<comment>//[^\\n]*|/\\*[\\s\\S]*?\\*/)|(?<string>\"(?:\\\\.|[^\"\\\\])*\"|'(?:\\\\.|[^'\\\\])*')"
      + "|(?<keyword>\\b(?:abstract|assert|boolean|break|byte|case|catch|char|class|const|continue|default|do|double|else|enum|extends|final|finally|float|for|if|implements|import|instanceof|int|interface|long|native|new|null|package|private|protected|public|return|short|static|strictfp|super|switch|synchronized|this|throw|throws|transient|true|false|try|void|volatile|while|record|var|yield)\\b)"
      + "|(?<number>\\b(?:0x[0-9a-fA-F]+|[0-9]+(?:\\.[0-9]+)?)[lLfFdD]?\\b)"
      + "|(?<annotation>@[\\w.]+)|(?<method>\\b[A-Za-z_$][\\w$]*(?=\\s*\\())"
      + "|(?<type>\\b[A-Z][\\w$]*\\b)|(?<operator>[-+*/%=!<>?:&|^~]+)");

  static final class SourcePane extends JTextPane {
    public boolean getScrollableTracksViewportWidth() { return false; }
    public void setSize(Dimension size) {
      super.setSize(new Dimension(Math.max(size.width, getPreferredSize().width), size.height));
    }
  }

  static void show(Component parent, JsonObject frame) {
    if (AnalyzerSettings.jadxPath().isBlank()) {
      AnalyzerSettings.show(parent);
      if (AnalyzerSettings.jadxPath().isBlank()) return;
    }
    new Viewer(parent, frame).open();
  }
  static final class Viewer {
    final JsonObject frame;
    final JDialog dialog;
    final JTextPane code = new SourcePane();
    final JLabel status = new JLabel("Loading...");
    final JButton local = UiLayout.button("Open APK / DEX...");
    final JTextArea numbers = new JTextArea();
    SwingWorker<String, Void> worker;
    Viewer(Component parent, JsonObject frame) {
      this.frame = frame.deepCopy();
      dialog = new JDialog(SwingUtilities.getWindowAncestor(parent), "Java Source", Dialog.ModalityType.MODELESS);
      dialog.setDefaultCloseOperation(WindowConstants.DISPOSE_ON_CLOSE);
      JPanel panel = new JPanel(new BorderLayout(6, 6));
      JPanel heading = new JPanel(new BorderLayout());
      heading.add(new JLabel(DebuggerView.str(frame, "className") + "." + DebuggerView.str(frame, "methodName")
          + "  |  captured line " + DebuggerView.str(frame, "lineNumber")));
      heading.add(local, BorderLayout.EAST);
      panel.add(heading, BorderLayout.NORTH);
      code.setEditable(false);
      code.setFont(new Font(Font.MONOSPACED, Font.PLAIN, 13));
      numbers.setFont(code.getFont());
      numbers.setEditable(false);
      numbers.setForeground(Color.GRAY);
      numbers.setBackground(new Color(245, 245, 245));
      JScrollPane scroll = new JScrollPane(code);
      scroll.setHorizontalScrollBarPolicy(ScrollPaneConstants.HORIZONTAL_SCROLLBAR_ALWAYS);
      scroll.setRowHeaderView(numbers);
      panel.add(scroll);
      panel.add(status, BorderLayout.SOUTH);
      dialog.setContentPane(panel);
      dialog.setSize(1000, 720);
      dialog.setLocationRelativeTo(parent);
      local.addActionListener(e -> choose());
      dialog.addWindowListener(new WindowAdapter() {
        public void windowClosed(WindowEvent e) {
          if (worker != null) worker.cancel(true);
        }
      });
    }
    void open() {
      dialog.setVisible(true);
      if (remoteOrigin(DebuggerView.str(frame, "origin")).isEmpty()) {
        status.setText("No APK/DEX origin in this frame. Select its APK, DEX or JAR with Open APK / DEX...");
      } else load(null);
    }
    void choose() {
      JFileChooser picker = new JFileChooser();
      picker.setDialogTitle("Select the APK / DEX / JAR containing this class");
      if (picker.showOpenDialog(dialog) == JFileChooser.APPROVE_OPTION)
        load(picker.getSelectedFile().toPath());
    }
    void load(Path input) {
      if (worker != null && !worker.isDone()) return;
      local.setEnabled(false);
      status.setText(input == null ? "Reading APK/DEX from device and running jadx..." : "Running jadx...");
      String executable = AnalyzerSettings.jadxPath();
      worker = new SwingWorker<>() {
        protected String doInBackground() throws Exception { return decompile(executable, frame, input); }
        protected void done() {
          if (!dialog.isDisplayable()) return;
          local.setEnabled(true);
          try {
            String source = get();
            highlight(code, source);
            StringBuilder gutter = new StringBuilder();
            int lines = source.split("\n", -1).length;
            for (int i = 1; i <= lines; i++) gutter.append(i).append("  \n");
            numbers.setText(gutter.toString());
            int at = sourceOffset(source, frame);
            markMethod(code, at);
            code.setCaretPosition(Math.max(0, at));
            SwingUtilities.invokeLater(() -> {
              try { code.scrollRectToVisible(code.modelToView2D(Math.max(0, at)).getBounds()); }
              catch (BadLocationException ignored) { }
            });
            status.setText(at < 0 ? "Method declaration not found in decompiled source."
                : "Highlighted: method declaration. Gutter numbers refer to decompiled source; overloaded methods use captured debug lines when available.");
          } catch (Exception ex) {
            Throwable cause = ex.getCause() == null ? ex : ex.getCause();
            status.setText("Unable to show source. Use Open APK / DEX... or check Settings.");
            code.setText(cause.toString());
            numbers.setText("");
          }
        }
      };
      worker.execute();
    }
  }
  static String remoteOrigin(String origin) {
    int archive = origin.indexOf('!');
    if (archive >= 0) origin = origin.substring(0, archive);
    return origin.matches("/[A-Za-z0-9_./@~+=-]+\\.(apk|dex|jar)") ? origin : "";
  }
  static List<String> jadxCommand(String executable) throws IOException {
    Path path = Path.of(executable).toAbsolutePath();
    if (!Files.isRegularFile(path)) throw new IOException("jadx CLI not found: " + path);
    String java = Path.of(System.getProperty("java.home"), "bin",
        System.getProperty("os.name").startsWith("Windows") ? "java.exe" : "java").toString();
    if (path.toString().toLowerCase(Locale.ROOT).endsWith(".bat")) {
      // Use the distribution's Java entry point, avoiding cmd.exe argument expansion.
      Path lib = path.getParent().getParent().resolve("lib");
      if (!Files.isDirectory(lib)) throw new IOException("Select bin/jadx.bat from the full jadx CLI distribution (lib/ required)");
      return new ArrayList<>(List.of(java, "-cp", lib + File.separator + "*", "jadx.cli.JadxCLI"));
    }
    if (path.toString().endsWith(".jar")) return new ArrayList<>(List.of(java, "-jar", path.toString()));
    return new ArrayList<>(List.of(path.toString()));
  }
  static String decompile(String executable, JsonObject frame, Path local) throws Exception {
    String className = DebuggerView.str(frame, "className");
    if (!className.matches("[\\p{L}_$][\\p{L}\\p{N}_$]*(?:\\.[\\p{L}_$][\\p{L}\\p{N}_$]*)*"))
      throw new IOException("No supported Java class name in frame");
    Path temp = Files.createTempDirectory("ebpf-jadx-");
    try {
      if (local == null) {
        String remote = remoteOrigin(DebuggerView.str(frame, "origin"));
        if (remote.isEmpty()) throw new IOException("No APK/DEX path; choose a local file");
        local = temp.resolve("input" + remote.substring(remote.lastIndexOf('.')));
        run(List.of(System.getProperty("packetproxy.adb", "adb"), "-s",
            System.getProperty("packetproxy.adb.serial", "emulator-5554"), "pull", remote, local.toString()), temp, 60);
      }
      Path output = temp.resolve("source.java");
      List<String> command = jadxCommand(executable);
      command.addAll(List.of("--single-class", className, "--single-class-output", output.toString(),
          "--add-debug-lines", "--no-inline-anonymous", "--no-move-inner-classes", "-r", "-j", "2", local.toAbsolutePath().toString()));
      run(command, temp, 120);
      if (!Files.isRegularFile(output)) throw new IOException("Class not found in this APK/DEX. Try its split APK or framework JAR.");
      if (Files.size(output) > 1024 * 1024) throw new IOException("Source exceeds the 1 MiB viewer limit");
      return Files.readString(output);
    } finally {
      // Only this operation's newly created temporary directory is cleaned up.
      try (var paths = Files.walk(temp)) {
        for (Path path : paths.sorted(Comparator.reverseOrder()).toList()) Files.deleteIfExists(path);
      }
    }
  }
  static void run(List<String> args, Path directory, int seconds) throws Exception {
    Path log = directory.resolve("process.log");
    Process process = new ProcessBuilder(args).redirectErrorStream(true).redirectOutput(log.toFile()).start();
    try {
      if (!process.waitFor(seconds, TimeUnit.SECONDS)) throw new IOException("Command timed out: " + args.get(0));
      if (process.exitValue() != 0) {
        String message;
        try (InputStream input = Files.newInputStream(log)) {
          message = new String(input.readNBytes(8192), StandardCharsets.UTF_8);
        }
        throw new IOException(args.get(0) + " failed:\n" + message);
      }
    } finally {
      if (process.isAlive()) {
        process.descendants().forEach(ProcessHandle::destroyForcibly);
        process.destroyForcibly();
        process.waitFor(5, TimeUnit.SECONDS);
      }
    }
  }
  static int sourceOffset(String source, JsonObject frame) {
    // Mask comments and strings so calls or braces inside them cannot be declarations.
    StringBuilder masked = new StringBuilder(source);
    Matcher tokens = TOKENS.matcher(source);
    while (tokens.find()) if (tokens.group("comment") != null || tokens.group("string") != null)
      for (int i = tokens.start(); i < tokens.end(); i++)
        if (masked.charAt(i) != '\n' && masked.charAt(i) != '\r') masked.setCharAt(i, ' ');
    String method = DebuggerView.str(frame, "methodName");
    boolean constructor = method.equals("<init>");
    if (method.equals("<init>")) {
      String name = DebuggerView.str(frame, "className");
      method = name.substring(Math.max(name.lastIndexOf('.'), name.lastIndexOf('$')) + 1);
    }
    String modifiers = "(?:(?:public|protected|private|static|final|synchronized|native|abstract|strictfp|default)[ \\t]+)*";
    Matcher match = Pattern.compile("(?m)^[ \\t]*" + modifiers
        + (constructor ? "" : "(?:<[^>\\n]+>[ \\t]+)?[\\w.$<>\\[\\], ?]+[ \\t]+")
        + Pattern.quote(method) + "[ \\t]*\\(").matcher(masked);
    int first = -1, debugAt = -1;
    String line = DebuggerView.str(frame, "lineNumber");
    if (line.matches("[1-9][0-9]*")) {
      Matcher debug = Pattern.compile("/\\*\\s*" + line + "\\s*\\*/").matcher(source);
      if (debug.find()) debugAt = debug.start();
    }
    while (match.find()) {
      String declaration = masked.substring(match.start(), match.end());
      if (declaration.matches("(?s).*\\b(return|throw|new)\\b.*")) continue;
      int at = match.end(), depth = 1;
      while (at < masked.length() && depth > 0) {
        char c = masked.charAt(at++);
        if (c == '(') depth++; else if (c == ')') depth--;
      }
      while (at < masked.length() && masked.charAt(at) != '{' && masked.charAt(at) != ';') at++;
      if (at == masked.length()) continue;
      int start = match.start();
      if (first < 0) first = start;
      if (masked.charAt(at) == '{') {
        int end = at + 1;
        depth = 1;
        while (end < masked.length() && depth > 0) {
          char c = masked.charAt(end++);
          if (c == '{') depth++; else if (c == '}') depth--;
        }
        if (debugAt >= start && debugAt < end) return start;
      }
    }
    return first;
  }
  static void markMethod(JTextPane pane, int at) throws BadLocationException {
    DefaultHighlighter highlighter = new DefaultHighlighter();
    // Paint the row once, before any syntax-colored glyphs. A layered painter is
    // invoked for every styled run and would erase text painted by earlier runs.
    highlighter.setDrawsLayeredHighlights(false);
    pane.setHighlighter(highlighter);
    if (at < 0) return;
    int end = pane.getText().indexOf('\n', at);
    if (end < 0) end = pane.getDocument().getLength();
    pane.getHighlighter().addHighlight(at, end, new DefaultHighlighter.DefaultHighlightPainter(new Color(255, 240, 185)) {
      public void paint(Graphics g, int start, int finish, Shape bounds, JTextComponent component) {
        try {
          Rectangle row = component.modelToView2D(start).getBounds();
          row.x = 0;
          row.width = component.getWidth();
          g.setColor(getColor());
          g.fillRect(row.x, row.y, row.width, row.height);
        } catch (BadLocationException ignored) { }
      }
    });
  }
  static void highlight(JTextPane pane, String source) {
    pane.setText(source);
    StyledDocument doc = pane.getStyledDocument();
    SimpleAttributeSet normal = new SimpleAttributeSet();
    StyleConstants.setForeground(normal, new Color(35, 40, 50));
    doc.setCharacterAttributes(0, source.length(), normal, true);
    Matcher matcher = TOKENS.matcher(source);
    while (matcher.find()) {
      SimpleAttributeSet style = new SimpleAttributeSet();
      Color color = matcher.group("comment") != null ? new Color(100, 115, 100)
          : matcher.group("string") != null ? new Color(30, 125, 65)
          : matcher.group("keyword") != null ? new Color(125, 45, 155)
          : matcher.group("annotation") != null ? new Color(150, 100, 15)
          : matcher.group("method") != null ? new Color(35, 85, 175)
          : matcher.group("type") != null ? new Color(20, 125, 135)
          : matcher.group("operator") != null ? new Color(150, 65, 45) : new Color(30, 90, 180);
      StyleConstants.setForeground(style, color);
      StyleConstants.setBold(style, matcher.group("keyword") != null);
      doc.setCharacterAttributes(matcher.start(), matcher.end() - matcher.start(), style, true);
    }
  }
}

```

```java
/** Reverse lookup of retained I/O stacks, not a trace of all function calls. */
final class ProcessBrowser extends JPanel {
  static final class Entry {
    final String kind, owner, method, offset;
    final Set<String> events = new LinkedHashSet<>();
    JsonObject frame;
    Entry(String kind, String owner, String method) { this(kind, owner, method, ""); }
    Entry(String kind, String owner, String method, String offset) { this.kind = kind; this.owner = owner; this.method = method; this.offset = offset; }
    String key() { return kind + "\n" + owner + "\n" + method + (offset.isEmpty() ? "" : "\n" + offset); }
    public String toString() {
      String label = method.isEmpty() ? owner.substring((kind.equals("module") ? owner.lastIndexOf('/') : owner.lastIndexOf('.')) + 1) : method;
      return offset.isEmpty() ? label : offset;
    }
  }
  final JTree libraries = new JTree(new DefaultMutableTreeNode("Libraries"));

  ProcessBrowser(Consumer<JsonObject> request, Consumer<String> navigate) {

      public Component getTableCellRendererComponent(JTable t, Object value, boolean selected, boolean focus, int row, int col) {
        super.getTableCellRendererComponent(t, value, selected, focus, row, col);
        Color bg = t.getBackground(); boolean dark = bg.getRed() + bg.getGreen() + bg.getBlue() < 384;
        setBackground(selected ? t.getSelectionBackground() : bg);
        setForeground(selected ? t.getSelectionForeground() : t.getForeground());
        setBorder(BorderFactory.createEmptyBorder(0, 8, 0, 8));
        setToolTipText(value == null ? null : value.toString());

      }
    });
    table.setAutoResizeMode(JTable.AUTO_RESIZE_OFF);
    int[] widths = {100, 55, 80, 120, 145, 145};
    for (int i = 0; i < widths.length; i++) table.getColumnModel().getColumn(i).setPreferredWidth(widths[i]);
    table.getTableHeader().setToolTipText("Frame: zero-based matching stack indices. I/O Result: syscall return value in bytes/messages, or a negative errno.");
    JPanel actions = new JPanel(new FlowLayout(FlowLayout.LEFT, 6, 2));

    resultHeader.add(new JLabel("I/O Result: transferred bytes/messages, or an OS error code."), BorderLayout.SOUTH);

    results.add(new JLabel("Matches are captured I/O stacks. Java overloads are grouped by name."), BorderLayout.SOUTH);

        if (rebuilding) return;
        Object node = tree.getLastSelectedPathComponent();

        updateMatches();

      tree.addTreeWillExpandListener(new TreeWillExpandListener() {
        public void treeWillExpand(TreeExpansionEvent e) {
          Object value = ((DefaultMutableTreeNode) e.getPath().getLastPathComponent()).getUserObject();
          if (!rebuilding && !observedOnly.isSelected() && value instanceof Entry entry && entry.method.isEmpty()) requestMembers(entry);
        }
        public void treeWillCollapse(TreeExpansionEvent e) { }
      });
    }
    trees.addChangeListener(e -> {
      JTree tree = trees.getSelectedIndex() == 0 ? libraries : javaTree;
      Object node = tree.getLastSelectedPathComponent();
      selected = node instanceof DefaultMutableTreeNode n && n.getUserObject() instanceof Entry value ? value : null;
      updateMatches();
    });
    load.addActionListener(e -> { if (selected != null) requestMembers(selected); });
    source.addActionListener(e -> {
      if (selected == null || !selected.kind.equals("class")) return;
      Entry owner = index.get(new Entry(selected.kind, selected.owner, "").key());
      JsonObject captured = selected.frame != null ? selected.frame : owner == null ? null : owner.frame;
      JsonObject frame = captured == null ? new JsonObject() : captured.deepCopy();
      frame.addProperty("className", selected.owner);
      frame.addProperty("methodName", selected.method);
      JavaSourceView.show(this, frame);
    });
    openEvent.addActionListener(e -> openSelected());
    table.addMouseListener(new MouseAdapter() { public void mouseClicked(MouseEvent e) { if (e.getClickCount() == 2) openSelected(); } });
    table.getSelectionModel().addListSelectionListener(e -> openEvent.setEnabled(table.getSelectedRow() >= 0));
    refresh = new javax.swing.Timer(150, e -> rebuild());
    refresh.setRepeats(false);
    search.getDocument().addDocumentListener(new DocumentListener() {
      public void insertUpdate(DocumentEvent e) { refresh.restart(); }
      public void removeUpdate(DocumentEvent e) { refresh.restart(); }
      public void changedUpdate(DocumentEvent e) { refresh.restart(); }
    });
    direction.addActionListener(e -> rebuild());
    observedOnly.addActionListener(e -> rebuild());
    firstFrame.addChangeListener(e -> rebuild());
    lastFrame.addChangeListener(e -> rebuild());
    updateMatches();
  }
  void reset(long pid) {
    refresh.stop(); cleared.clear(); catalogCleared = false; this.pid = pid; catalog = new JsonObject(); members.clear(); pending.clear(); events.clear(); expansion.clear(); knownPaths.clear(); selected = null;
    status.setText(pid == 0 ? "Select a process and start Frida in Settings" : "PID " + pid + " / waiting for Frida catalog...");
    rebuild();
  }
  void clear() {
    refresh.stop(); cleared.addAll(events.keySet());
    events.clear(); catalog = new JsonObject(); members.clear(); pending.clear();
    expansion.clear(); knownPaths.clear(); selected = null; catalogCleared = true;
    status.setText("Cleared / waiting for new I/O events. Refresh catalog to restore loaded libraries/classes.");
    rebuild();
  }
  void accept(JsonObject value) {
    String type = DebuggerView.str(value, "type");
    if (type.equals("catalog")) {
      if (catalogCleared) return;
      catalog = value.deepCopy();
      members.clear(); pending.clear();
      status.setText("PID " + pid + " / loaded libraries and Java classes"
          + (value.has("truncated") && value.get("truncated").getAsBoolean() ? " / catalog limit reached" : "")
          + " / " + DebuggerView.str(value, "javaError"));
    } else if (type.equals("members")) {
      String key = DebuggerView.str(value, "kind") + "\n" + DebuggerView.str(value, "name");
      if (catalogCleared && !pending.contains(key)) return;
      pending.remove(key);
      List<String> names = new ArrayList<>();
      for (JsonElement member : value.getAsJsonArray("members")) names.add(member.getAsString());
      members.put(key, names);
      status.setText("PID " + pid + " / " + names.size() + " members loaded"
          + (value.has("truncated") && value.get("truncated").getAsBoolean() ? " (limited)" : ""));
    } else if (type.equals("catalogError")) {
      pending.remove(DebuggerView.str(value, "kind") + "\n" + DebuggerView.str(value, "name"));
      status.setText("Catalog: " + DebuggerView.str(value, "error"));
    }
    rebuild();
  }
  void setEvents(List<JsonObject> values) {
    events.clear();
    Set<String> retained = new HashSet<>();
    for (JsonObject event : values) {
      String id = DebuggerView.str(event, "eventId"); retained.add(id);
      if (!cleared.contains(id)) events.put(id, event);
    }
    cleared.retainAll(retained);
    if (!refresh.isRunning()) refresh.start();
  }
  Entry entry(String kind, String owner, String method) {
    Entry value = new Entry(kind, owner, method);
    return index.computeIfAbsent(value.key(), ignored -> value);
  }
  static String nativeOwner(JsonObject frame) {
    String module = DebuggerView.str(frame, "module");
    if (!module.isEmpty()) return module;
    String symbol = DebuggerView.str(frame, "symbol").replaceFirst("(?i)^0x[0-9a-f]+\\s*", "");
    int bang = symbol.indexOf('!');
    return bang >= 0 ? symbol.substring(0, bang) : "[unknown module]";
  }
  static String nativeName(JsonObject frame) {
    String name = DebuggerView.str(frame, "symbolName");
    if (!name.isEmpty()) return stripOffset(name);
    String symbol = DebuggerView.str(frame, "symbol");
    int bang = symbol.indexOf('!');
    return bang >= 0 ? stripOffset(symbol.substring(bang + 1)) : DebuggerView.str(frame, "address");
  }
  static String stripOffset(String name) { return name.replaceFirst("(?i)\\s*\\+0x[0-9a-f]+$", ""); }
  void observe(String kind, String owner, String method, JsonObject frame, String id) {
    Entry parent = entry(kind, owner, ""), child = entry(kind, owner, method);
    parent.events.add(id); child.events.add(id);
    if (kind.equals("module")) {
      Entry point = new Entry(kind, owner, method, nativeOffset(frame));
      point = index.computeIfAbsent(point.key(), ignored -> new Entry(kind, owner, method, nativeOffset(frame)));
      point.events.add(id); point.frame = frame;
    }
    if (parent.frame == null || !DebuggerView.str(frame, "origin").isEmpty()) parent.frame = frame;
    if (child.frame == null || !DebuggerView.str(frame, "origin").isEmpty()) child.frame = frame;
  }
  void rebuild() {
    String selection = selected == null ? "" : selected.key();
    index.clear();
    if (catalog.has("modules")) for (JsonElement module : catalog.getAsJsonArray("modules")) entry("module", DebuggerView.str(module.getAsJsonObject(), "path"), "");
    if (catalog.has("classes")) for (JsonElement klass : catalog.getAsJsonArray("classes")) entry("class", klass.getAsString(), "");
    for (var list : members.entrySet()) {
      String[] key = list.getKey().split("\n", 2);
      for (String name : list.getValue()) entry(key[0], key[1], key[0].equals("class") ? name.replaceFirst("\\(.*$", "") : name);
    }
    for (var item : events.entrySet()) {
      JsonObject event = item.getValue();
      if (!includesOperation(event)) continue;
      int depth = 0;
      if (event.has("nativeFrames")) for (JsonElement element : event.getAsJsonArray("nativeFrames")) {
        if (!includesFrame(depth++)) continue;
        JsonObject frame = element.getAsJsonObject();
        observe("module", nativeOwner(frame), nativeName(frame), frame, item.getKey());
      }
      depth = 0;
      if (event.has("java") && event.getAsJsonObject("java").has("frames")) for (JsonElement element : event.getAsJsonObject("java").getAsJsonArray("frames")) {
        if (!includesFrame(depth++)) continue;
        JsonObject frame = element.getAsJsonObject();
        observe("class", DebuggerView.str(frame, "className"), DebuggerView.str(frame, "methodName"), frame, item.getKey());
      }
    }
    rebuilding = true;
    try { buildTree(libraries, "module", "Libraries"); buildTree(javaTree, "class", "Java Sources"); }
    finally { rebuilding = false; }
    libraryTable.reload(); javaSourceTable.reload();
    boolean visible = false;
    for (JTree tree : new JTree[] {libraries, javaTree}) {
      Object node = tree.getLastSelectedPathComponent();
      if (node instanceof DefaultMutableTreeNode n && n.getUserObject() instanceof Entry entry && entry.key().equals(selection)) visible = true;
    }
    selected = visible ? index.get(selection) : null;
    updateMatches();
  }
  void buildTree(JTree tree, String kind, String title) {
    Set<String> expanded = expansion.computeIfAbsent(tree, ignored -> new HashSet<>());
    if (tree.getModel().getChildCount(tree.getModel().getRoot()) == 0) expanded.add("/" + title);
    String selectedKey = tree.getSelectionPath() == null ? "" : pathKey(tree.getSelectionPath());
    DefaultMutableTreeNode root = new DefaultMutableTreeNode(title);
    Map<String, DefaultMutableTreeNode> owners = new TreeMap<>(), packages = new HashMap<>(), functions = new HashMap<>();
    String filter = search.getText().trim().toLowerCase(Locale.ROOT);
    List<Entry> ordered = new ArrayList<>(index.values());
    ordered.sort((a, b) -> {
      int order = (a.kind + "\n" + a.owner + "\n" + a.method).compareTo(b.kind + "\n" + b.owner + "\n" + b.method);
      if (order != 0) return order;
      if (a.offset.startsWith("+0x") && b.offset.startsWith("+0x"))
        return new java.math.BigInteger(a.offset.substring(3), 16).compareTo(new java.math.BigInteger(b.offset.substring(3), 16));
      return a.offset.compareTo(b.offset);
    });
    for (Entry entry : ordered) {
      if (!entry.kind.equals(kind) || (observedOnly.isSelected() && entry.events.isEmpty())) continue;
      if (!(entry.owner + " " + entry.method + " " + entry.offset).toLowerCase(Locale.ROOT).contains(filter)) continue;
      DefaultMutableTreeNode parent = owners.get(entry.owner);
      if (parent == null) {
        DefaultMutableTreeNode group = root;
        if (kind.equals("class") && entry.owner.contains(".")) {
          String[] parts = entry.owner.substring(0, entry.owner.lastIndexOf('.')).split("\\.");
          String key = "";
          for (String part : parts) {
            key += "." + part;
            DefaultMutableTreeNode node = packages.get(key);
            if (node == null) { node = new DefaultMutableTreeNode(part); packages.put(key, node); group.add(node); }
            group = node;
          }
        }
        parent = new DefaultMutableTreeNode(entry(kind, entry.owner, ""));
        owners.put(entry.owner, parent); group.add(parent);
      }
      if (!entry.method.isEmpty()) {
        String functionKey = new Entry(kind, entry.owner, entry.method).key();
        DefaultMutableTreeNode function = functions.get(functionKey);
        if (function == null) {
          function = new DefaultMutableTreeNode(entry(kind, entry.owner, entry.method));
          functions.put(functionKey, function); parent.add(function);
        }
        if (!entry.offset.isEmpty()) function.add(new DefaultMutableTreeNode(entry));
      }
    }
    for (var item : owners.entrySet()) if (!observedOnly.isSelected() && item.getValue().getChildCount() == 0 && !members.containsKey(kind + "\n" + item.getKey()))
      item.getValue().add(new DefaultMutableTreeNode("Expand to load " + (kind.equals("module") ? "exported functions" : "methods")));
    Set<String> known = knownPaths.computeIfAbsent(tree, ignored -> new HashSet<>());
    HierarchyTable hierarchy = tree == libraries ? libraryTable : javaSourceTable;
    Enumeration<?> defaults = root.depthFirstEnumeration();
    while (defaults.hasMoreElements()) {
      DefaultMutableTreeNode node = (DefaultMutableTreeNode) defaults.nextElement();
      String key = pathKey(new TreePath(node.getPath()));
      if (!node.isLeaf() && known.add(key) && (node.isRoot() || hierarchy.autoExpand.getSelectedIndex() == 0)) expanded.add(key);
    }
    tree.setModel(new DefaultTreeModel(root));

    Enumeration<?> nodes = root.depthFirstEnumeration();
    while (nodes.hasMoreElements()) {
      DefaultMutableTreeNode node = (DefaultMutableTreeNode) nodes.nextElement();
      TreePath path = new TreePath(node.getPath());
      String key = pathKey(path);
      if (expanded.contains(key)) tree.expandPath(path);
    }
    // Expanding a hidden child opens its ancestors; restore each collapsed ancestor afterwards.
    nodes = root.depthFirstEnumeration();
    while (nodes.hasMoreElements()) {
      DefaultMutableTreeNode node = (DefaultMutableTreeNode) nodes.nextElement();
      TreePath path = new TreePath(node.getPath());
      if (!expanded.contains(pathKey(path))) tree.collapsePath(path);
      if (selectedKey.equals(pathKey(path))) tree.setSelectionPath(path);
    }
  }
  JPanel hierarchyPanel(HierarchyTable table) {
    JPanel panel = new JPanel(new BorderLayout());
    JPanel controls = new JPanel(new FlowLayout(FlowLayout.LEFT, 4, 3));
    controls.add(new JLabel("Automatic:")); controls.add(table.autoExpand);
    controls.add(new JLabel("Click arrow to expand; right-click for more"));
    panel.add(UiLayout.horizontal(controls), BorderLayout.NORTH);
    panel.add(hierarchyScroll(table));
    return panel;
  }
  static void styleTable(JTable table) {
    table.setRowHeight(27);
    table.setShowVerticalLines(false);
    table.setShowHorizontalLines(false);
    table.setIntercellSpacing(new Dimension(0, 0));
    table.setFillsViewportHeight(true);
  }
  static JScrollPane hierarchyScroll(JTable table) {
    JScrollPane pane = new JScrollPane(table);
    pane.setColumnHeaderView(table.getTableHeader());
    return pane;
  }
  static String nativeOffset(JsonObject frame) {
    String offset = DebuggerView.str(frame, "functionOffset");
    if (offset.matches("(?i)0x[0-9a-f]+")) return "+0x" + new java.math.BigInteger(offset.substring(2), 16).toString(16);
    java.util.regex.Matcher match = java.util.regex.Pattern.compile("\\+(0x[0-9a-fA-F]+)$").matcher(DebuggerView.str(frame, "symbolName"));
    if (!match.find()) match = java.util.regex.Pattern.compile("\\+(0x[0-9a-fA-F]+)$").matcher(DebuggerView.str(frame, "symbol"));
    else return "+0x" + new java.math.BigInteger(match.group(1).substring(2), 16).toString(16);
    if (match.find()) return "+0x" + new java.math.BigInteger(match.group(1).substring(2), 16).toString(16);
    String address = DebuggerView.str(frame, "address");
    return address.isEmpty() ? "Offset unavailable" : "PC " + address + " (offset unavailable)";
  }
  static String nodeType(DefaultMutableTreeNode node, boolean javaTree) {
    if (node.getUserObject() instanceof Entry entry) {
      if (!entry.offset.isEmpty()) return "Offset";
      if (!entry.method.isEmpty()) return entry.kind.equals("class") ? "Method" : "Function";
      return entry.kind.equals("class") ? "Class" : "Module";
    }
    if (node.isRoot()) return "Catalog";
    return javaTree && !node.isLeaf() ? "Package" : "Info";
  }
  /** Vector icons stay crisp at display scaling and inherit the selected row's contrast. */
  static final class NodeIcon implements Icon {
    final String type;
    final boolean branch, expanded;
    NodeIcon(String type, boolean branch, boolean expanded) { this.type = type; this.branch = branch; this.expanded = expanded; }
    public int getIconWidth() { return 36; }
    public int getIconHeight() { return 18; }
    public void paintIcon(Component c, Graphics graphics, int x, int y) {
      Graphics2D g = (Graphics2D) graphics.create();
      g.translate(x, y); g.setColor(c.getForeground());
      g.setRenderingHint(RenderingHints.KEY_ANTIALIASING, RenderingHints.VALUE_ANTIALIAS_ON);
      g.setStroke(new BasicStroke(1.7f, BasicStroke.CAP_ROUND, BasicStroke.JOIN_ROUND));
      if (branch) {
        if (expanded) g.drawPolyline(new int[] {4, 8, 12}, new int[] {6, 10, 6}, 3);
        else g.drawPolyline(new int[] {6, 10, 6}, new int[] {4, 8, 12}, 3);
      }
      g.translate(18, 0);
      switch (type) {
        case "Package":
          java.awt.geom.Path2D folder = new java.awt.geom.Path2D.Float();
          folder.moveTo(1, 5); folder.quadTo(1, 3, 3, 3); folder.lineTo(6, 3);
          folder.lineTo(8, 5); folder.lineTo(14, 5); folder.quadTo(16, 5, 16, 7);
          folder.lineTo(16, 13); folder.quadTo(16, 15, 14, 15); folder.lineTo(3, 15);
          folder.quadTo(1, 15, 1, 13); folder.closePath(); g.fill(folder); break;
        case "Class":
          g.drawRoundRect(2, 2, 13, 14, 3, 3);
          g.drawLine(2, 7, 15, 7); g.drawLine(6, 10, 11, 10); g.drawLine(6, 13, 10, 13); break;
        case "Module":
        case "Catalog":
          for (int row = 0; row < 2; row++) for (int col = 0; col < 2; col++)
            g.fillRoundRect(2 + col * 8, 2 + row * 8, 6, 6, 2, 2);
          break;
        case "Function":
          g.drawPolyline(new int[] {5, 1, 5}, new int[] {4, 9, 14}, 3);
          g.drawPolyline(new int[] {12, 16, 12}, new int[] {4, 9, 14}, 3);
          g.drawLine(10, 3, 7, 15); break;
        case "Method":
          g.drawLine(2, 4, 2, 10); g.drawLine(2, 10, 14, 10);
          g.drawPolyline(new int[] {10, 14, 10}, new int[] {6, 10, 14}, 3); break;
        case "Offset":
          g.fillOval(2, 7, 4, 4); g.drawLine(9, 9, 16, 9); break;
        default:
          g.drawOval(2, 2, 14, 14); g.drawLine(9, 8, 9, 12); g.fillOval(8, 5, 2, 2);
      }
      g.dispose();
    }
  }
  /** A table of visible tree paths; the backing tree owns expansion and selection state. */
  final class HierarchyTable extends JTable {
    final JTree tree;
    final JComboBox<String> autoExpand = new JComboBox<>(new String[] {"Expand", "Collapse"});
    final JPopupMenu menu = new JPopupMenu();
    boolean syncing;
    HierarchyTable(JTree tree) {
      this.tree = tree;
      autoExpand.setSelectedIndex(1);
      autoExpand.setToolTipText("Default for new items. Changing this reapplies the setting to the whole hierarchy; right-click overrides individual branches.");
      autoExpand.addActionListener(e -> {
        tree.clearSelection(); selected = null;
        expansion.remove(tree); knownPaths.remove(tree); rebuild();
      });
      for (String action : new String[] {"Expand", "Collapse", "Expand subtree", "Collapse subtree"}) {
        JMenuItem item = new JMenuItem(action);
        item.addActionListener(e -> {
          TreePath path = tree.getSelectionPath(); if (path == null) return;
          if (action.endsWith("subtree")) branch(action.startsWith("Expand"));
          else if (action.equals("Expand")) tree.expandPath(path);
          else tree.collapsePath(path);
        });
        menu.add(item);
      }
      setModel(new javax.swing.table.AbstractTableModel() {
        public int getRowCount() { return tree.getRowCount(); }
        public int getColumnCount() { return 1; }
        public String getColumnName(int col) { return "Name"; }
        public Class<?> getColumnClass(int col) { return Object.class; }
        public Object getValueAt(int row, int col) {
          DefaultMutableTreeNode node = (DefaultMutableTreeNode) tree.getPathForRow(row).getLastPathComponent();
          return node.getUserObject();
        }
      });
      styleTable(this); setSelectionMode(ListSelectionModel.SINGLE_SELECTION);
      setAutoResizeMode(JTable.AUTO_RESIZE_LAST_COLUMN);
      getColumnModel().getColumn(0).setPreferredWidth(360);

      getTableHeader().setReorderingAllowed(false);
      getTableHeader().setToolTipText("Right-click an item to expand or collapse it or its subtree");
      getColumnModel().getColumn(0).setCellRenderer(new javax.swing.table.DefaultTableCellRenderer() {
        public Component getTableCellRendererComponent(JTable table, Object value, boolean selected, boolean focus, int row, int col) {
          super.getTableCellRendererComponent(table, value, selected, focus, row, col);
          TreePath path = tree.getPathForRow(row);
          DefaultMutableTreeNode node = (DefaultMutableTreeNode) path.getLastPathComponent();
          Color bg = table.getBackground();
          boolean dark = bg.getRed() + bg.getGreen() + bg.getBlue() < 384;
          setBackground(selected ? table.getSelectionBackground() : bg);
          Color color = value instanceof Entry entry
              ? !entry.offset.isEmpty() ? (dark ? new Color(230, 183, 115) : new Color(146, 88, 26))
              : entry.method.isEmpty() ? (dark ? new Color(158, 180, 242) : new Color(61, 82, 143))
              : (dark ? new Color(115, 207, 177) : new Color(29, 118, 91))
              : nodeType(node, tree == javaTree).equals("Package") ? (dark ? new Color(221, 185, 115) : new Color(150, 107, 40))
              : table.getForeground();
          setForeground(selected ? table.getSelectionForeground() : color);
          setFont(table.getFont().deriveFont(value instanceof Entry entry && entry.method.isEmpty() ? Font.BOLD : Font.PLAIN));
          setBorder(BorderFactory.createEmptyBorder(0, 5 + (path.getPathCount() - 1) * 18, 0, 4));
          String type = nodeType(node, tree == javaTree);
          setIcon(new NodeIcon(type, !node.isLeaf(), tree.isExpanded(path)));
          setIconTextGap(5);
          setText(value.toString());
          setToolTipText(type + ": " + (value instanceof Entry entry ? entry.owner + (entry.method.isEmpty() ? "" : "!" + entry.method) + entry.offset : value.toString()));
          getAccessibleContext().setAccessibleDescription(type + ": " + value);
          return this;
        }
      });
      getSelectionModel().addListSelectionListener(e -> {
        if (!syncing && !e.getValueIsAdjusting()) tree.setSelectionRow(getSelectedRow());
      });
      tree.addTreeSelectionListener(e -> syncSelection());
      tree.addTreeExpansionListener(new TreeExpansionListener() {
        public void treeExpanded(TreeExpansionEvent e) { if (!rebuilding) expansion.computeIfAbsent(tree, ignored -> new HashSet<>()).add(pathKey(e.getPath())); if (!rebuilding) reload(); }
        public void treeCollapsed(TreeExpansionEvent e) { if (!rebuilding) expansion.computeIfAbsent(tree, ignored -> new HashSet<>()).remove(pathKey(e.getPath())); if (!rebuilding) reload(); }
      });
      addMouseListener(new MouseAdapter() {
        public void mouseClicked(MouseEvent e) {
          if (!SwingUtilities.isLeftMouseButton(e)) return;
          int row = rowAtPoint(e.getPoint());
          if (row < 0 || columnAtPoint(e.getPoint()) != 0) return;
          TreePath path = tree.getPathForRow(row);
          int handle = 5 + (path.getPathCount() - 1) * 18;
          boolean disclosure = e.getX() >= handle && e.getX() < handle + 18;
          if ((disclosure && e.getClickCount() == 1) || (!disclosure && e.getClickCount() == 2)) toggle(path);
        }
        public void mousePressed(MouseEvent e) { popup(e); }
        public void mouseReleased(MouseEvent e) { popup(e); }
        void popup(MouseEvent e) {
          if (!e.isPopupTrigger()) return;
          int row = rowAtPoint(e.getPoint());
          if (row < 0) return;
          setRowSelectionInterval(row, row);
          boolean branch = !((DefaultMutableTreeNode) tree.getPathForRow(row).getLastPathComponent()).isLeaf();
          for (Component item : menu.getComponents()) item.setEnabled(branch);
          menu.show(HierarchyTable.this, e.getX(), e.getY());
        }
      });
      getInputMap().put(KeyStroke.getKeyStroke("shift F10"), "popup");
      getActionMap().put("popup", new AbstractAction() {
        public void actionPerformed(ActionEvent e) {
          int row = getSelectedRow(); if (row < 0) return;
          boolean branch = !((DefaultMutableTreeNode) tree.getPathForRow(row).getLastPathComponent()).isLeaf();
          for (Component item : menu.getComponents()) item.setEnabled(branch);
          Rectangle cell = getCellRect(row, 0, true); menu.show(HierarchyTable.this, cell.x + 20, cell.y + cell.height);
        }
      });
      for (String key : new String[] {"LEFT", "RIGHT"}) {
        getInputMap().put(KeyStroke.getKeyStroke(key), key);
        getActionMap().put(key, new AbstractAction() {
          public void actionPerformed(ActionEvent e) {
            TreePath path = tree.getSelectionPath(); if (path == null) return;
            if (key.equals("RIGHT")) tree.expandPath(path);
            else if (tree.isExpanded(path)) tree.collapsePath(path);
            else tree.setSelectionPath(path.getParentPath());
          }
        });
      }
    }
    void branch(boolean expand) {
      TreePath path = tree.getSelectionPath();
      if (path == null) path = new TreePath(((DefaultMutableTreeNode) tree.getModel().getRoot()).getPath());
      DefaultMutableTreeNode root = (DefaultMutableTreeNode) path.getLastPathComponent();
      Set<String> state = expansion.computeIfAbsent(tree, ignored -> new HashSet<>());
      if (expand) for (TreePath parent = path.getParentPath(); parent != null; parent = parent.getParentPath()) state.add(pathKey(parent));
      // Bulk expansion only exposes existing nodes; it never fetches every class's members.
      rebuilding = true;
      try {
        Enumeration<?> nodes = root.depthFirstEnumeration();
        while (nodes.hasMoreElements()) {
          DefaultMutableTreeNode node = (DefaultMutableTreeNode) nodes.nextElement();
          TreePath child = new TreePath(node.getPath());
          if (expand) { if (!node.isLeaf()) state.add(pathKey(child)); tree.expandPath(child); }
          else { state.remove(pathKey(child)); tree.collapsePath(child); }
        }
      } finally { rebuilding = false; }
      tree.setSelectionPath(path); reload();
    }
    void toggle(TreePath path) { if (tree.isExpanded(path)) tree.collapsePath(path); else tree.expandPath(path); }
    void syncSelection() {
      syncing = true;
      try { int row = tree.getSelectionPath() == null ? -1 : tree.getRowForPath(tree.getSelectionPath()); if (row < 0) clearSelection(); else setRowSelectionInterval(row, row); }
      finally { syncing = false; }
    }
    void reload() {
      syncing = true;
      try { ((javax.swing.table.AbstractTableModel) getModel()).fireTableDataChanged(); }
      finally { syncing = false; }
      syncSelection();
    }
  }
  static String pathKey(TreePath path) {
    StringBuilder key = new StringBuilder();
    for (Object part : path.getPath()) {
      Object value = ((DefaultMutableTreeNode) part).getUserObject();
      key.append('/').append(value instanceof Entry entry ? entry.key() : value);
    }
    return key.toString();
  }
  boolean includesOperation(JsonObject event) {
    String operation = DebuggerView.str(event, "operation");
    return direction.getSelectedIndex() == 0 || operation.equals(direction.getSelectedIndex() == 1 ? "send" : "receive");
  }
  boolean includesFrame(int frame) {
    int start = (Integer) firstFrame.getValue(), count = (Integer) lastFrame.getValue();
    return frame >= start && (count == 0 || frame - start < count);
  }
  void requestMembers(Entry entry) {
    String key = entry.kind + "\n" + entry.owner;
    if (members.containsKey(key) || !pending.add(key)) return;
    JsonObject command = new JsonObject(); command.addProperty("action", "members");
    command.addProperty("kind", entry.kind); command.addProperty("name", entry.owner);
    request.accept(command);
  }
  void updateMatches() {
    matches.setRowCount(0); matchingIds.clear();
    load.setEnabled(selected != null); source.setEnabled(selected != null && selected.kind.equals("class"));
    openEvent.setEnabled(false);
    if (selected == null) return;
    for (String id : selected.events) {
      JsonObject event = events.get(id);
      if (event == null) continue;
      matchingIds.add(id);
      String time = event.has("capturedAtEpochMs") ? java.time.Instant.ofEpochMilli(event.get("capturedAtEpochMs").getAsLong()).atZone(java.time.ZoneId.systemDefault()).toLocalTime().toString() : "";
      matches.addRow(new Object[] {time, DebuggerView.str(event, "tid"), DebuggerView.str(event, "operation"), matchingFrames(event), DebuggerView.str(event, "syscallPc"), ioResult(event)});
    }
  }
  String matchingFrames(JsonObject event) {
    boolean nativeStack = selected.kind.equals("module");
    JsonArray frames = nativeStack ? event.getAsJsonArray("nativeFrames")
        : event.has("java") ? event.getAsJsonObject("java").getAsJsonArray("frames") : null;
    if (frames == null) return "";
    List<String> indices = new ArrayList<>();
    for (int i = 0; i < frames.size(); i++) {
      if (!includesFrame(i)) continue;
      JsonObject frame = frames.get(i).getAsJsonObject();
      String owner = nativeStack ? nativeOwner(frame) : DebuggerView.str(frame, "className");
      String method = nativeStack ? nativeName(frame) : DebuggerView.str(frame, "methodName");
      if (selected.owner.equals(owner) && (selected.method.isEmpty() || selected.method.equals(method))
          && (selected.offset.isEmpty() || selected.offset.equals(nativeOffset(frame)))) indices.add("#" + i);
    }
    return (nativeStack ? "Native " : "Java ") + String.join(", ", indices);
  }
  static String ioResult(JsonObject event) {
    String value = DebuggerView.str(event, "result");
    if (value.isEmpty()) return "Not captured";
    try {
      long result = Long.parseLong(value);
      if (result < 0) return "Error (errno " + -result + ")";
      String unit = DebuggerView.str(event, "resultUnit");
      return value + (unit.isEmpty() ? " (return value)" : " " + unit);
    } catch (NumberFormatException ignored) { return value; }
  }
  void openSelected() {
    int row = table.getSelectedRow();
    if (row >= 0) navigate.accept(matchingIds.get(table.convertRowIndexToModel(row)));
  }
}
```

`plugin/src/SymbolLookup.java`:

```java
package ebpfproxy;

import com.google.gson.*;
import java.nio.charset.StandardCharsets;
import java.util.*;
import java.util.concurrent.*;

final class SymbolLookup {
  // Only immutable per-event results, bounded across both debugger views.
  private static final Map<String, JsonObject> cache = new LinkedHashMap<>(32, .75f, true) {
    protected boolean removeEldestEntry(Map.Entry<String, JsonObject> e) {
      return size() > 16;
    }
  };
  static synchronized JsonObject get(String key) {
    return cache.get(key);
  }
  static synchronized void put(String key, JsonObject value) {
    cache.put(key, value);
  }
  static String key(JsonObject snapshot) {
    return snapshot.get("connectionId").getAsString() + "/" + snapshot.get("pid").getAsString()
        + "/" + snapshot.get("capturedAtBootNs").getAsString();
  }
  static JsonObject resolve(JsonObject snapshot, Set<String> addresses) throws Exception {
    return resolve(snapshot, addresses,
        snapshot.has("syscallPc") ? snapshot.get("syscallPc").getAsString() : "");
  }
  static JsonObject resolve(JsonObject snapshot, Set<String> addresses, String disassemble)
      throws Exception {
    return resolve(snapshot, addresses, disassemble, null);
  }
  static JsonObject resolve(JsonObject snapshot, Set<String> addresses, String disassemble,
      JsonObject previous) throws Exception {
    return resolve(snapshot, addresses, disassemble, previous, false);
  }
  static JsonObject resolve(JsonObject snapshot, Set<String> addresses, String disassemble,
      JsonObject previous, boolean before) throws Exception {
    String connection = snapshot.get("connectionId").getAsString();
    String boot = connection.split("/")[0];
    if (!boot.matches("[0-9a-fA-F-]{36}"))
      throw new IllegalArgumentException("Capture has no valid device boot ID");
    JsonObject request = new JsonObject();
    request.addProperty("before", before);
    request.add("pid", snapshot.get("pid"));
    request.addProperty("bootId", boot);
    request.add("eventNs", snapshot.get("capturedAtBootNs"));
    JsonArray list = new JsonArray();
    addresses.stream().limit(128).forEach(list::add);
    request.add("addresses", list);
    if (previous == null) {
      JsonObject stack = new JsonObject();
      stack.add("base", snapshot.get("stackBase"));
      stack.add("data", snapshot.get("stack"));
      stack.add("registers", snapshot.get("registers"));
      stack.add("pc", snapshot.get("syscallPc"));
      request.add("stack", stack);
    } else {
      if (previous.has("codeMapping"))
        request.add("codeMapping", previous.get("codeMapping"));
      else
        request.add("mapsHash", previous.get("mapsHash"));
      request.add("fileId", previous.getAsJsonObject("disassembly").get("fileId"));
      request.addProperty("codeOnly", true);
    }
    if (!disassemble.isEmpty()) {
      request.addProperty("disassemble", disassemble);
      if (snapshot.has("syscallPc")
          && disassemble.equalsIgnoreCase(snapshot.get("syscallPc").getAsString())
          && snapshot.has("instructionHex"))
        request.add("expectedBytes", snapshot.get("instructionHex"));
    }
    String remote =
        System.getProperty("packetproxy.symbols.remote", "/data/local/tmp/ebpf-analyzer");
    if (!remote.matches("/[A-Za-z0-9/_.-]+"))
      throw new IllegalArgumentException("Invalid resolver path");
    Process p = new ProcessBuilder(System.getProperty("packetproxy.adb", "adb"), "-s",
        System.getProperty("packetproxy.adb.serial", "emulator-5554"), "shell", "-T", "su", "0",
        remote, "symbols")
                    .redirectErrorStream(true)
                    .start();
    CompletableFuture.delayedExecutor(15, TimeUnit.SECONDS).execute(() -> {
      if (p.isAlive())
        p.destroyForcibly();
    });
    try {
      try (var input = p.getOutputStream()) {
        input.write((request.toString() + "\n").getBytes(StandardCharsets.UTF_8));
      }
      byte[] output = p.getInputStream().readNBytes(4 * 1024 * 1024 + 1);
      if (output.length > 4 * 1024 * 1024)
        throw new IllegalStateException("Symbol response exceeds limit");
      if (!p.waitFor(1, TimeUnit.SECONDS) || p.exitValue() != 0)
        throw new IllegalStateException(
            "Resolver failed; check adb, root and the installed resolver");
      JsonObject result;
      try {
        result =
            JsonParser.parseString(new String(output, StandardCharsets.UTF_8)).getAsJsonObject();
      } catch (RuntimeException e) {
        throw new IllegalStateException("Resolver unavailable or invalid response");
      }
      if (result.has("error"))
        throw new IllegalStateException(result.get("error").getAsString());
      return result;
    } finally {
      if (p.isAlive())
        p.destroyForcibly();
    }
  }
}
```

`plugin/src/UiLayout.java`:

```java
package ebpfproxy;

import java.awt.*;
import javax.swing.*;

final class UiLayout {
  static JButton button(String text) {
    return new JButton(text) {
      public Dimension getPreferredSize() {
        Dimension size = super.getPreferredSize();
        Insets insets = getInsets();
        int width = getFontMetrics(getFont()).stringWidth(getText()) + insets.left + insets.right + 14;
        if (getIcon() != null) width += getIcon().getIconWidth() + getIconTextGap();
        return new Dimension(Math.max(size.width, width), Math.max(size.height,
            getFontMetrics(getFont()).getHeight() + insets.top + insets.bottom + 4));
      }
      public Dimension getMinimumSize() { return getPreferredSize(); }
    };
  }
  static void combo(JComboBox<String> combo) {
    combo.setPrototypeDisplayValue("  Manual  ");
    combo.setMinimumSize(combo.getPreferredSize());
  }
  static JScrollPane horizontal(Component content) {
    JScrollPane pane = new JScrollPane(content, ScrollPaneConstants.VERTICAL_SCROLLBAR_NEVER,
        ScrollPaneConstants.HORIZONTAL_SCROLLBAR_AS_NEEDED);
    pane.setBorder(null);
    pane.setMinimumSize(new Dimension(0, 0));
    pane.setPreferredSize(new Dimension(content.getPreferredSize().width, content.getPreferredSize().height + 14));
    return pane;
  }
}
```

`plugin/test/DebuggerViewTest.java`:

```java
import com.google.gson.*;
import java.net.*;
import java.nio.*;
import java.nio.file.*;
import java.lang.reflect.*;
import java.util.*;
import javax.swing.*;
import java.awt.*;
import java.awt.image.*;
import javax.imageio.ImageIO;

public class DebuggerViewTest {
  static Object field(Object o, String name) throws Exception {
    Field f = o.getClass().getDeclaredField(name);
    f.setAccessible(true);
    return f.get(o);
  }
  static void layout(Container c) {
    c.doLayout();
    for (Component child : c.getComponents())
      if (child instanceof Container cc)
        layout(cc);
  }
  public static void main(String[] args) throws Exception {
    System.setProperty("java.awt.headless", "true");
    try (URLClassLoader loader = new URLClassLoader(new URL[] {Path.of(args[0]).toUri().toURL()})) {
      Class<?> plugin = loader.loadClass("ebpfproxy.CommunicationOrigins");
      Method ip = plugin.getDeclaredMethod("displayIP", String.class);
      ip.setAccessible(true);
      if (!ip.invoke(null, "::ffff:c000:201").equals("192.0.2.1")
          || !ip.invoke(null, "0:0:0:0:0:ffff:192.0.2.1").equals("192.0.2.1")
          || !ip.invoke(null, "2001:db8::1").equals("2001:db8::1"))
        throw new AssertionError("IP formatting");
      Object view = loader.loadClass("ebpfproxy.DebuggerView").getConstructor().newInstance();
      Method show = view.getClass().getMethod("showSnapshot", JsonObject.class);
      JsonObject snap = new JsonObject(), regs = new JsonObject();
      snap.addProperty("connectionId", "test/run/1");
      snap.addProperty("capturedAtBootNs", "1");
      snap.addProperty("pid", 42);
      snap.addProperty("tid", 43);
      snap.addProperty("syscallPc", "0x0000000000401000");
      snap.addProperty("stackBase", "0x0000000000001000");
      snap.addProperty("status", "partial-read");
      snap.addProperty("operation", "send");
      regs.addProperty("rsp", "0x0000000000001000");
      regs.addProperty("rbp", "0x0000000000001010");
      regs.addProperty("rip", "0x0000000000401002");
      regs.addProperty("rflags", "0x0000000000000246");
      snap.add("registers", regs);
      byte[] raw = new byte[64];
      ByteBuffer b = ByteBuffer.wrap(raw).order(ByteOrder.LITTLE_ENDIAN);
      b.putLong(16, 0x1020);
      b.putLong(24, 0x401234);
      b.putLong(32, 0);
      b.putLong(40, 0x405678);
      snap.addProperty("stack", Base64.getEncoder().encodeToString(raw));
      SwingUtilities.invokeAndWait(() -> {
        try {
          show.invoke(view, snap);
          JTable frames = (JTable) field(view, "frames"), memory = (JTable) field(view, "memory");
          if (((JTable) field(view, "registers")).getColumnCount() != 2)
            throw new AssertionError("unexpected Meaning column");
          if (frames.getRowCount() != 3 || !frames.getValueAt(2, 1).equals("0x0000000000405678")
              || memory.getRowCount() != 8)
            throw new AssertionError("frame chain or word decoding");
          Method follow = view.getClass().getDeclaredMethod("selectAddress", long.class);
          follow.setAccessible(true);
          follow.invoke(view, 0x1020L);
          if (memory.getSelectedRow() != 4)
            throw new AssertionError("pointer navigation");
          JPanel panel = (JPanel) view;
          panel.setSize(1280, 650);
          layout(panel);
          BufferedImage image = new BufferedImage(1280, 650, BufferedImage.TYPE_INT_RGB);
          Graphics2D g = image.createGraphics();
          panel.paint(g);
          g.dispose();
          ImageIO.write(image, "png", Path.of(args[1]).toFile());
          b.putLong(32, 0x1010);
          snap.addProperty("stack", Base64.getEncoder().encodeToString(raw));
          show.invoke(view, snap);
          if (frames.getRowCount() != 3
              || !((JLabel) field(view, "unwindStatus")).getText().contains("Non-increasing"))
            throw new AssertionError("cycle handling");
          snap.addProperty("stack", Base64.getEncoder().encodeToString(Arrays.copyOf(raw, 25)));
          show.invoke(view, snap);
          if (frames.getRowCount() != 1)
            throw new AssertionError("read beyond snapshot");
          show.invoke(view, new Object[] {null});
          if (memory.getRowCount() != 0 || frames.getRowCount() != 0)
            throw new AssertionError("stale event");
        } catch (Exception e) {
          throw new RuntimeException(e);
        }
      });
      System.out.println("PASS: IPv4 normalization, bounded RBP chain, pointer navigation, "
                         + "cycle/truncation handling, selection clearing");
    }
  }
}
```

`plugin/test/JavaSourceViewTest.java`:

```java
package ebpfproxy;

import com.google.gson.*;
import java.awt.Color;
import java.nio.file.*;
import javax.swing.*;
import javax.swing.text.*;

public class JavaSourceViewTest {
  static java.awt.image.BufferedImage render(JComponent component) {
    component.setSize(1000, 100);
    component.doLayout();
    java.awt.image.BufferedImage image = new java.awt.image.BufferedImage(1000, 100, java.awt.image.BufferedImage.TYPE_INT_RGB);
    java.awt.Graphics2D g = image.createGraphics();
    component.paint(g);
    g.dispose();
    return image;
  }
  static void checkHighlightedText() throws Exception {
    JavaSourceView.SourcePane pane = new JavaSourceView.SourcePane();
    pane.setFont(new java.awt.Font(java.awt.Font.MONOSPACED, java.awt.Font.PLAIN, 18));
    JavaSourceView.highlight(pane, "public void onCreate() { String text = \"visible\"; return; }\n");
    java.awt.image.BufferedImage before = render(pane);
    JavaSourceView.markMethod(pane, 0);
    java.awt.image.BufferedImage after = render(pane);
    int dark = 0, retained = 0;
    for (int y = 0; y < 40; y++) for (int x = 0; x < 950; x++) {
      Color original = new Color(before.getRGB(x, y));
      if (original.getRed() < 180 && original.getGreen() < 180 && original.getBlue() < 190) {
        dark++;
        Color highlighted = new Color(after.getRGB(x, y));
        if (highlighted.getRed() < 180 && highlighted.getGreen() < 180 && highlighted.getBlue() < 190) retained++;
      }
    }
    if (dark < 100 || retained < dark * .95) throw new AssertionError("background erased syntax-colored glyphs: " + retained + "/" + dark);
    if (after.getRGB(940, 10) != new Color(255, 240, 185).getRGB()) throw new AssertionError("full row background missing");
    javax.imageio.ImageIO.write(after, "png", Path.of("plugin/build/tests/source-highlight.png").toFile());
  }
  public static void main(String[] args) throws Exception {
    JsonObject snapshot = JsonParser.parseString("{\"schemaVersion\":1,\"mode\":\"passive\",\"connections\":[],\"packets\":[],\"origins\":[{\"connectionId\":\"a\",\"architecture\":\"x86_64\",\"stackBase\":\"0x0000000000001000\",\"registers\":{}}]}").getAsJsonObject();
    JsonObject saved = snapshot.getAsJsonArray("origins").get(0).getAsJsonObject();
    Path capture = Files.createTempFile("stack-compatibility-", ".json");
    try {
      for (int size : new int[] {4096, 16384}) {
        saved.addProperty("requestedBytes", size);
        saved.addProperty("capturedBytes", size);
        saved.addProperty("stack", java.util.Base64.getEncoder().encodeToString(new byte[size]));
        Files.writeString(capture, snapshot.toString());
        CommunicationOrigins.read(capture);
      }
      saved.addProperty("requestedBytes", 4096);
      Files.writeString(capture, snapshot.toString());
      try {
        CommunicationOrigins.read(capture);
        throw new AssertionError("accepted stack beyond requested size");
      } catch (java.io.IOException expected) { }
    } finally { Files.deleteIfExists(capture); }
    JsonObject frame = new JsonObject();
    frame.addProperty("className", "com.example.networktest.MainActivity");
    frame.addProperty("methodName", "onCreate");
    frame.addProperty("lineNumber", "42");
    if (!JavaSourceView.remoteOrigin("/data/app/~~a/b==/base.apk!classes2.dex").equals("/data/app/~~a/b==/base.apk")
        || !JavaSourceView.remoteOrigin("/data/app/a;touch x.apk").isEmpty())
      throw new AssertionError("APK origin handling");
    String source = "// public 123\npublic class Sample {\n/* 42 */ void onCreate() { String s = \"return 123\"; }\n}";
    String methods = "class Sample {\n  void caller() { onCreate(); }\n  public void onCreate() {\n    /* 41 */ work();\n  }\n  public void onCreate(int value) {\n    /* 42 */ work();\n  }\n}\n";
    if (JavaSourceView.sourceOffset(methods, frame) != methods.indexOf("  public void onCreate(int"))
      throw new AssertionError("method declaration/overload navigation");
    SwingUtilities.invokeAndWait(() -> {
      try { checkHighlightedText(); }
      catch (Exception ex) { throw new RuntimeException(ex); }
      JTextPane pane = new JTextPane();
      JavaSourceView.highlight(pane, source);
      try {
        JavaSourceView.markMethod(pane, JavaSourceView.sourceOffset(source, frame));
        if (pane.getHighlighter().getHighlights().length != 1) throw new AssertionError("method background missing");
      } catch (BadLocationException ex) { throw new RuntimeException(ex); }
      JavaSourceView.SourcePane wide = new JavaSourceView.SourcePane();
      wide.setText("public class " + "LongName".repeat(100) + " {}\n");
      wide.setSize(new java.awt.Dimension(200, 100));
      if (wide.getScrollableTracksViewportWidth() || wide.getWidth() <= 200) throw new AssertionError("source wraps to viewport");
      StyledDocument doc = pane.getStyledDocument();
      Color comment = StyleConstants.getForeground(doc.getCharacterElement(3).getAttributes());
      Color keyword = StyleConstants.getForeground(doc.getCharacterElement(source.indexOf("public class")).getAttributes());
      Color literal = StyleConstants.getForeground(doc.getCharacterElement(source.indexOf("return 123")).getAttributes());
      if (comment.equals(keyword) || keyword.equals(literal) || comment.equals(literal))
        throw new AssertionError("syntax highlighting token precedence");
      DebuggerView view = new DebuggerView();
      view.memoryRows.addRow(new Object[] {"0x1000", "+0x0", "0x2000", "", ""});
      Color addressColor = view.memory.getCellRenderer(0, 0).getTableCellRendererComponent(view.memory, "0x1000", false, false, 0, 0).getForeground();
      Color valueColor = view.memory.getCellRenderer(0, 2).getTableCellRendererComponent(view.memory, "0x2000", false, false, 0, 2).getForeground();
      if (addressColor.equals(valueColor)) throw new AssertionError("address/value colors are identical");
      view.setEventMode(1);
      JsonObject pc = new JsonObject();
      pc.addProperty("syscallPc", "0x1234");
      view.snapshot = pc;
      view.instructionRows.addRow(new Object[] {"0x0000000000001234", "0f05", "syscall", ""});
      java.awt.Component cell = view.instructions.getCellRenderer(0, 0).getTableCellRendererComponent(view.instructions, "0x1234", true, false, 0, 0);
      if (cell.getBackground().equals(view.instructions.getSelectionBackground())) throw new AssertionError("selected PC background lost");
      for (int i = 0; i < view.stackTabs.getTabCount(); i++)
        if (view.stackTabs.getTitleAt(i).equals("Symbols")) throw new AssertionError("Symbols tab still present");
      FridaView stacks = new FridaView();
      JsonObject event = new JsonObject();
      JsonObject java = new JsonObject();
      JsonArray frames = new JsonArray();
      frames.add(frame);
      java.add("frames", frames);
      event.add("java", java);
      event.add("nativeFrames", new JsonArray());
      event.addProperty("result", 1);
      event.addProperty("capturedAtEpochMs", 1000);
      stacks.display(event);
      JButton sourceIcon = (JButton) stacks.javaTable.getCellRenderer(0, 4).getTableCellRendererComponent(stacks.javaTable, "Show", false, false, 0, 4);
      if (!stacks.javaTable.getColumnName(4).equals("Source") || sourceIcon.getIcon() == null
          || !sourceIcon.getText().isEmpty() || sourceIcon.getToolTipText() == null)
        throw new AssertionError("source icon or column label missing");
      if (stacks.javaTable.getColumnCount() != 5 || !stacks.javaFrames.isCellEditable(0, 4)
          || stacks.javaFrames.isCellEditable(0, 1) || !stacks.javaDetails.get(0).equals(frame))
        throw new AssertionError("Java frame Show action metadata");
    });
    if (args.length >= 3) {
      String output = JavaSourceView.decompile(args[1], frame, Path.of(args[2]));
      if (!output.contains("MainActivity") || !output.contains("onCreate"))
        throw new AssertionError("jadx source missing");
      if (JavaSourceView.sourceOffset(output, frame) < 0)
        throw new AssertionError("jadx method declaration not found");
      if (args.length >= 4) {
        frame.addProperty("origin", args[3]);
        String remote = JavaSourceView.decompile(args[1], frame, null);
        if (!remote.contains("MainActivity")) throw new AssertionError("ADB source missing");
      }
      System.out.println("PASS: real jadx CLI decompilation");
    }
    System.out.println("PASS: 4/16 KiB compatibility, origin validation, syntax colors, original-line navigation, Java Show metadata, no Symbols tab");
  }
}
```
