`plugin/test/PacketSelectionTest.java`:

```java
import com.google.gson.*;
import java.net.*;
import java.nio.file.*;
import java.lang.reflect.*;
import javax.swing.*;
import javax.swing.table.*;

/** Regression: losing a connection selection must never broaden to all packets. */
public class PacketSelectionTest {
  static Object plugin;
  static Class<?> type;
  static Object field(String name) throws Exception {
    Field f = type.getDeclaredField(name);
    f.setAccessible(true);
    return f.get(plugin);
  }
  static String viewText(String name) throws Exception {
    Object view = field("eventView");
    Field f = view.getClass().getDeclaredField(name);
    f.setAccessible(true);
    JTable table = (JTable) f.get(view);
    StringBuilder out = new StringBuilder();
    for (int r = 0; r < table.getRowCount(); r++)
      for (int c = 0; c < table.getColumnCount(); c++)
        out.append(table.getValueAt(r, c)).append(' ');
    return out.toString();
  }
  static void call(String name, Class<?>[] signature, Object... args) throws Exception {
    Method m = type.getDeclaredMethod(name, signature);
    m.setAccessible(true);
    m.invoke(plugin, args);
  }
  static JsonObject flow(String id, int pid, String ip, int port) {
    JsonObject c = new JsonObject();
    c.addProperty("connectionId", id);
    c.addProperty("pid", pid);
    c.addProperty("uid", 10000 + pid);
    c.addProperty("appName", "app" + pid);
    c.addProperty("clientIp", "10.0.2.15");
    c.addProperty("clientPort", port);
    c.addProperty("serverIp", ip);
    c.addProperty("serverPort", 443);
    c.addProperty("validFromEpochMs", 100);
    c.addProperty("validToEpochMs", 200);
    c.addProperty("closed", false);
    return c;
  }
  static JsonObject packet(String id, String from, String to) {
    JsonObject p = new JsonObject();
    p.addProperty("connectionId", id);
    p.addProperty("source", from);
    p.addProperty("destination", to);
    p.addProperty("protocol", "TCP");
    p.addProperty("data", "AQ==");
    p.addProperty("originalLength", 1);
    p.addProperty("atEpochMs", 150);
    return p;
  }
  static JsonObject fixture() {
    JsonObject root = new JsonObject();
    root.addProperty("schemaVersion", 1);
    root.addProperty("mode", "passive");
    root.addProperty("running", true);
    root.addProperty("updatedAtEpochMs", System.currentTimeMillis());
    JsonArray flows = new JsonArray();
    flows.add(flow("A", 10, "1.1.1.1", 40001));
    flows.add(flow("B", 20, "8.8.8.8", 40002));
    root.add("connections", flows);
    JsonArray packets = new JsonArray();
    packets.add(packet("A", "10.0.2.15:40001", "1.1.1.1:443"));
    packets.add(packet("A", "1.1.1.1:443", "10.0.2.15:40001"));
    packets.add(packet("B", "10.0.2.15:40002", "8.8.8.8:443"));
    packets.add(packet("", "9.9.9.9:443", "10.0.2.15:40003"));
    root.add("packets", packets);
    return root;
  }
  static JsonObject event(String id, int tid, String pc, String stack) {
    JsonObject e = new JsonObject();
    e.addProperty("connectionId", "A");
    e.addProperty("eventId", id);
    e.addProperty("capturedAtBootNs", Integer.toString(tid));
    e.addProperty("tid", tid);
    e.addProperty("pid", 10);
    e.addProperty("operation", "send");
    e.addProperty("phase", "entry");
    e.addProperty("syscallPc", pc);
    e.addProperty("stackBase", "0x0000000000001000");
    e.addProperty("stack", stack);
    e.addProperty("architecture", "x86_64");
    e.addProperty("requestedBytes", 16384);
    e.addProperty("capturedBytes", 1);
    e.add("registers", new JsonObject());
    return e;
  }
  static void count(int expected) throws Exception {
    int actual = ((JTable) field("packets")).getRowCount();
    if (actual != expected)
      throw new AssertionError("expected " + expected + " packets, got " + actual);
  }
  public static void main(String[] args) throws Exception {
    System.setProperty("java.awt.headless", "true");
    Path input = Files.createTempFile("packet-selection-", ".json");
    Files.writeString(input, fixture().toString());
    System.setProperty("packetproxy.monitor.file", input.toString());
    try (URLClassLoader loader = new URLClassLoader(new URL[] {Path.of(args[0]).toUri().toURL()})) {
      type = loader.loadClass("ebpfproxy.CommunicationOrigins");
      plugin = type.getConstructor().newInstance();
      SwingUtilities.invokeAndWait(() -> {
        try {
          type.getMethod("createPanel").invoke(plugin);
        } catch (Exception e) {
          throw new RuntimeException(e);
        }
      });
      boolean[] busy = {true};
      for (int i = 0; i < 50 && busy[0]; i++) {
        Thread.sleep(50);
        SwingUtilities.invokeAndWait(() -> {
          try {
            busy[0] = (boolean) field("busy");
          } catch (Exception e) {
            throw new RuntimeException(e);
          }
        });
      }
      if (busy[0])
        throw new AssertionError("reader did not finish");
      SwingUtilities.invokeAndWait(() -> {
        try {
          JTable connections = (JTable) field("connections"), packets = (JTable) field("packets");
          count(0); // Previously displayed all 4 packets without any selection.
          connections.setRowSelectionInterval(0, 0);
          count(2);
          JsonObject withEvents = fixture();
          JsonArray ev = new JsonArray();
          ev.add(event("first", 101, "0x0000000000001234", "AQ=="));
          ev.add(event("second", 102, "0x0000000000005678", "Ag=="));
          withEvents.add("ioEvents", ev);
          call("render", new Class<?>[] {JsonObject.class}, withEvents);
          JTable events = (JTable) field("ioEvents");
          if (events.getRowCount() != 2)
            throw new AssertionError("events overwritten by connection");
          if (events.getColumnCount() != 6 || !events.getColumnName(0).equals("Time")
              || !events.getColumnName(4).equals("Length"))
            throw new AssertionError("I/O columns");
          if (connections.getColumnCount() != 7 || !connections.getColumnName(0).equals("SYN Time")
              || !connections.getColumnName(6).equals("Updated"))
            throw new AssertionError("connection times");
          events.setRowSelectionInterval(0, 0);
          if (!viewText("registers").contains("5678") || !viewText("memory").contains("02 "))
            throw new AssertionError("second event context missing");
          events.setRowSelectionInterval(1, 1);
          if (!viewText("registers").contains("1234") || !viewText("memory").contains("01 "))
            throw new AssertionError("first event context missing");
          events.getRowSorter().toggleSortOrder(2);
          call("render", new Class<?>[] {JsonObject.class}, withEvents);
          if (!"first".equals(field("selectedEvent")))
            throw new AssertionError("sorted event identity changed");
          call("render", new Class<?>[] {JsonObject.class}, fixture());
          if (field("selectedEvent") != null || viewText("registers").contains("1234"))
            throw new AssertionError("evicted event context remains");
          if (!packets.getValueAt(0, 5).equals("\u2192")
              || !packets.getValueAt(1, 5).equals("\u2190") || packets.getColumnCount() != 6)
            throw new AssertionError("directions/context incorrect");
          packets.setRowSelectionInterval(0, 0);
          JsonObject reordered = fixture();
          JsonArray reverse = new JsonArray();
          reverse.add(reordered.getAsJsonArray("connections").get(1));
          reverse.add(reordered.getAsJsonArray("connections").get(0));
          reordered.add("connections", reverse);
          call("render", new Class<?>[] {JsonObject.class}, reordered);
          count(2);
          if (!"A".equals(field("selected")))
            throw new AssertionError("refresh switched connection identity");
          if (!((JTextArea) field("bytes")).getText().contains("01"))
            throw new AssertionError("selected packet hex lost");
          connections.clearSelection();
          count(0);
          if (!((JTextArea) field("bytes")).getText().isEmpty())
            throw new AssertionError("stale packet hex remains");
          connections.setRowSelectionInterval(0, 0);
          count(1);
          @SuppressWarnings("unchecked")
          TableRowSorter<DefaultTableModel> sorter =
              (TableRowSorter<DefaultTableModel>) connections.getRowSorter();
          sorter.setRowFilter(RowFilter.regexFilter("no-match"));
          count(0);
          sorter.setRowFilter(null);
          connections.setRowSelectionInterval(1, 1);
          count(2);
          JsonObject removed = fixture();
          removed.getAsJsonArray("connections").remove(0);
          call("render", new Class<?>[] {JsonObject.class}, removed);
          count(0);
          call("clear", new Class<?>[] {});
          count(0);
          JsonObject timed = fixture();
          timed.getAsJsonArray("connections")
              .get(0)
              .getAsJsonObject()
              .addProperty("connectionId", "boot/1000500000");
          call("render", new Class<?>[] {JsonObject.class}, timed);
          JsonObject timedEvent = event("clock", 101, "0x000000000000abcd", "AQ==");
          timedEvent.addProperty("connectionId", "boot/1000500000");
          timedEvent.addProperty("capturedAtBootNs", "1001750000");
          Method clock = type.getDeclaredMethod("eventTime", JsonObject.class);
          clock.setAccessible(true);
          String expectedTime = java.time.format.DateTimeFormatter.ofPattern("HH:mm:ss.SSS")
                                    .format(java.time.Instant.ofEpochMilli(101).atZone(
                                        java.time.ZoneId.systemDefault()));
          if (!clock.invoke(plugin, timedEvent).equals(expectedTime))
            throw new AssertionError("I/O clock conversion");
        } catch (Exception e) {
          throw new RuntimeException(e);
        }
      });
      System.out.println("PASS: no implicit all-packets view, TX/RX, refresh by connection ID, "
                         + "clear/filter/eviction stay empty, stale hex cleared");
    } finally {
      Files.deleteIfExists(input);
    }
  }
}
```

`plugin/test/ProcessBrowserTest.java`:

```java
package ebpfproxy;

import com.google.gson.*;
import java.awt.*;
import java.awt.image.BufferedImage;
import java.nio.file.*;
import java.util.*;
import java.util.List;
import javax.imageio.ImageIO;
import javax.swing.*;
import javax.swing.tree.*;

public class ProcessBrowserTest {
  static TreePath find(JTree tree, String owner, String method) {
    DefaultMutableTreeNode root = (DefaultMutableTreeNode) tree.getModel().getRoot();
    Enumeration<?> nodes = root.depthFirstEnumeration();
    while (nodes.hasMoreElements()) {
      DefaultMutableTreeNode node = (DefaultMutableTreeNode) nodes.nextElement();
      if (node.getUserObject() instanceof ProcessBrowser.Entry entry && entry.owner.equals(owner) && entry.method.equals(method)) return new TreePath(node.getPath());
    }
    throw new AssertionError("Missing tree entry: " + owner + "/" + method);
  }
  static Object field(Object object, String name) throws Exception {
    var field = object.getClass().getDeclaredField(name); field.setAccessible(true); return field.get(object);
  }
  static void layout(Container component) {
    component.doLayout();
    for (Component child : component.getComponents()) if (child instanceof Container nested) layout(nested);
  }
  public static void main(String[] args) throws Exception {
    SwingUtilities.invokeAndWait(() -> {
      try {
        List<JsonObject> requests = new ArrayList<>(); List<String> selected = new ArrayList<>();
        ProcessBrowser view = new ProcessBrowser(requests::add, selected::add);
        view.reset(42);
        view.accept(JsonParser.parseString("{\"type\":\"catalog\",\"modules\":[{\"name\":\"libnet.so\",\"path\":\"/lib/libnet.so\"}],\"classes\":[\"com.example.Client\",\"com.example.Idle\"]}").getAsJsonObject());
        JsonObject event = JsonParser.parseString("{\"eventId\":\"event1\",\"tid\":43,\"operation\":\"send\",\"nativeFrames\":[{\"module\":\"/lib/libnet.so\",\"symbolName\":\"send\"}],\"java\":{\"frames\":[{\"className\":\"com.example.Client\",\"methodName\":\"request\",\"origin\":\"/data/app/base.apk\"},{\"className\":\"com.example.Client\",\"methodName\":\"request\"}]}}").getAsJsonObject();
        view.setEvents(List.of(event)); view.refresh.stop(); view.rebuild();
        view.javaTree.setSelectionPath(find(view.javaTree, "com.example.Client", "request"));
        if (view.matches.getRowCount() != 1 || view.selected.events.size() != 1) throw new AssertionError("duplicate frames counted as calls");
        view.table.setRowSelectionInterval(0, 0); view.openSelected();
        if (!selected.equals(List.of("event1"))) throw new AssertionError("event navigation");
        if (!requests.isEmpty()) throw new AssertionError("observed frames requested full inventory");
        if (!DebuggerView.str(view.selected.frame, "origin").equals("/data/app/base.apk")) throw new AssertionError("source origin lost");
        if (!view.index.get("module\n/lib/libnet.so\n").toString().startsWith("libnet.so")) throw new AssertionError("module label");
        view.firstFrame.setValue(1); view.lastFrame.setValue(1);
        find(view.javaTree, "com.example.Client", "request");
        if (!view.index.get("module\n/lib/libnet.so\n").events.isEmpty()) throw new AssertionError("native depth filter");
        view.firstFrame.setValue(2);
        if (!view.index.get("class\ncom.example.Client\n").events.isEmpty()) throw new AssertionError("Java depth filter");
        view.firstFrame.setValue(0); view.lastFrame.setValue(0);
        view.observedOnly.setSelected(false); view.rebuild();
        view.javaTree.expandPath(find(view.javaTree, "com.example.Idle", ""));
        if (requests.stream().noneMatch(r -> DebuggerView.str(r, "name").equals("com.example.Idle"))) throw new AssertionError("lazy class members missing");
        view.accept(JsonParser.parseString("{\"type\":\"members\",\"kind\":\"class\",\"name\":\"com.example.Client\",\"members\":[\"request(int): void\",\"request(): void\",\"idle(): void\"]}").getAsJsonObject());
        find(view.javaTree, "com.example.Client", "idle");
        view.observedOnly.setSelected(true); view.rebuild();
        try { find(view.javaTree, "com.example.Client", "idle"); throw new AssertionError("unobserved member not filtered"); }
        catch (AssertionError ex) { if (!ex.getMessage().startsWith("Missing tree entry")) throw ex; }
        view.javaTree.setSelectionPath(find(view.javaTree, "com.example.Client", "request"));
        JsonObject offsetEvent = event.deepCopy();
        offsetEvent.addProperty("eventId", "event2");
        JsonObject first = event.getAsJsonArray("nativeFrames").get(0).getAsJsonObject();
        first.addProperty("address", "0x1002"); first.addProperty("functionOffset", "0x2");
        JsonObject second = offsetEvent.getAsJsonArray("nativeFrames").get(0).getAsJsonObject();
        second.addProperty("address", "0x1010"); second.addProperty("functionOffset", "0x10");
        event.getAsJsonArray("nativeFrames").add(first.deepCopy());
        view.setEvents(List.of(event, offsetEvent)); view.refresh.stop(); view.rebuild();
        TreePath function = find(view.libraries, "/lib/libnet.so", "send").getParentPath();
        // find() visits offset leaves first; select the function parent explicitly.
        DefaultMutableTreeNode functionNode = (DefaultMutableTreeNode) function.getLastPathComponent();
        if (!(functionNode.getUserObject() instanceof ProcessBrowser.Entry functionEntry) || !functionEntry.offset.isEmpty()) throw new AssertionError("offset hierarchy");
        if (functionNode.getChildCount() != 2 || !functionNode.getChildAt(0).toString().equals("+0x2") || !functionNode.getChildAt(1).toString().equals("+0x10")) throw new AssertionError("numeric offset ordering");

        TreePath offset = function.pathByAddingChild(functionNode.getChildAt(1));
        view.libraries.expandPath(function); view.libraries.setSelectionPath(offset);
        if (view.matches.getRowCount() != 1 || !view.matchingIds.equals(List.of("event2"))) throw new AssertionError("offset reverse lookup");
        int offsetRow = view.libraries.getRowForPath(offset);
        if (view.libraryTable.getSelectedRow() != offsetRow) throw new AssertionError("table selection differs from offset");
        if (view.libraryTable.getColumnCount() != 1) throw new AssertionError("Counter still visible");
        view.libraryTable.toggle(function);
        int collapsedRows = view.libraryTable.getRowCount();
        view.rebuild();
        function = find(view.libraries, "/lib/libnet.so", "send").getParentPath();
        if (view.libraries.isExpanded(function) || view.libraryTable.getRowCount() != collapsedRows) throw new AssertionError("collapse lost on refresh");
        view.libraryTable.toggle(function);
        if (view.libraryTable.getRowCount() != collapsedRows + 2) throw new AssertionError("expand offsets");
        view.libraries.setSelectionPath(function.pathByAddingChild(((DefaultMutableTreeNode) function.getLastPathComponent()).getChildAt(0)));
        if (!ProcessBrowser.nativeOffset(JsonParser.parseString("{\"symbol\":\"lib.so!send+0x000a\"}").getAsJsonObject()).equals("+0xa")) throw new AssertionError("legacy offset parsing");

        offsetEvent.addProperty("result", -11);
        if (!ProcessBrowser.ioResult(offsetEvent).equals("Error (errno 11)")) throw new AssertionError("error result");
        TreePath module = function.getParentPath();
        view.libraries.collapsePath(module); view.rebuild();
        module = find(view.libraries, "/lib/libnet.so", "send").getParentPath().getParentPath();
        if (view.libraries.isExpanded(module)) throw new AssertionError("nested collapse reopened on refresh");
        view.libraries.expandPath(module);
        function = find(view.libraries, "/lib/libnet.so", "send").getParentPath();
        if (!view.libraries.isExpanded(function)) throw new AssertionError("hidden nested expansion was lost");
        view.libraries.setSelectionPath(module); view.libraryTable.branch(false); view.rebuild();
        function = find(view.libraries, "/lib/libnet.so", "send").getParentPath();
        module = function.getParentPath(); view.libraries.expandPath(module);

        view.libraries.setSelectionPath(module); int requestCount = requests.size(); view.libraryTable.branch(true);
        if (!view.libraries.isExpanded(function) || requests.size() != requestCount) throw new AssertionError("recursive expansion fetched inventory or failed");
        view.libraries.setSelectionPath(function); view.updateMatches();
        view.libraryTable.autoExpand.setSelectedIndex(0);
        function = find(view.libraries, "/lib/libnet.so", "send").getParentPath();
        if (!view.libraries.isExpanded(function)) throw new AssertionError("automatic expand");
        view.libraries.setSelectionPath(function);
        ((JMenuItem) view.libraryTable.menu.getComponent(3)).doClick(); view.rebuild();
        function = find(view.libraries, "/lib/libnet.so", "send").getParentPath();
        if (view.libraries.isExpanded(function)) throw new AssertionError("context collapse not preserved");

        if (view.libraries.isExpanded(function.getParentPath())) throw new AssertionError("automatic collapse");
        view.libraryTable.autoExpand.setSelectedIndex(0);

        eventDebugger.showRecorded(recorded.deepCopy());
        if (eventDebugger.stackTabs.getSelectedComponent() != eventDebugger.disassemblyPanel) throw new AssertionError("disassembly tab reset");
        eventDebugger.showRecorded(null); eventDebugger.showRecorded(recorded);
        if (eventDebugger.stackTabs.getSelectedComponent() != eventDebugger.disassemblyPanel) throw new AssertionError("tab lost across empty selection");

        CommunicationOrigins plugin = new CommunicationOrigins();

        JTabbedPane tabs = (JTabbedPane) field(plugin, "tabs");
        ProcessBrowser browser = (ProcessBrowser) field(plugin, "processBrowser");
        if (SwingUtilities.isDescendingFrom(mode, panel)) throw new AssertionError("mode selector still on main panel");
        if (((DebuggerView) field(plugin, "connectView")).symbolMode.getItemCount() != 2) throw new AssertionError("Connect still exposes Frida");
        mode.setSelectedIndex(2);
        if (tabs.indexOfComponent(browser) < 0) throw new AssertionError("Frida tab missing");
        mode.setSelectedIndex(1);
        if (tabs.indexOfComponent(browser) >= 0) throw new AssertionError("Frida tab visible outside Frida mode");
        JButton button = UiLayout.button("Settings");
        button.setFont(button.getFont().deriveFont(24f));
        if (button.getPreferredSize().width < button.getFontMetrics(button.getFont()).stringWidth(button.getText()) + button.getInsets().left + button.getInsets().right)
          throw new AssertionError("button text clipped at larger font size");
      } catch (Exception ex) { throw new RuntimeException(ex); }
    });
    System.out.println("PASS: reverse lookup, deduplication, lazy members, observed filter, event navigation, PID reset, Settings-only mode, Frida-only tab, button sizing");
  }
```

`plugin/test/TableRefreshTest.java`:

```java
package ebpfproxy;
import com.google.gson.*;
import java.lang.reflect.*;
import javax.swing.*;
public class TableRefreshTest {
  static Object field(Object o, String name) throws Exception {
    Field f = o.getClass().getDeclaredField(name);
    f.setAccessible(true);
    return f.get(o);
  }
  static void call(Object o, String name) throws Exception {
    Method m = o.getClass().getDeclaredMethod(name);
    m.setAccessible(true);
    m.invoke(o);
  }
  public static void main(String[] args) throws Exception {
    System.setProperty("java.awt.headless", "true");
    SwingUtilities.invokeAndWait(() -> {
      try {
        CommunicationOrigins p = new CommunicationOrigins();
        p.createPanel();
        JsonObject root =
            JsonParser
                .parseString(
                    "{\"running\":true,\"updatedAtEpochMs\":1000,\"connections\":[{"
                    + "\"connectionId\":\"boot/run/"
                    + "1000000\",\"pid\":12,\"uid\":1,\"validFromEpochMs\":1000,\"validToEpochMs\":"
                    + "2000}],\"packets\":[{\"connectionId\":\"boot/run/"
                    + "1000000\",\"atEpochMs\":1000,\"data\":\"\",\"originalLength\":0}],"
                    + "\"ioEvents\":[{\"connectionId\":\"boot/run/"
                    + "1000000\",\"eventId\":\"event1\",\"capturedAtBootNs\":\"1000000\","
                    + "\"operation\":\"send\",\"tid\":13}]}")
                .getAsJsonObject();
        Method render = p.getClass().getDeclaredMethod("render", JsonObject.class);
        render.setAccessible(true);
        render.invoke(p, root);
        JTable main = (JTable) field(p, "connections"), packets = (JTable) field(p, "packets"),
               io = (JTable) field(p, "ioEvents");
        Object before = main.getValueAt(0, 0);
        root.getAsJsonArray("connections")
            .get(0)
            .getAsJsonObject()
            .addProperty("validFromEpochMs", 1001);
        render.invoke(p, root);
        if (!before.equals(main.getValueAt(0, 0)))
          throw new AssertionError("SYN time changed");
        main.setRowSelectionInterval(0, 0);
        if (packets.getRowCount() != 1 || io.getRowCount() != 1)
          throw new AssertionError("Missing detail rows");
        ((JMenuItem) packets.getComponentPopupMenu().getComponent(0)).doClick();
        ((JMenuItem) io.getComponentPopupMenu().getComponent(0)).doClick();
        render.invoke(p, root);
        if (packets.getRowCount() != 0 || io.getRowCount() != 0)
          throw new AssertionError("Cleared rows reappeared");
        JsonObject next = root.getAsJsonArray("ioEvents").get(0).getAsJsonObject().deepCopy();
        next.addProperty("eventId", "event2");
        root.getAsJsonArray("ioEvents").add(next);
        JsonObject packet = root.getAsJsonArray("packets").get(0).getAsJsonObject().deepCopy();
        packet.addProperty("atEpochMs", 2000);
        root.getAsJsonArray("packets").add(packet);
        render.invoke(p, root);
        if (packets.getRowCount() != 1 || io.getRowCount() != 1)
          throw new AssertionError("New rows hidden");
        ((JMenuItem) main.getComponentPopupMenu().getComponent(0)).doClick();
        render.invoke(p, root);
        if (main.getRowCount() != 0)
          throw new AssertionError("Connections reappeared");
        JsonObject flow = root.getAsJsonArray("connections").get(0).getAsJsonObject().deepCopy();
        flow.addProperty("connectionId", "boot/run/2000000");
        root.getAsJsonArray("connections").add(flow);
        render.invoke(p, root);
        if (main.getRowCount() != 1)
          throw new AssertionError("New connection hidden");
      } catch (Exception e) {
        throw new RuntimeException(e);
      }
    });
    System.out.println("PASS: stable SYN time, Clear on all tables, refresh suppression, new "
                       + "observations visible");
  }
}
```

`scripts/build-device.sh`:

```sh
#!/usr/bin/env bash
set -euo pipefail
cd "$(dirname "$0")/.."
mkdir -p bin/android-x86_64
clang -O2 -g -target bpfel -Wall -Werror -DSTACK_X86_64=1 -c bpf/tcp.c -o go/internal/device/collector/tcp_bpfel.o
clang -O2 -g -target bpfel -Wall -Werror -c bpf/frida.c -o go/internal/device/fridacapture/frida_bpfel.o
clang -O2 -g -target bpfel -Wall -Werror -c bpf/fridastream.c -o go/internal/device/fridastream/fridastream_bpfel.o
# Static Linux binaries run on the rooted Android x86_64 target without bionic/NDK linking.
export GOOS=linux GOARCH=amd64 CGO_ENABLED=0
go -C go build -trimpath -o ../bin/android-x86_64/ebpf-analyzer ./cmd/device

```

`scripts/build.py`:

```py
"""Build the PacketProxy plugin and assemble a desktop distribution."""

import argparse
import os
import pathlib
import shutil
import subprocess
from go_notices import collect as collect_go_notices

ROOT = pathlib.Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]


def run(*args, **kwargs):
    subprocess.run(args, cwd=ROOT, check=True, **kwargs)


def plugin(jar):
    output = ROOT / "plugin/build"
    classes = output / "classes"
    classes.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    sources = sorted(str(p) for p in (ROOT / "plugin/src").glob("*.java"))
    run(
        "javac",
        "-encoding",
        "UTF-8",
        "--release",
        "17",
        "-cp",
        str(jar),
        "-d",
        str(classes),
        *sources,
    )
    artifact = output / "CommunicationOrigins.jar"
    run("jar", "--create", "--file", str(artifact), "-C", str(classes), "ebpfproxy")
    return artifact


def package(args):
    jar = plugin(pathlib.Path(args.packetproxy_jar).resolve())
    output = pathlib.Path(args.output_dir).resolve() / f"ebpf-analyzer-{args.os}-{args.arch}"
    if output.exists():
        raise RuntimeError(f"Output already exists: {output}; choose a clean workspace")
    output.mkdir(parents=True)
    env = dict(os.environ, GOOS=args.os, GOARCH=args.arch, CGO_ENABLED="0")
    monitor = output / ("monitor.exe" if args.os == "windows" else "monitor")
    run(
        "go",
        "-C",
        str(ROOT / "go"),
        "build",
        "-trimpath",
        "-o",
        str(monitor),
        "./cmd/monitor",
        env=env,
    )
    (output / "plugin").mkdir()
    shutil.copy2(jar, output / "plugin" / jar.name)
    device = pathlib.Path(args.device_dir).resolve()
    for name in ("ebpf-analyzer",):
        if not (device / name).is_file():
            raise FileNotFoundError(device / name)
    (output / "android-x86_64").mkdir()
    shutil.copy2(device / "ebpf-analyzer", output / "android-x86_64/ebpf-analyzer")
    for name in ("ebpf-analyzer",):
        (output / "android-x86_64" / name).chmod(0o755)
    (output / "frida").mkdir()
    for name in ("agent.js", "bridge.py", "stream.py"):
        shutil.copy2(ROOT / "frida" / name, output / "frida" / name)
    for name in ("README.md", "LICENSE"):
        shutil.copy2(ROOT / name, output / name)
    shutil.copytree(ROOT / "LICENSES", output / "LICENSES")
    collect_go_notices(ROOT, [monitor, output / "android-x86_64/ebpf-analyzer"],
                       output / "LICENSES/third-party-go")
    (output / "docs").mkdir()
    shutil.copy2(ROOT / "docs/demo.gif", output / "docs/demo.gif")
    # Include the BPF sources corresponding to the embedded GPL objects.
    shutil.copytree(ROOT / "bpf", output / "bpf")
    if args.os != "windows":
        monitor.chmod(0o755)
    archive = shutil.make_archive(str(output), "zip", root_dir=output.parent, base_dir=output.name)
    print(archive)


if __name__ == "__main__":
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__)
    parser.add_argument("--packetproxy-jar", required=True)
    parser.add_argument("--output-dir", default="dist")
    parser.add_argument("--device-dir", default="bin/android-x86_64")
    parser.add_argument("--os", choices=["windows", "linux", "darwin"], required=True)
    parser.add_argument("--arch", choices=["amd64", "arm64"], required=True)
    package(parser.parse_args())

```

`scripts/go_notices.py`:

```py
"""Collect upstream notice files for the modules actually linked into shipped binaries."""

import json
import pathlib
import shutil
import subprocess


def collect(root, binaries, destination):
    def go(*args):
        return subprocess.check_output(
            ["go", "-C", str(root / "go"), *args], text=True, encoding="utf-8"
        )

    modules = {}
    versions = set()
    for binary in binaries:
        lines = go("version", "-m", str(binary)).splitlines()
        versions.add(lines[0].rsplit(": ", 1)[1])
        for line in lines[1:]:
            fields = line.split()
            if fields and fields[0] == "=>":
                raise RuntimeError("Resolve replaced Go modules explicitly before distributing")
            if fields and fields[0] == "dep":
                key = (fields[1], fields[2])
                modules.setdefault(key, {"path": fields[1], "version": fields[2],
                                         "sum": fields[3] if len(fields) > 3 else "",
                                         "binaries": []})["binaries"].append(binary.name)

    env = json.loads(go("env", "-json", "GOROOT", "GOVERSION"))
    if versions != {env["GOVERSION"]}:
        raise RuntimeError("Rebuild all shipped Go binaries with the configured toolchain before collecting notices")
    destination.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    toolchain = destination / "go"
    toolchain.mkdir()
    for name in ("LICENSE", "PATENTS"):
        source = pathlib.Path(env["GOROOT"]) / name
        if source.is_file():
            shutil.copy2(source, toolchain / name)
    if not (toolchain / "LICENSE").is_file():
        raise RuntimeError("Go toolchain LICENSE missing")

    notice_names = {"LICENSE", "LICENCE", "NOTICE", "COPYING", "COPYRIGHT", "PATENTS", "AUTHORS"}
    for (path, version), record in sorted(modules.items()):
        module = json.loads(go("mod", "download", "-json", path + "@" + version))
        if module.get("Sum", "") != record["sum"]:
            raise RuntimeError("Module checksum differs from the shipped binary: " + path)
        source = pathlib.Path(module["Dir"])
        target = destination / (path + "@" + version)
        files = []
        for item in sorted(source.rglob("*")):
            if not item.is_file():
                continue
            name = item.name.upper()
            if not any(name == base or name.startswith(base + ".") or name.startswith(base + "-")
                       for base in notice_names):
                continue
            relative = item.relative_to(source)
            output = target / relative
            output.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
            shutil.copy2(item, output)
            # Downloaded module cache files can be read-only on Windows.
            output.chmod(0o644)
            files.append(relative.as_posix())
        if not any(pathlib.PurePosixPath(name).name.upper().startswith(("LICENSE", "LICENCE", "COPYING")) for name in files):
            raise RuntimeError("No upstream license found for " + path)
        record["noticeFiles"] = files

    manifest = {"goVersion": env["GOVERSION"], "modules": list(modules.values())}
    (destination / "manifest.json").write_text(json.dumps(manifest, indent=2) + "\n", encoding="utf-8")
    lines = ["# Go dependency notices", "", "Generated from the shipped binaries' Go build metadata.",
             "Upstream LICENSE/NOTICE and related files are copied unchanged from checksum-verified modules.",
             "The Go toolchain license is in `go/`. These files retain their upstream license terms.", "",
             "| Module | Version | Included in |", "| --- | --- | --- |"]
    for (path, version), record in sorted(modules.items()):
        lines.append(f"| {path} | {version} | {', '.join(record['binaries'])} |")
    lines += ["", "See `manifest.json` for checksums and the list of included notice files.", ""]
    (destination / "README.md").write_text("\n".join(lines), encoding="utf-8")

```

`scripts/test-plugin.py`:

```py
"""Run device-independent Swing tests on the desktop build."""

import argparse
import os
import pathlib
import subprocess

root = pathlib.Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__)
parser.add_argument("--packetproxy-jar", required=True)
args = parser.parse_args()
jar = root / "plugin/build/CommunicationOrigins.jar"
tests = root / "plugin/build/tests"
tests.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
cp = os.pathsep.join([str(jar), str(pathlib.Path(args.packetproxy_jar).resolve())])
sources = ["TableRefreshTest.java", "DebuggerViewTest.java", "PacketSelectionTest.java", "JavaSourceViewTest.java", "ProcessBrowserTest.java"]
subprocess.run(
    [
        "javac",
        "-encoding",
        "UTF-8",
        "--release",
        "17",
        "-cp",
        cp,
        "-d",
        str(tests),
        *[str(root / "plugin/test" / s) for s in sources],
    ],
    check=True,
    cwd=root,
)
for name in ["ebpfproxy.TableRefreshTest", "DebuggerViewTest", "PacketSelectionTest", "ebpfproxy.JavaSourceViewTest", "ebpfproxy.ProcessBrowserTest"]:
    extra = [str(tests / "debugger.png")] if name == "DebuggerViewTest" else []
    subprocess.run(
        [
            "java",
            "-Djava.awt.headless=true",
            "-cp",
            str(tests) + os.pathsep + cp,
            name,
            str(jar),
            *extra,
        ],
        check=True,
        cwd=root,
    )

```
